- **Summary**: 针对AI生成的法律证据检测问题，构建SLED-1400数据集，评估人类和MLLM。人类准确率64.8%，MLLM对强生成器检测率低。建议结合人类审查、MLLM筛选和来源基础设施。

<h3 id="207">[207] Structured Neuron Pruning in Deep Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07615) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Salem Ameen, Sunil Vadera
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Multi-Armed Bandit
- **Summary**: 针对深度神经网络冗余神经元，提出基于多臂老虎机的结构化剪枝框架，将每个神经元视为臂。UCB1和Thompson Sampling在多个任务上优于未剪枝模型和基线方法。

<h3 id="208">[208] Item Response Scaling Laws: A Measurement Theory Approach for Efficient and Generalizable Neural Scaling Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07616) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sang Truong, Yuheng Tu, Rylan Schaeffer, Sanmi Koyejo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Scaling Laws, Model Evaluation, Item Response Theory
- **Summary**: 针对缩放定律推导成本高的问题，将项目反应理论融入缩放定律框架（Beta-IRT），将参数复杂度从O(M×N)降至O(M+N)。仅需50个问题即可获得可靠估计，减少99.9%数据。

<h3 id="209">[209] Query Lens: Interpreting Sparse Key-Value Features with Indirect Effects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07617) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hwiyeong Lee, Ingyu Bang, Uiji Hwang, Hyelim Lim, Taeuk Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：稀疏自编码器提供的特征可解释性不足。方法：提出Query Lens，扩展Logit Lens，联合考虑编码器侧关键特征和解码器侧值特征，并考虑下游模块的间接效应。结果：产生连贯的令牌签名，使先前不可解释的特征变得可解释，并提出子空间通道假说。

<h3 id="210">[210] ScaleSweep: Accurate NVFP4 Post-Training Quantization of LLMs via Block Scale Initialization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07618](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07618) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Li Lin, Xiaojun Wan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Quantization, LLM Inference, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 问题：NVFP4量化尺度初始化（AbsMax）与最优解存在差距。方法：ScaleSweep，通过扫描可行的块尺度候选并选择最小化目标的候选，理论推导扫描范围上下界。结果：持续改善量化性能，在激进量化下保留超过93%的全精度性能。

<h3 id="211">[211] SENTRY: Statistical Reliability Analysis of Vision Transformers Under Soft Errors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07620) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pramit Kumar Bhaduri, Mahdi Taheri, Samira Nazari, Maksim Jenihhin, Christian Herglotz, Michael Hubner
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Fault Tolerance
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉Transformer在安全关键领域易受软错误影响，穷举故障注入不可行。方法：统计故障注入框架，利用有限总体抽样理论提供正式可靠性保证。结果：仅需数千样本即可保证1%误差界限（99%置信度），实验成本降低10700倍，定位到归一化层和指数位的脆弱性。

<h3 id="212">[212] HASA: Subnet Allocation for Compute-Constrained Model-Heterogeneous Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07621) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amir Hossein Shahdadian, Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem, Mahdi Taheri, Samira Nazari, Christian Herglotz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Model Heterogeneity
- **Summary**: 问题：模型异构联邦学习中的子网分配未考虑统计异质性。方法：HASA，基于客户端异质性分数分配子网宽度，同时保持固定计算预算。结果：在下一词预测基准上，均匀分配的平均测试准确率从13.82%提升至14.32%，最差客户端准确率提升。

<h3 id="213">[213] Eyes All Around: Design and Analysis of 360-Degree LiDAR Perception Using Equivariant Feature Learning in Unstructured Traffic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07626) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pranav Darshan, Raghuveer Narayanan Rajesh, M Uttara Kumari
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Equivariant Learning, LiDAR Perception
- **Summary**: 问题：密集非结构化城市交通中的360度LiDAR感知。方法：结合扇形全景处理和旋转等变稀疏卷积的360度感知框架。结果：对汽车（92.02/90.51）、公交车（80.53/76.34）和卡车（78.59/74.16）检测性能强，行人（67.45/61.02）和骑行者较低。

<h3 id="214">[214] Large Language Models Should Learn Personalized Rather Than Aggregated Human Preferences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07629](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07629) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cristina Garbacea
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Personalization, Preference Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 观点：LLM应学习个性化偏好而非聚合偏好。方法：立场论文，理论论证聚合偏好丢失多样性，调查个性化技术路线，回应可扩展性、安全等反驳。结论：通过有界个性化框架可管理安全挑战。

<h3 id="215">[215] Active Learning with Foundation Model Priors: Efficient Learning under Class Imbalance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07630) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiancheng Zhang, Meiqing Li, Qi Zhang, Yinglun Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Class Imbalance, Foundation Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：真实数据集类别不平衡和标签噪声损害模型性能。方法：利用基础模型先验的主动学习框架，进行不平衡感知的协同决策。结果：相较于最佳基线节省超过50%标注量，对标签噪声保持鲁棒。

<h3 id="216">[216] Trait-space Monitoring for Emergent Misalignment During Supervised Finetuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huy Nghiem, Sy-Tuyen Ho, Sarah Wiegreffe, Hal Daumé III
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 问题：监督微调中涌现的错位难以检测。方法：在激活空间中跟踪沿对齐相关特质的表征漂移。结果：低开销监测器以2.2%假阴性率、2.9%假阳性率、0.990 AUROC检测危险检查点，揭示几何特征。

<h3 id="217">[217] AMN: An Adaptive Multi-Scale Fusion Network with Boundary and Uncertainty Modeling for Nuclei Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07633](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07633) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Spoorthi M, Suja Palaniswamy
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Vision Transformer
- **Summary**: 问题：组织病理学图像核分割中单一编码器难以同时捕捉局部纹理和长程上下文。方法：AMN双编码器（Swin Transformer + ResNet-50）分割框架，带逐通道门控融合和多目标损失。结果：在CoNIC上平均Dice 0.82，F1 0.68，优于八种基线，跨数据集泛化良好。

<h3 id="218">[218] NeuroAlign: Hierarchical Multimodal Fusion of Dynamic and Structural Neuroimaging for MCI Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07635) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiongri Shen, Zhenxi Song, Jiaqi wang, Yi Zhong, Leilei Zhao, Chenqi Xu, Linling Li, Yichen Wei, Lingyan Liang, Demao Deng, Luping Song, Ping Luan, Ahmed M. Anter, Shuqiang Wang, Baiying Lei, Zhiguo Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Neuroimaging
- **Summary**: 问题：功能MRI和扩散张量成像的异构特征空间和对齐表示挑战。方法：NeuroAlign层次融合框架，包括双模态层次对齐和双域层次交互，以及协同激活映射。结果：在MCI/SCD检测上表现有竞争力，初步跨数据集迁移。

<h3 id="219">[219] Anchor-Conditioned Compositional Control for Landscape Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07638) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gadha Lekshmi P, Govind Arun, Rohith Syam, Ahmed Elgammal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Diffusion Model, Controllable Generation
- **Venue**: International Conference on Computational Creativity, ICCC 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：图像生成模型缺乏摄影师所用的构图控制。方法：锚点条件微调框架，使用四维构图向量、解耦交叉注意力和傅里叶编码。结果：地平线检测率0.850，三分法对齐0.817，类别依赖的构图精度。

<h3 id="220">[220] MOSS-Video-Preview: Toward Real-Time Video Understanding via Cross-Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengyu Wang, Chenkun Tan, Shaojun Zhou, Wei Huang, Qirui Zhou, Zhan Huang, Zhen Ye, Jijun Cheng, Xiaomeng Qian, Yanxin Chen, Xingyang He, Huazheng Zeng, Chenghao Wang, Pengfei Wang, Hongkai Wang, Shanqing Gao, Yixian Tian, Chenghao Liu, Xinghao Wang, Botian Jiang, Xipeng Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Real-Time Systems
- **Summary**: 问题：视频理解从离线转向实时交互，现有模型无法在生成过程中感知新帧。方法：双通道交叉注意力架构，数据合成管道，训练实时行为。结果：仅轻微离线性能下降，首令牌延迟加速约5倍，解码吞吐量提升2.7倍，获得持续感知和答案修正能力。

<h3 id="221">[221] No Free Lunch for Synthetic Images under Data Scarcity Conditions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07640) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Borja Arroyo Galende, Alejandro Almodóvar, Patricia A. Apellániz, Juan Parras, Silvia Uribe, Santiago Zazo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Data Privacy, Differential Privacy
- **Summary**: 问题：数据稀缺和隐私敏感条件下合成数据的保真度、隐私和效用权衡。方法：评估框架，比较VAE、GAN和DDPM在三个数据集上的表现。结果：GAN和DDPM在差分隐私下更鲁棒，VAE退化更快。

<h3 id="222">[222] AVI-Bench: Toward Human-like Audio-Visual Intelligence of Omni-MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07643](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07643) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaoting Wang, Ziyi Zhang, Wenming Tu, Shaoxuan Xu, Wenjie Du, Cheng Liang, Weijun Wang, Yuanchao Li, Guangyao Li, Hao Fei, Yuanchun Li, Henghui Ding, Yunxin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Benchmark, Audio-Visual Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：全模态大语言模型的音频-视觉智能缺乏系统性基准。方法：AVI-Bench基准，涵盖感知、理解和推理三个阶段，以及原始刺激扩展。结果：揭示当前模型显著局限性，提出四层AVI分类。

<h3 id="223">[223] FineGen: A VLM-based Multi-Agent Framework for Fine-Grained Image-Text Dataset Construction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07645) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chang Kong, Yuebing Li, Peng Mo, Haigang Zhang, Qiuming Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Data Synthesis, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉-语言数据集中困难负样本稀缺。方法：FineGen，基于VLM的多智能体框架，含生成-验证-校正流水线和闭环反馈。结果：构建FineGen-100K，属性有效性96.7%，下游硬样本准确率提升14.4%。

<h3 id="224">[224] DOME: Learning Transferable Domain Variables from Sparse Supervision for Test-Time Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoran Xu, Yifan Xu, Yupeng Wu, Xiaoshan Yang, Changsheng Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, Domain Adaptation, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：测试时自适应方法隐式推断单一全局域分布，忽略多维样本特定偏移。方法：DOME，域编码器利用视觉-语言预训练和动量更新的稀疏域库。结果：在ImageNet-C等上达到最优性能，证明显式域表示优于复杂自适应算法。

<h3 id="225">[225] AQIFormer: A Transformer-Based Multi-View Architecture for Cross-City Air Quality Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07648) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Om Kathalkar, Nitin Nilesh, Sachin Chaudhari, Anoop Namboodiri
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Air Quality Monitoring, Image Classification, Multi-View Learning
- **Venue**: ICVGIP 2025
- **Summary**: 针对图像空气质量估计方法跨城市泛化能力不足和多视角利用不充分的问题，提出AQIFormer，一种基于Transformer的集成架构，通过双视图融合、天气感知注意力和多任务学习实现鲁棒分类。在26678个图像对上达到89.96%准确率，相比SOTA提升14.96%，跨城市泛化仅下降8.29%。

<h3 id="226">[226] ViMax: Agentic Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07649) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lingxuan Huang, Sizhe He, Hengji Zhou, Liqiang Nie, Lianghao Xia, Chao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Multi-Agent System, Narrative Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对长视频生成缺乏叙事规划和视觉一致性的问题，提出ViMax代理视频生成框架，通过多代理协作、层级叙事引擎和依赖感知视觉一致性机制生成连贯叙事。该框架能保持多场景时间线上的叙事完整性和视觉一致性。

<h3 id="227">[227] A Dataset for Dynamic Human Preferences for Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07653) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hannah Gao, Dylan Hadfield-Menell, Rachel Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Preference Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型评估忽视动态人偏好的问题，构建了一个动态多模态人偏好基准，提供自动化生成流水线和评估结果。评估了当前SOTA模型在上下文偏好适应方面的能力。

<h3 id="228">[228] MM-Matryoshka: Towards Budget-Elastic Visual Document Retrieval via a 2D Multimodal Matryoshka Training Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haowen Xiang, Yibo Yan, Jiahao Huo, Yu Huang, Yi Cao, Mingdong Ou, Xuming Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Document Retrieval, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对多向量视觉文档检索器存储和计算开销大的问题，提出MM-Matryoshka，一种2D Matryoshka训练框架，支持沿维度和层的弹性检索。在保持高质量的同时显著降低存储和计算开销。

<h3 id="229">[229] Seq103: A Unified Neuroevolution Framework for Compact Sequence Architecture Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07664) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenxiao Li, Yongjian Liu, Qing Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuroevolution, Sequence Modeling, Time Series Classification
- **Summary**: 针对序列分类任务中紧凑架构搜索的问题，提出Seq103，一个统一的NEAT风格神经进化框架，支持循环和单向序列分类。在文本和时间序列数据上，平均保留86.96%和81.95%的最佳基线准确率，参数量减少数十至数十万倍。

<h3 id="230">[230] AgentCompile: An LLM-Guided Compiler for Direct CUDA Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07665](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07665) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuanzhe Li, Ziyan Weng, Zhiyu Zhu, Junhui Hou
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Compiler Optimization, GPU Computing
- **Summary**: 针对Transformer推理需要专用编译器支持的问题，提出AgentCompile，一个LLM指导的CUDA推理编译器，利用LLM输出作为搜索元数据。在生成任务中对Qwen3等模型实现4-5.66倍加速。

<h3 id="231">[231] MemoVAD: Resource-Efficient Video Anomaly Detection via Dynamic Semantic Memory in Edge Computing Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07669) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guo Li, Jiandian Zeng, Yang Li, Zihao Peng, Ke Chen, Tian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Edge Computing, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对边缘设备上视频异常检测语义不足的问题，提出MemoVAD，一个边缘-云协作框架，通过不确定性感知门控和动态语义内存有效利用VLM语义。在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence上降低通信开销并超越SOTA。

<h3 id="232">[232] Liquid Neural Networks as a Drop-in Continuous-Time Deformation Field for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07670) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingzhao Li, Arghya Pal, Guan Yuan Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Gaussian Splatting, Liquid Neural Network
- **Summary**: 针对动态3D高斯泼溅中变形场缺乏时间平滑性的问题，用液体神经网络（CfC单元）替代原有MLP，实现连续时间变形。在多个动态场景上达到或超过MLP基线，尤其在高频运动场景中提升显著。

<h3 id="233">[233] A Hierarchical Feature Engineering Framework for Automated Classification of Phonotraumatic and Non-Phonotraumatic Vocal Hyperfunction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07673](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07673) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: June-Woo Kim, Kangwook Jang, Minu Kim, Hyunju Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Medical AI, Feature Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对发声过度分类中生物标记物不足的问题，提出一个层级特征工程框架，包含静态、动态、比率和耦合特征。在PVH和NPVH分类上分别达到0.891和0.728的AUC。

<h3 id="234">[234] Single-Cell Cross-Modal Transfer by Adversarial Fine-Tuning of Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07676) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joseph Boyd, Matthew Lyon, Martino Mansoldo, Christian Hurry, Finnian Firth
- **Subjects**: q-bio.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Single-cell Analysis, Foundation Model, Computational Biology
- **Summary**: 针对空间转录组数据稀缺但单细胞数据丰富的问题，通过对单细胞基础模型进行对抗微调实现跨模态翻译。在未配对数据上表现优于多组学翻译方法。

<h3 id="235">[235] DOG-DPO:Dynamic Optimization in Geometry for Safety Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07678) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Nian, Tiankai Yang, Yudi Zhang, Qi Pan, Zelong Xu, Shenzhe Zhu, Qingqing Luan, Yue Huang, Xiangliang Zhang, Yue Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 针对安全对齐中偏好数据选择忽略方向信息的问题，提出DOG-DPO，一种将偏好对表示为几何方向并选择多样性子集的训练无关框架。仅用11%数据实现强大效用-鲁棒性权衡。

<h3 id="236">[236] Systematic LLM Translation of Legacy Scientific Code to Differentiable Frameworks: Application to a Land Surface Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07681) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aya Lahlou, Linnia Hawkins, Pierre Gentine
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Scientific Computing, Code Generation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对遗留科学代码迁移到可微框架的挑战，提出一个五阶段LLM代理流水线，将Fortran代码翻译为JAX。对CLM-ml-v2模型实现全雅可比计算、参数恢复加速和24倍加速。

<h3 id="237">[237] SWE-Marathon: Can Agents Autonomously Complete Ultra-Long-Horizon Software Work?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07682) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rishi Desai, Jesse Hu, Joan Cabezas, Neel Harsola, Pratyush Shukla, Roey Ben Chaim, Adnan El Assadi, Omkaar Mukund Kamath, Fenil Faldu, Prannay Hebbar, Jiankai Sun, Yiyuan Li, Pramod Srinivasan, Ishan Gupta, Christopher Settles, Daniel Wang, Derek Chen, Pranav Raja, Albert Liu, Marek Šuppa, Nevasini Sasikumar, Luyang Kong, Erik Quintanilla, Xiangyi Li, Ivan Bercovich, Steven Dillmann
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Agent Evaluation, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对现有代理基准局限于短任务的问题，推出SWE-Marathon，包含20个超长任务。当前前沿代理解决率低于30%，失败原因包括自我验证不足和奖励黑客。

<h3 id="238">[238] Semantic Cache Distillation: Efficient State Transfer via Reuse and Selective Patching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07684) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qianli Ma, Zhiqing Tang, Hanshuai Cui, Zhi Yao, Weijia Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Cache Policy
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对解耦服务中KV缓存传输成为通信瓶颈的问题，提出语义缓存蒸馏框架，通过低秩重建和稀疏补丁实现高效传输。在带宽受限场景下获得2.65倍TTFT加速，质量损失在5%F1以内。

<h3 id="239">[239] Test-Time Adaptive Composition for Machine Learning as a Service (MLaaS) in IoT Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07685) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deepak Kanneganti, Sajib Mistry, Sheik Mohammad Mostakim Fattah, Aneesh Krishna
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, IoT, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 针对IoT环境下MLaaS组合动态适应困难的问题，提出测试时自适应组合框架，包含TTA感知可组合模型和服务级适应模型。实验表明比传统方法更有效降低计算时间。

<h3 id="240">[240] Knowledge-Inclusive Adaptive Physics-Informed Neural Network for Microbial Interaction Modelling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ravisha Rupasinghe, Rajith Vidanaarachchi, Asela Hevapathige, Sachith Seneviratne, Sen-Lin Tang, Saman Halgamuge
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Neural Network, Computational Biology, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 针对PINN仅利用数据发现参数而忽略其他知识的问题，提出知识包含自适应PINN框架，整合文本和网络结构知识。在微生物群落建模上提升高达53%，知识带来额外23%准确率增益。

<h3 id="241">[241] What Makes Video World Model Latents Action-Relevant: Prediction over Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07687) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jewon Yeom, Hanseul Kim, Jeongjae Park, Sungmok Jung, Jaejin Lee, Taesup Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Video Understanding, World Model
- **Summary**: 视频世界模型的潜在表征中哪些预训练信号能诱导出与动作相关的结构尚不明确。本文通过统一探针评估发现，时间视频预训练（而非像素重建保真度）是驱动动作相关结构的主要因素，视频预训练自监督编码器在视觉保真度和动作预测之间实现了最佳帕累托权衡。逆动力学监督还显著提高了对视觉扰动的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="242">[242] TRACER: Token ReAssignment for Concept ERasure in Generative Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07688](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07688) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziheng Chen, Jiali Cheng, Zezhong Fan, Hadi Amiri, Diyuan Wu, Gabriele Tolomei, Yang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Machine Unlearning
- **Summary**: 现有大模型遗忘方法无法直接应用于生成式推荐，因为语义ID是抽象标识符，遗忘项和保留项共享导致冲突。提出TRACER，一个基于令牌重分配的概念遗忘框架，通过重分配概念相关令牌到替代令牌并引入一致性正则化，有效移除目标概念的同时更好地保留推荐效用。

<h3 id="243">[243] HARP: Efficient Data Selection for Finetuning Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07690](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07690) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ning Wang, Zhengxin Zhang, Maosen Tang, Yitang Gao, Claire Cardie, Sainyam Galhotra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Fine-Tuning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 微调数据选择需平衡下游目标提升和成本，无训练选择器可扩展但代理可能与目标不匹配，有训练选择器成本高。提出HARP，一种高效有训练选择器，通过层次结构、经验贝叶斯后验和两种互补包络线，以约7倍少的训练样本实现最佳结果，超越最强基线最高8.9点。

<h3 id="244">[244] BCG-FM: A Foundation Model for Ambient Cardiac Health Sensing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07692) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Magnus Ruud Kjaer, Haejun Han, Ashish Neupane, David Q. Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Healthcare Monitoring, Biosignal Processing
- **Summary**: 现有可穿戴生物信号基础模型均需用户主动操作，缺乏环境机械生物信号的基础模型。提出BCG-FM，首个基于心冲击图（BCG）的基础模型，使用压电传感器夜间无感记录，通过参与者级对比学习预训练，在生物年龄估计上实现3.26年MAE，并跨15种健康状况和三个外部队列展示临床相关判别能力。

<h3 id="245">[245] DSFNet: Learning Dual-Domain Spectral Operators for Multi-Modality Spatio-Temporal Forecasting in Urban Transportation Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07695](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07695) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongchao Li, Yang Li, Zhuoxuan Li, Jun Chen, Chu Zhang, Jinde Cao, Leszek Rutkowski
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Forecasting, Multi-Modal Fusion, Spatiotemporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有多模态时空预测方法未能显式建模不同模态变量间的耦合关系。提出双域谱滤波网络（DSFNet），利用双域谱算子捕获异构空间模式并建模变量关系，外加门控机制适应外部影响。在五个真实交通数据集上MAE降低3.21%-10.16%，显著优于现有基线。

<h3 id="246">[246] Adversarial Robustness of Activation Steering in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07696](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07696) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kien Le, Thai Le
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Adversarial Robustness, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: 激活干预在大语言模型中的鲁棒性尚未被研究。本文首次系统评估了在对抗性文本扰动下激活干预的鲁棒性，覆盖多种提取方法、攻击策略和模型，发现攻击普遍成功：方向鲁棒性下降高达64%，置信度崩溃，层选择也脆弱。从对抗扰动输入提取向量可部分恢复可操控性，但局限性明显。

<h3 id="247">[247] TianJi-Environ: An Autonomous AI Scientist for Atmospheric Environmental Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07697) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoluo Zhao, Hongchun Zhang, Nan Li, Jing-Jia Luo, Kaikai Zhang, Mengyang Yu, Nan Chen, Tao Song, Fan Meng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Scientific Discovery, Environmental AI
- **Summary**: 大气化学中污染机制的可解释验证仍依赖专家知识，机制假设需转换为可执行实验。提出TianJi-Environ，一个基于WRF-Chem的多智能体框架，自主驱动复杂模拟，将机制假设转化为可执行配置和证据标准。在夏季臭氧和冬季PM2.5案例中展示了机制验证能力，使专家驱动的验证变得明确、结构化和可审计。

<h3 id="248">[248] Pharmacogenomic Knowledge Graph Augmentation for Graph Neural Network-Based Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07698) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juergen Dietrich
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Graph Neural Network, Drug-Drug Interaction
- **Summary**: 基于GNN的药物相互作用预测受限于分子结构的信息天花板。本文通过整合药理基因组学先验知识（CYP注释）作为特征向量，部分填补该天花板。在药物对层面分割下，知识图谱增强显著提升DDI类型分类（F1-macro 0.532 vs 0.241），但二元检测和药物层面泛化仍受限于信息天花板。

<h3 id="249">[249] EssentialGIN: a new approach for gene essentiality prediction based on graph isomorphism neural networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07700](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07700) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sahar Mansouri-Rad, Zahra Narimani, Parvin Razzaghi, Nazanin Hosseinkhan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Bioinformatics, Gene Essentiality Prediction
- **Summary**: 必需基因预测实验成本高，传统中心性方法假阳性多。提出EssentialGIN，基于图同构网络整合基因表达、直系同源和亚细胞定位等生物数据。实验证明该方法优于中心性方法和Node2Vec、MLP、GAT等，在人类数据上显著更优。

<h3 id="250">[250] EvoCSFL: Surrogate-Assisted Evolutionary Client Selection for Efficient and Robust Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07702) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lin Qiang, Sun Xiaoyan, Hu Yao, Fang Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Evolutionary Algorithm, Client Selection
- **Summary**: 客户端数据和系统异质性使得随机选择难以保证联邦学习的收敛速度和鲁棒性。提出基于代理辅助的进化客户端选择框架，将性能、延迟和能耗整合为指标，使用代理模型和进化算法搜索最优子集。在多个数据集上实现更快收敛、更低能耗和更好鲁棒性。

<h3 id="251">[251] How Much Dense Attention is Necessary? Oracle-Guided Sparse Prefill for Full/GQA Layers in Hybrid Long-Context Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07703) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongxing Wang, Harenome Razanajato, Zhen Zhang, Yujie Yuan, Hongsheng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Long Context, Sparse Attention
- **Summary**: 长上下文预填充即使在混合模型中仍昂贵，因为全/GQA层仍需计算历史序列。本文通过注意力质量top-k oracle研究稀疏预算可行性，并导出蒸馏索引器用于稀疏服务。oracle显示可行性，蒸馏索引器在GPU上实现1.93倍加速，质量损失小。

<h3 id="252">[252] FunctionEvolve: Structure-Guided Symbolic Regression with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07704](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07704) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Xia, Jun Zhu, Dong Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Evolutionary Algorithm, LLM
- **Summary**: 现有LLM驱动的符号回归结构盲，缺乏局部变异和可靠评分。提出FunctionEvolve，使用表达式树的进化框架，结构摘要促进多样化父选择，局部树编辑保留有用子表达式，结构感知拟合提高评分。在LLM-SRBench合成子集上恢复107个精确形式，SA@50达82.9%，比同骨干基线高4.5倍。

<h3 id="253">[253] SAW: Stage-Aware Dynamic Weighting for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07705](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07705) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen He, Baolong Bi, Shenghua Liu, Huaming Liao, Yuyao Ge, Bolin Wan, Siqian Tong, Juan Chen, Jiafeng Guo, Xueqi Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Zhaolutuan/SAW)
- **Summary**: 多目标强化学习中目标间学习异步，静态加权忽略此现象。提出阶段感知动态加权（SAW），利用变异系数作为信息量代理，在批内动态重加权各维度。在工具调用和文本摘要任务上，SAW在GRPO和GDPO框架下一致提升训练效率和最终性能。

<h3 id="254">[254] MLingualFC: Evaluating Jailbreak Vulnerabilities in Multilingual Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07706](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07706) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rishabh Makwana, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Jailbreak Detection, Multilingual Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Rishabhpm23/MLingualFC)
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型在结构化视觉提示（如流程图）下的安全鲁棒性在多语言设置中缺乏研究。提出MLingualFC，一个多语言多模态基准，用流程图编码有害指令评估五种语言的VLM。发现拉丁语系攻击成功率高，非拉丁语（如旁遮普语）因视觉文本识别限制成功率低，表明当前安全机制跨语言泛化不足。

<h3 id="255">[255] Cross-View Urban Traffic Dataset: Drone-Supervised Ground Truth for Monocular Bird's-Eye View Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07708) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prakhar Bhardwaj, Simone Weikl, Kilian Mang, Elia Jonas Sandtner
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cross-View Localization, Dataset, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 缺乏从自我中心视频和无人机视频进行交叉视角城市交通感知的带身份级对齐的数据集。本文引入包含同步自行车和无人机视频的数据集和基准，用于交叉视角身份匹配和自我到鸟瞰图预测。结果显示可行但具挑战性，交叉视角匹配召回率高但存在过度分配和时间不一致性。

<h3 id="256">[256] WhiFlash: Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Token-Level Cross-Paradigm Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07710) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Young D. Kwon, Miles Williams, Rui Li, Alexandros Kouris, Stylianos I. Venieris
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference, KV Cache
- **Summary**: 推测解码中静态起草范式因序列内准确率波动而性能不佳。提出WhiFlash，首个跨范式的推测解码方法，在令牌级控制器下统一自回归和扩散起草，采用轻量熵或学习策略以及缓存管理优化。相比EAGLE-3和DFlash，分别实现最高69.6%和37.3%的吞吐量提升。

<h3 id="257">[257] Rosetta Memory: Adaptive Memory for Cross-LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07711) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Yang, Shiqi Shen, Haoxuan Li, Zhipeng Wang, Zhi Gong, Xu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有记忆系统针对特定LLM设计，无法在不同模型间迁移。该文提出记忆为中心的LLM适应方法，通过两个轮廓条件算子实现跨LLM记忆读写，并设计最小增益采样课程和性能差距奖励进行训练。在HotpotQA等数据集上，该方法持续优于基线，并对未见模型替换保持鲁棒。

<h3 id="258">[258] MatMind: A Structure-Activity Knowledge-Driven Generative Foundation Model for Materials Science</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07712](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07712) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhan&#39;ao Yao, Boxuan Zhang, Jingyuan Shu, Xiaoyu Wu, Rongyan Wang, Linjing Li, Dajun Zeng, Yudong Yao, Tingwei Chen, Youwei Wang, Xiaolin Zhao, Jiahui Shi, Jianjun Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Foundation Model, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 当前材料科学AI模型各司其职，缺乏统一骨干。作者提出MatMind，一个基于LLM的生成式基础模型，通过结构-活性知识注入、双头架构和多目标物理信息强化学习统一性质预测与晶体生成。在多个任务上达到或超过专用模型性能，如能带隙MAE最低、无条件生成S.U.N.率达65.3%。

<h3 id="259">[259] Attention at the Theoretical Minimum: A Mathematics of Arrays Framework for Memory-Optimal Transformer Kernels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07713) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lenore Mullin, Gaetan Hains
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Transformer Architecture, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: 标准注意力机制实现导致二次存储流量，能耗极高。该文利用数组数学框架推导出注意力的指称范式，消除所有中间数组，实现O(n dk + n dv)数据移动。预测可实现2-100倍加速和2-50倍能耗降低，数值验证与PyTorch一致。

<h3 id="260">[260] Beyond Accuracy: Interpreting Topic Representation in Suicide Ideation Detection Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hamideh Ghanadian, Isar Nejadgholi, Hussein Al Osman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Mental Health, Interpretability, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 自杀意念检测模型通常仅用准确率评估，但内部表征缺乏理解。该文通过可视化和几何分析，比较原始数据和主题增强数据训练模型的内在表征。结果表明主题感知增强提高了心理风险因素的清晰度与分离性。

<h3 id="261">[261] SHIELD-IDS: Structurally Heterogeneous Ensemble with Integrated Layered Defense for Intrusion Detection Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07716) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maryam Zaman, Muhammad Khuram Shahzad
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Intrusion Detection, Adversarial Robustness, Ensemble Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/maryamzaman-git/SHEILD-IDS)
- **Summary**: 对抗攻击威胁基于ML的入侵检测系统，之前方法缺乏结构多样性。该文提出IDS-Anta++，集成XGBoost和LightGBM并采用三层黑盒防御：隔离森林异常筛选、中值特征平滑和六路多数投票。在CIC-IDS-2017等数据集上，干净数据准确率\>99%，且对抗条件下鲁棒性优于基线。

<h3 id="262">[262] Multi-planar 2D-U-Net Segmentation of 3D-CT Abdominal Organs augmented by Spatial Occurrence Maps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daria Kern, Negar Chabi, Souraj Adhikary, Andre Mastmeyer
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 3D CT腹部器官分割任务。该文提出轻量级2D-U-Net框架，结合粗到细分割、多平面预测和模糊3D空间图提供解剖位置线索。在80个CT扫描上评估，使用空间发生图后Dice最多提升约4%。

<h3 id="263">[263] Quantum-Enhanced Similarity Measures for Polarimetric Materials Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07766) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sara Shojaei, Seyed Mohamad Ali Tousi, Emma Bennett, Param Sangani, Ali Shiri Sichani, Ilker Ersoy, Hadi Ali-Akbarpour, Filiz Bunyak, G. N. DeSouza
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Materials Science, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 极化材料分类问题。该文提出量子-经典混合流水线：编码器生成32维嵌入，编码为量子态，通过SWAP测试计算保真度进行匹配分类。在23种材料的Mueller矩阵数据集上，分类准确性与最优运输方法相当，并具有开放集判别能力。

<h3 id="264">[264] Beyond Point Estimates: Benchmarking Uncertainty Quantification Methods on the AION-1 Astronomical Foundation Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07771) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Karla Tame-Narvaez, Aleksandra Ćiprijanović, Shubhendu Trivedi
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; astro-ph.GA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Conformal Prediction, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 星系性质回归任务需要可靠的不确定性量化。该文比较了七种UQ方法在AION-1基础模型嵌入上的表现。结果表明分布自由共形方法在90%名义覆盖下边际覆盖误差约1%，而LVD框架还提供了有限样本局部有效性。

<h3 id="265">[265] Memetic Capture: A Pluralistic Policy Framework for Governing AI-Driven Cultural Disempowerment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07802) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Subramanyam Sahoo
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, AI Governance
- **Venue**: Pluralistic Alignment Workshop at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有AI治理框架忽视文化影响，文化替代会侵蚀人类价值观。该文提出“模因捕获”概念和文化多元治理框架（CPGF），包含定量文化影响指标、民主价值集会等四层架构，认为多元主义是结构性必要。

<h3 id="266">[266] SLMJury: Can Small Language Models Judge as Well as Large Ones?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07810) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anish Laddha, Nitesh Pradhan, Gaurav Srivastava
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Small Language Model, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/anishh15/SLMJury)
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型作为评判员成本高延迟大。该文提出SLMJury框架，评估16个小语言模型评判员在封闭式和开放式任务上的性能。发现过度思考效应领域依赖，Phi-4在二元判断中最佳但在MT-Bench上仅列第9，多智能体辩论反而降低准确率。

<h3 id="267">[267] The ACUTE Protocol: Operationalizing Language Model Activations for Better Calibration, Utility, and Trust</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nishant Subramani, Palash Goyal, Yiwen Song, Mani Malek, Yuan Xue, Tomas Pfister, Hamid Palangi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Calibration, Uncertainty Estimation, Trust Calibration
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 语言模型校准不佳且可被操纵。该文提出新指标EURO平衡校准与信息量，以及ACUTE协议用于基于激活的置信度估计。在多项选择题、工具调用和科学文档摘要任务上，ACUTE在EURO上优于强基线，同时保持低校准误差。

<h3 id="268">[268] Jas: AI-Paired Engineering as a Revival of N-Version Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07828) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jason Hickey
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Code Generation, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: AI辅助软件工程的安全保障问题。该文通过个案研究，采用精确可执行YAML规范和并行实现作为差分测试层，单个开发者在约120小时内完成了矢量插图应用的五个平台移植。认为AI配对工程使传统需多人年的工作成为可能。

<h3 id="269">[269] Beyond Pass/Fail: Using Process Mining to Understand How LLMs Resist (and Fail) Red Team Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zvi Topol
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Red Teaming, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 标准红队评估仅以攻击成功率二元结果衡量，忽略时序结构。该文将过程挖掘应用于红队追踪，从8,575个评分事件中提取直接跟随图和状态转移矩阵，揭示了不同模型防御轮廓的结构性差异，如GPT-OSS的接近吸收性拒绝状态与Llama的多条逃逸路径。

<h3 id="270">[270] Cherry-pick Override: Unsafe Directional Commitment in LLM Judges under Mixed Evidence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07834) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoran Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Safety, Fact Checking
- **Summary**: LLM法官在混合证据下会错误地返回方向性裁决，违反任务契约。该文定义了Cherry-pick Override（CCO）失败，并采用协议诊断。实验发现三选项法官在84%以上混合证据中返回方向性裁决，多数投票放大方向倾向，而常见单通道修复均留有残余失败。

<h3 id="271">[271] Agentic multi-fidelity learning of quasiparticle and excitonic properties</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07836](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07836) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arnab Neogi, Aaron Forde, Christopher A. Lane, Sergei Tretiak, Jian-Xin Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Active Learning
- **Summary**: GW-BSE计算昂贵且数值不稳定。该文提出智能体引导的多保真度框架，通过结构智能体分配置信权重，利用高斯过程校正，在应变MoS2-WS2双层中修正数值伪影。结果改善了准粒子带隙和激子结合能，且不确定性校准良好。

<h3 id="272">[272] Does Persona Make LLMs K-pop Fans? A Pilot Study of LLM-Based Online Concert Audience Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07837) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kirak Kim, Hyojin Kim, Yejin Son, Sungyoung Kim, Kyung Myun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Persona, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Culture x AI: Evaluating AI as a Cultural Technology
- **Summary**: 录播表演缺乏集体观众体验。该文构建多智能体系统，十个LLM智能体通过角色扮演生成实时粉丝聊天。实验表明角色条件显著提高了聊天质量和自然度，但未转化为社交连接感或参与度的提升，访谈揭示线上K-pop聊天本质上是集体独白。

<h3 id="273">[273] Cost-Aware Speculative Execution for LLM-Agent Workflows: An Integrated Five-Dimension Method</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07846) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Faisal Fareed
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Speculative Execution
- **Summary**: LLM-Agent工作流中上游操作等待导致大量空闲时间，投机执行可回收空闲时间但存在成本和成功概率估计问题。本文提出一种五维设计方法，包括定价、贝叶斯后验概率估计等，并规定运行时机制和校准流程。合成验证实验确认了决策边界、概率阈值和后验恢复等行为。

<h3 id="274">[274] Beyond English benchmarks: clinical llm evaluation in Brazilian Portuguese</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07853) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giordano de Pinho Souza, Glaucia Melo, Josefino Cabral Melo Lima, Daniel Schneider
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Clinical NLP, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 现有临床LLM基准大多基于英文，缺乏跨语言评估。本文构建了ClinicalBr，首个巴葡-英双语临床基准，包含2892个案例和四项评估任务。评估发现葡英性能差距因任务而异：诊断检索中英语优势明显，但其他任务差距不显著；检查推荐是最难任务。

<h3 id="275">[275] Model Multiplicity for Adversarial Detection in Small Language Model Training on Edge Devices</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07857) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefan Behfar, Richard Mortier
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Adversarial Robustness, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 边缘设备上的分布式LLM微调易受投毒攻击。本文提出基于模型多重性的防御机制，通过并行训练多个小模型并由不同节点子集更新，利用模型间差异检测异常。实验表明该方法能比单模型防御更早、更可靠地检测投毒。

<h3 id="276">[276] The Last Visible Pixel: Probing Fine-Scale Perception in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lujun Li, Lama Sleem, Niccolo Gentile, Yangjie Xu, Yewei Song, Wenbo Wu, Radu State
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型对精细尺度的感知能力尚未充分探索。本文提出FineSightBench基准，系统探测模型在4-48像素尺度下的感知和推理能力。结果表明感知能力在12像素左右饱和，而推理能力即使在更大尺度下也受限。

<h3 id="277">[277] Instrumented data for causal scientific machine learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07865) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel N. Wilke
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Data Synthesis, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 科学机器学习受限于数据质量，观测数据和模板合成数据各有不足。本文提出仪器化数据概念，每个数据点携带产生它的机理模型、不确定性及反事实可执行族。该方法在计算生物学、气候等领域有潜在应用。

<h3 id="278">[278] The Cross-Architecture Substrate: A Domain-Transcendent, Calibration-Surviving Geometric Invariant of Modern Vision Encoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yousef Radwan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Model Evaluation, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 不同视觉神经网络训练后内部表示应不同，但研究发现其前十六个主成分方向收敛到相同的几何对象——跨架构基板。该基板跨域传输，并可用于无标签迁移滤波、域检测、低样本探测和无教师蒸馏。

<h3 id="279">[279] Strained Coherence: A Pre-Failure Signal in Coding Agent Execution Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07889) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marut Pandya, Kasey Zhang, Baiqing Lyu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 编码智能体有时会承认推理问题但仍继续行动，形成“强制一致性”失败模式。本文给出操作定义并构建检测器，在Terminal-bench-2轨迹上评估，被标记轨迹失败率为94%，未标记为46%，差距显著。

<h3 id="280">[280] 3D Oral Modelling with Improved Vertex Distribution Using Matching-Based Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jihun Cho, Soo-Yeon Jeong, Eun-Jeong Bae, Sun-Young Ihm
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Medical Imaging, Point Cloud
- **Venue**: Korea Multimedia Society Conference
- **Summary**: 先前3D口腔重建模型预测顶点集中于高密度区域，分布不均。本文引入匈牙利匹配和排斥损失改进损失函数，使得顶点分布更均匀。虽然准确率从77.49%降至68.02%，但聚类问题显著缓解。

<h3 id="281">[281] Larch: Learned Query Optimization for Semantic Predicates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07923) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fuheng Zhao, Pawel Liskowski, Zihan Li, Benjamin Han, Puxuan Yu, Varich Boonsanong, Dimitris Tsirogiannis, Anupam Datta
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Database Optimization, Graph Neural Network, Query Optimization
- **Summary**: 数据库语义谓词操作推理成本高且难以优化。本文提出Larch框架，包含两种变体：Larch-A2C使用图神经网络和MDP优化过滤顺序，Larch-Sel监督预测选择性。实验表明Larch比现有方法减少3-19倍令牌开销。

<h3 id="282">[282] Decoupling Semantics and Logic: A Training-Free Coarse-to-Fine Pipeline for Video Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07924](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07924) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxin Dai, Zehang Wei, Jiamin Yan, Xiang Xiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: RAG, Video Understanding, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 MAGMAR Workshop
- **Summary**: 跨语言长视频理解、人设遵循和零幻觉时间定位是挑战。本文提出无需训练的两阶段级联视频RAG流程：第一阶段语义预取，第二阶段基于LLM的认知重排。在MAGMaR检索赛道获得第一名。

<h3 id="283">[283] Illusions of the Gold Standard: A Large-scale Analysis of Human Evaluation Protocols for Long-form Text Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07936) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katelyn Xiaoying Mei, Yi-Li Hsu, Minjoon Choi, Zongwan Cao, Chenjun Xu, Bingbing Wen, Su Lin Blodgett, Lucy Lu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Text Generation, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/larchlab/Illusions-of-the-Gold-Standard)
- **Summary**: 人类评估在长文本生成中至关重要，但协议细节常缺失。本文对2023-2025年*CL论文进行大规模分析，定义20项可报告准则。发现评估设计的重要方面普遍报告不足，并提出改进建议。

<h3 id="284">[284] POISE: Position-Aware Undetectable Skill Injection on LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07943](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07943) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haochang Hao, Dehai Min, Zhifang Zhang, Yunbei Zhang, Miao Xu, Yingqiang Ge, Lu Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 技能注入攻击面临可靠性与隐蔽性的权衡。本文提出位置感知攻击POISE，将触发指令压缩成单个看似无害的正文指令，实现89.3%攻击成功率，同时保持隐蔽性。现有静态防御无效。

<h3 id="285">[285] From `May' to `Is': Certainty Distortion in Language Model Rewriting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07951) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Catarina G Belem, Shang Wu, Hongyu Yao, Mark Steyvers, Sameer Singh, Padhraic Smyth
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 语言模型在改写任务中可能改变原文的确定性表述。本文提出基于LM的评估指标，发现75%的输出存在确定性扭曲，且倾向提高确定性（1.5-2倍）。多次改写后效果累积，在医疗领域尤为明显。

<h3 id="286">[286] Minibatch Selection via Partition Matroid Constrained Gradient Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07954) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prayas Agrawal, Prateek Chanda, Ishita Khatri, Ganesh Ramakrishnan, Bamdev Mishra, Pratik Jawanpuria
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Data Selection, Optimization
- **Summary**: 在异构数据上训练LLM需要选择平衡收敛速度和覆盖的小批次。本文提出PartitionSel，通过分区拟阵约束下的梯度匹配效用进行跨域选择。实验在Qwen和Llama上取得稳健提升，并减少了梯度冲突对。

<h3 id="287">[287] RecurGuard: Runtime Monitoring for Reasoning-Token Consumption Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07968) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abid Aziz, Hafsa Binte Kibria
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Security, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 推理型LLM可能被诱导将生成预算消耗在注入的诱饵任务上。本文构建运行时监控RecurGuard，分析推理轨迹中的重复率、卷增长和任务进展信号。在DS-R1-Qwen-7B上检测率99%（OverThink）和92%（ExtendAttack），假阳性率极低。

<h3 id="288">[288] Neutrality Bites: Gender Representation in AI-Generated Animal Stories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07969) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Imani Finkley, Yuanxi Li, Melanie Walsh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Bias Mitigation, Text Generation
- **Summary**: AI生成故事中的性别偏见已受关注，但中立性策略是否真正公平待考。本文使用6个LLM生成动物故事，发现模型常避免性别指定（19%）或使用中性语言（38.2%），但指定性别时男性角色占40.6%，女性仅2.2%。中立性可能加剧边缘群体抹除。

<h3 id="289">[289] Defending Against Malicious Finetuning by Scaling Train-time Adversarial Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07970](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07970) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoming Wen, Shi Chen, Qingyu Shi, Siyuan Liu, Minrui Luo, Jingzhao Zhang, Tianxing He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Adversarial Training
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/haomingwen/patcher)
- **Summary**: 当前开放权重的大语言模型容易受到恶意微调攻击。本文提出Patcher方法，采用对抗训练和双层优化思想，通过扩大对抗循环中的优化步骤来迫使模型参数对更强攻击不敏感，并设计了高效并行算法。实验表明，Patcher显著提升了模型鲁棒性，并能迁移到多种攻击场景和模型规模。

<h3 id="290">[290] PRISM: PRior-guided Imagination Sampling in world Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07974) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhai Wang, Jiawei Xia, Rongxuan Zhou, Xiao Hu, Yongliang Shi, Jing Du, Yang Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 在基于世界模型的连续控制规划中，候选动作的选择至关重要。本文提出PRISM框架，从世界模型自身学习到的表征中提取动作先验，并通过精度加权的乘积高斯更新将其融入规划器的采样分布。该方法在Cube和PushT任务上分别将成功率提高了35和32个百分点，且无显著推理开销。

<h3 id="291">[291] MC-PDD: Masked Corpus-Level Pretraining Data Detection for Black-Box Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07996](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07996) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaixin Lan, Mu You, Tao Fang, Binkai Ou, Lidia S. Chao, Derek F. Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Attribution, Benchmark Contamination
- **Summary**: 现有预训练数据检测方法需要模型概率分布，不适用于仅提供输入输出接口的闭源大语言模型。本文提出MC-PDD方法，受掩码语言建模启发，通过遮罩特定词并让模型预测，比较候选语料与参照非成员语料的预测命中率差异来统计判断。实验表明，在更严格的黑盒设置下，MC-PDD性能与现有方法相当。

<h3 id="292">[292] Enhancing AI Interpretability and Safety through Localised Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ian Seet, Jonas Bozenhard, Simon Osterman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, AI Safety, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 大型生成式AI模型的解释性、安全性和可持续性令人担忧。本文论证了局部化架构（低带宽、每节点高表达性）在解释性和计算效率上的潜在优势，并评估了多种硬件机器学习范式对实现这些架构的适用性。

<h3 id="293">[293] Summarization is Not Dead Yet</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08000) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongqi Liu, Chenxi Whitehouse, Zheng Zhao, Zhuchen Cao, Jian Li, Yabiao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, LLM Evaluation, Fact Checking
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型生成的摘要是否已超越人类的问题，本文进行了多维度评估。结果显示，人类参考摘要在信息量和忠实度上仍具优势，而模型输出仅在表面连贯性和流畅性上更受欢迎。事实核查表明人类参考更可靠，且模型输出存在风格同质化现象。

<h3 id="294">[294] Rewrite to Translate, Translate to Reward: Reinforcement Learning for Source Rewriting in Machine Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08011) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boxuan Lyu, Haiyue Song, Zhi Qu, Hidetaka Kamigaito, Kotaro Funakoshi, Manabu Okumura
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Reinforcement Learning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 现有通过大语言模型改写源文本来提升机器翻译质量的方法需要为每个翻译模型手动调优提示词。本文提出RLSR框架，利用强化学习直接以下游翻译质量的提升作为奖励来训练改写模型。实验表明，4B参数的改写模型显著优于无改写基线和同等规模的提示改写基线，与235B模型的提示方法性能相当。

<h3 id="295">[295] GVC-Seg: Training-Free 3D Instance Segmentation via Geometric Visual Correspondence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08014](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08014) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liang Xu, Fangjing Wang, Jinyu Yang, Feng Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Open-Vocabulary Segmentation, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有基于多个基础模型的3D实例分割方法存在置信度偏差问题。本文提出GVC-Seg，一种无需训练的3D实例分割方法，通过几何视觉对应来缓解偏差，并引入3D提案生成和掩码感知的CLIP特征提取。在多个基准上达到最先进性能，并在开放词汇语义分割中表现优异。

<h3 id="296">[296] IEA: Amateur-Friendly Conversational Image Editing Agent via Three Stages of Multitask Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08016) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zichen Zhu, Yuheng Sun, Mingxuan Zhu, Wenjie Ma, Situo Zhang, Zhexiang Wang, Ziyue Yang, Danyang Zhang, Kunyao Lan, Zihan Zhao, Dingye Liu, Siqi Xiang, Lu Chen, Kai Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Vision-Language Model, Tool Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/OpenDFM/Image_Edit_Agent)
- **Summary**: 当前图像编辑软件对普通用户不友好，生成模型编辑结果常有瑕疵。本文提出IEA，一个对话式图像编辑智能体，通过学习操作参数化工具在显式动作空间中逐步编辑，并经过三阶段多任务训练。定量实验显示编辑任务像素距离更低，摘要任务ROUGE-L更高，用户研究在指令遵循和感知质量上均优于基线。

<h3 id="297">[297] Repair Before Veto, When Repair Is Hidden: Quantum-Accessible Features for Repair-Augmented Constraint Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08020) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Constraint Satisfaction
- **Summary**: 硬约束决策系统通常否决不可行候选方案，但若存在修复则不应否决。本文提出Q-RACL框架，定义修复优先的决策语义，并识别出量子特征可提供关键支持。在离散对数隐藏的修复族上，量子策略的虚假否决率低于1.1%，而经典策略接近随机水平。

<h3 id="298">[298] Semantic Quorum Assurance: Collective Certification for Non-Deterministic AI Infrastructure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08021](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08021) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun He, Deying Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 大语言模型智能体在自主云操作中可能生成语法正确但操作不安全的变更。本文提出语义仲裁保证（SQA），将提案路由到多样化的验证智能体面板，并通过风险自适应的仲裁谓词聚合判决。在500个基础设施场景中，将不安全审批率从18.5%降至0.3%，中位延迟1.45-4.12秒。

<h3 id="299">[299] CausShield: Sample Reconstruction-Resilient Vertical FL via Causal Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08027) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongqi Jiang, Yansong Gao, Siguang Chen, Anmin Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Privacy, Causal Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 纵向联邦学习面临主动样本重建攻击，现有防御难以平衡效用与隐私。本文基于结构因果模型提出CausShield，将共享表征分解为任务相关（因果）和无关部分，通过无监督表征学习求解优化问题。实验证明在隐私保护、模型效用和计算效率上均优于七种最先进方法。

<h3 id="300">[300] Voting Protocols as Coordination Mechanisms for Role-Constrained Multi-Agent Tutoring Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08030) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric S. Qiu, Joyce Gill
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Education Technology, Social Choice
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on AI4Good
- **Summary**: 多智能体辅导系统面临协调挑战：多个智能体提出不同干预方案。本文研究了四种投票协议（简单、排名、累积、批准）在四类角色约束教学智能体中的作用。在1200次模拟中，投票协议类型显著影响最终响应和协调模式，且短暂辅导回合即可带来可测量的学习增益。

<h3 id="301">[301] Sci-Rho: A Multilingual Visually-Grounded Symbolic Benchmark for STEM Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08034) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Falensi Azmi, Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif, Vallerie Alexandra Putra, Adi Yeltay, Abdullah Mubarak, Fajri Koto
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Vision-Language Model, STEM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有符号基准多限于数学推理、缺乏视觉基础且仅为英文。本文提出Sci-Rho，一个多语言、视觉基础的STEM问题动态基准，涵盖五门学科和七种语言，由领域专家设计可执行代码模板。评估17个视觉语言模型发现，最差准确率与平均准确率存在显著差距，小模型在多语言下性能下降，注意力头分析揭示了跨语言差异。

<h3 id="302">[302] GIScholarBench: Benchmarking LLM Overconfidence in GIS Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08036) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zongrng Li, Mingzheng Yang, Lei Zou, Hongxu Ma, Hao Tian, Siqi Zhou, Wenjing Gong, Kaili Zhang, Bingqian Chen, Mitch Zhang, Yifan Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Confidence Calibration
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在学术研究中常表现出过度自信。本文构建GIScholarBench基准，包含10865篇GIS论文，评估元数据检索、文献链接和研究方向生成三种任务。结果显示所有模型在各类任务中均持续过度自信，生成确定性内容时可靠性不足，AI生成的研究方向覆盖度和多样性较低。

<h3 id="303">[303] SafeECGMatch: Calibration-Aware Joint Frequency and Time Space Semi-Supervised Learning for Open-Set ECG Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08037) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongkyu Koh, Ikbeom Jang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Semi-Supervised Learning, Time Series Analysis
- **Venue**: KDD-UC 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/labhai/SafeECGMatch)
- **Summary**: 心电分类中标签稀缺且未标注池常含分布外异常。本文提出SafeECGMatch，一个校准感知的安全半监督学习框架，采用双分支时频表征，通过自适应标签平滑和温度缩放进行分布外拒绝和可靠伪标签。在PTB-XL等基准上达到最先进准确率和校准性能。

<h3 id="304">[304] When Behavioral Safety Evaluation Fails: A Representation-Level Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08044](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08044) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Enyi Jiang, Anders Gjølbye, Yibo Jacky Zhang, Sanmi Koyejo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 大语言模型的安全评估通常基于行为层面，无法反映内部鲁棒性。本文通过构建保持安全行为但潜在脆弱的解耦模型，提出基于干预的评估框架和潜在脆弱性分数（LVS）。实验表明，行为安全指标不足以衡量表征层面的鲁棒性，解耦模型在有害干预下LVS显著升高。

<h3 id="305">[305] What's the Point? Spatial Grammar & Index Resolution for Sign Language Processing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08056](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08056) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oline Ranum, Simon Hadfield, Richard Bowden
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sign Language Processing
- **Summary**: 手语模型未充分建模非词汇结构（如空间索引），该框架通过索引检测和话语实体链接实现索引感知建模，将索引专家作为辅助模块集成到冻结的SLR模型中。实验表明索引占手语内容的10%-15%但恢复较差，所提方法实现了自动标注和非词汇结构建模。

<h3 id="306">[306] EgoAERO: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from a Single Egocentric Video without Object Assets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08057) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yichen Niu, Haoran Lv, Xinrui Zhang, Xueyao Wan, Shiyu Gao, Ying Ai, Hui Xu, Yongqi Hu, Hengyi Zhang, Yang Xie, Zhaxizhuoma, Yue Zhao, Zhenshan Bing, Yan Ding, Jianxing Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dexterous Manipulation, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 从单一人称RGB-D视频学习灵巧操作缺乏物体资产，提出EgoAERO框架，通过无物体资产的跟踪与重建、自适应接触优化重建手-物轨迹，并采用两阶段残差学习转换为机器人策略。在仿真和真实实验中，单次演示即实现灵巧操作，性能接近基于CAD的重建方法。

<h3 id="307">[307] Robust-U1: Can MLLMs Self-Recover Corrupted Visual Content for Robust Understanding?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08063) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaqi Tang, Jianmin Chen, Youyang Zhai, Wei Wei, Runtao Liu, Mengjie Zhao, Xiangyu Wu, Qingfa Xiao, Qifeng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jqtangust/Robust-U1)
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型在真实视觉损坏下性能下降，现有鲁棒性方法缺乏可解释性或无法恢复像素细节。提出Robust-U1框架，包含监督微调初始恢复、强化学习优化视觉质量（SSIM+CLIP奖励）和多模态推理。在真实损坏基准上达到SOTA，并在对抗性损坏下保持较高VQA性能。

<h3 id="308">[308] "I understand your perspective": LLM Persuasion and Sycophancy through the Lens of Communicative Action Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08076) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Esra Dönmez, Agnieszka Falenska
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Persuasion, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: LLMs的说服能力和逢迎行为尚未从交际行为角度探索。基于哈贝马斯的交际行动理论，分析LLM生成的论辩中的言外意图。发现LLMs的言外意图表达常超过人类，且善于生成迎合观点持有者的逢迎回应。众包工作者更偏好LLM生成的论据，表明其说服力源于对人类交流模式的模仿。

<h3 id="309">[309] Aligned but Not Partner-Specific: Distinguishing How Multimodal LLM Agents Succeed in Reference Games Without Human-Like Conventions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08081) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Po-Ya Angela Wang, Chinmaya Mishra, Aslı Özyürek, Paula Rubio-Fernández, Esam Ghaleb
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, LLM Agent, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 多模态LLM在参照游戏中虽能形成对齐，但缺乏人类那种基于伙伴特定的约定。通过对比人类与智能体对，提出受约束伪对基线方法。结果表明人类通过压缩描述降低努力，而智能体始终使用冗长描述，标签重叠率接近天花板且与伪对无异。LLM通过冗长描述而非形成简约定制表达实现协调。

<h3 id="310">[310] Fast LLM-Based Semantic Filtering: From a Unified Framework to an Adaptive Two-Phase Method</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08090) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kyoungmin Kim, Martin Catheland, Anastasia Ailamaki
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Data Processing
- **Summary**: 基于LLM的语义过滤成本高昂，现有级联方法存在四项不足。提出自适应两阶段方法，组合模型无关聚类和在线代理，采用混合词感知模型和软标签训练，并针对稀疏样本添加安全边际。在三个10K文档语料库、90%准确率目标下，速度比最优方法快1.6-2.0倍，达到95%查询的目标，并给出未来加速空间。

<h3 id="311">[311] vla.cpp: A Unified Inference Runtime for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08094](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08094) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Khanh D. Nguyen, Hung T. Ho, Chinh T. Nguyen, Thanh Q. Duong, Linh D. Le, Duy M. H. Nguyen, Vien A. Ngo, An T. Le
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Edge Computing, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略通常依赖工作站GPU，不适合机器人硬件。提出vla.cpp，基于ggml的便携C++推理运行时，原生支持流匹配和扩散VLA推理模式。在LIBERO-Object上匹配SOTA，BitVLA在1.3GiB内存下达到100%成功率，可跨三种硬件层级运行，IMMA GEMM减少4.5倍延迟。

<h3 id="312">[312] Continual Quadruped Robots Coordination via Semantic Skill Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08102) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daoqing Wang, Yuchen Xiao, Weixuan Huang, Zhilong Zhang, Shenghua Wan, Meng Li, Lei Yuan, Yang Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Multi-Agent Coordination, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 多四足机器人协调在开放式持续学习中面临任务顺序到达和灾难性遗忘的挑战。提出Conquer框架，基于语义技能库的检索-适应-更新过程，使用团队结构SAG骨干支持可变规模团队。仿真最终平均成功率95.6%，遗忘可忽略；在Unitree Go2上验证了实际部署可行性。

<h3 id="313">[313] Ego-Pi: VLA Fine-Tuning for Ego-Centric Human and Robot Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08107) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ji Woong Kim, Ke Wang, Zipeng Fu, Sirui Chen, Cong Zhao, Jeff Lai, Chelsea Finn
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 机器人学面临数据稀缺问题，利用第一人称人类数据的前景广阔但研究不足。研究跨人类和人形机器人（灵巧手）的学习设计选择，基于π0.5模型。结果表明人类数据可使机器人学习新任务语义并组合已有技能，无需相应的机器人数据。

<h3 id="314">[314] Human-Centered Benchmarking of Driver Monitoring Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08123) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruben Dario Florez-Zela
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Model Evaluation, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rubendflorezzela/hcbf-driver-monitoring)
- **Summary**: 基于视觉的驾驶员监控模型仅用准确率评估不足以反映真实部署适宜性。提出HCBF框架，从准确率、可解释性、效率和鲁棒性四维度评估四种轻量架构。模型在干净集上准确率相近但各擅胜场；ShuffleNetV2综合排名第一但在噪声下性能骤降，而Transformer保持鲁棒。

<h3 id="315">[315] LCAM: A Framework for Diagnosing Interactional Alignment Failures in Con-versational AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08131](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08131) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Manuele Reani, Hongyu Tian
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, AI Ethics, Human-Agent Interaction
- **Summary**: 对话式AI的许多危害源于交互失准。提出分层认知对齐模型（LCAM），定义五层对齐和两种失准极性。应用于LLM咨询案例，展示看似支持性的回应可能强化有害信念、模拟不当关怀并模糊角色边界，为审计和治理提供理论视角。

<h3 id="316">[316] LogNEO: A GPT-Neo Reinforcement Learning Framework for Accurate Real-Time Log Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Eje, Tanmay Sharma, Khush Patel, Manuel Mazzara, Leonard Johard
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Log Analysis, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 大规模系统日志异常检测对可靠性和安全性至关重要。提出LogNEO，基于GPT-Neo（1.3B参数）采用PPO和位置感知奖励微调。在HDFS、BGL、Thunderbird上F1分别达0.927、0.913、0.984，召回率提升6个百分点；生产环境延迟45ms，吞吐量15000事件/秒。

<h3 id="317">[317] RAPID: Layer-Wise Redundancy-Aware Pruning and Importance-Driven Token Merging for Efficient ViT</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08156) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kyumin Choi, Ikbeom Jang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Model Compression, Token Pruning
- **Summary**: Vision Transformer计算成本高，现有令牌缩减忽略深度表征演化。提出RAPID，在浅中层使用冗余感知剪枝，深层转换到重要性感知合并。在ImageNet-1K上建立优于ToMe和ToFu的准确率-压缩Pareto前沿，极端压缩下准确率高出4.29%。

<h3 id="318">[318] Constrained Paraphrase Consistency for LLM Hallucination Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08158](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08158) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shanshan Lin, Dongsheng Hong, Sibo Ju, Chao Chen, Xi Zhang, Xiangwen Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination
- **Venue**: ICASSP 2026
- **Summary**: 现有幻觉检测器依赖扩充训练集，成本高且引入偏差，未充分利用语义等价的释义一致性。提出CCHD，将训练形式化为约束优化问题，加入释义一致性约束和标签保留约束，通过梯度下降-上升法求解。在DeBERTa和Flan-T5骨干上，在事实性基准上持续优于FactCG、MiniCheck等强基线。

<h3 id="319">[319] Explaining Data Mixing Scaling Laws</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08167) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Dai, Shuran Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scaling Laws, Data Mixing, Training Dynamics
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/meiqwq/Explaining-Data-Mixing-Scaling-Laws)
- **Summary**: 多领域数据混合的经验缩放律缺乏理论解释。提出统一框架，将标准神经缩放律推广到多领域场景，识别能力竞争和噪声降低两个关键因素。在拟合损失曲面和预测高混合效率方面优于现有基线，参数更少且可跨规模外推。

<h3 id="320">[320] Closing the Sim-to-Real Gap: An Evaluation Framework for Autonomous Cyber Defense Configuration of Commercial EDR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08168](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08168) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kerri Prinos, Lilianne Brush
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Autonomous Systems, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 现有自主防御智能体评估无法捕捉从仿真到商业EDR的差异。提出首个针对商业EDR自主防御智能体的评估框架，在GOAD环境中实例化，使用NodeZero和Microsoft Defender XDR。报告三条经验教训：遥测面向SOC分析师、需要逐策略归因、EDR自主行为在评估窗口内变化，凸显仿真到部署的差距。

<h3 id="321">[321] CLASP: Language-Driven Robot Skill Selection and Composition using Task-Parameterized Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08169) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Markus Knauer, Valentin Gieraths, Tai Mai, Samuel Bustamante, Alin Albu-Schäffer, Freek Stulp, João Silvério
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Skill Learning, Vision-Language Model

<h3 id="337">[337] Self-Supervised Vision Transformers for CBCT-Based Detection of Temporomandibular Joint Osteoarthritis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08364) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shradhdha Trivedi, Vrundan Sojitra, Mariela Padilla
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision Transformer, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 针对颞下颌关节骨关节炎在CBCT影像中骨质改变细微、自动检测困难的问题，提出基于DINO系列自监督视觉Transformer的切片级检测流程。该方法使用冻结或部分微调的ViT骨干逐切片编码，并通过注意力多实例学习聚合进行患者级二分类。实验表明，部分解冻最后两个Transformer块是提升性能的关键，AUC从0.671提升至0.902，优于其他DINO变体和有监督基线。

<h3 id="338">[338] Pre-Intervention Prediction of Sparse Autoencoder Steering Side Effects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08365) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Evan Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对稀疏自编码器特征干预常产生不一致行为和附带效应的问题，提出一种干预前筛选框架，利用特征统计量预测干预的模块性（稳定性和附带扩散）。在多个模型和字典上的实验表明，解码器几何、激活统计等预测优于基线，但预测信号和迁移的模块性轴因模型而异。该框架可预先选择干预更干净的特征。

<h3 id="339">[339] Emergence World: A Platform for Evaluating Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Autonomy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08367) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deepak Akkil, Ravi Kokku, Karthik Vikram, Tamer Abuelsaad, Aditya Vempaty, Satya Nitta
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有LLM智能体评估多为短时任务，无法捕捉长期动态（如行为漂移、跨模型影响）。为此提出Emergence World，一个持续运行的多智能体仿真平台，基于实时数据、120+工具和三大记忆系统，通过民主机制自治。跨厂商15天研究显示不同模型产生从稳定治理到完全崩溃的巨大差异，为长时多智能体自主性评估提供了平台。

<h3 id="340">[340] An Information-Theoretic Definition for Open-Ended Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08369](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08369) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wanqiao Xu, Yifan Zhu, Benjamin Van Roy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Open-Ended Evolution, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对开放式学习缺乏统一定义和探索理论的问题，提出基于信息论的“比特等价”概念，量化达成各期望奖励所需的信息量。证明经典赌博机非开放环境，并构造了一个开放学习赌博机环境及相应算法，实现线性比特等价增长。

<h3 id="341">[341] RiskNet: A large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents from news with alignment and multi-dimensional annotations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08376](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08376) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
