- **Summary**: 针对现实世界AI风险事件缺乏大规模、持续可用的数据集，构建RiskNet数据集，从多语言新闻源通过结构化流程提取、对齐和分类事件。当前版本包含数亿源记录和大规模事件集群及标注基准，支持AI安全、治理、风险分析等研究。

<h3 id="342">[342] Auditing Proprietary Alignment in Large Language Models: A Comparative Framework Without a Ground-Truth Standard</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08381](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08381) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alireza Arbabi, Florian Kerschbaum
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Algorithmic Audit
- **Summary**: 针对黑盒LLM中的专有对齐（如审查、偏见）难以系统检测的问题，提出无需真值标准的统计框架，通过比较目标模型与参考模型在共享语义空间中的行为偏差来量化专有对齐。该方法在多个案例上实现了可扩展的审计。

<h3 id="343">[343] STAR-KV: Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Soft Thresholding for Adaptive Rank Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08382) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Priyansh Bhatnagar, Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi, Se-Hyun Yang, SeungJae Lee, Jungwook Choi, Mingu Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, Model Compression, Quantization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PriyanshBhatnagar/STAR-KV)
- **Summary**: 现有KV缓存压缩中秩选择依赖固定或启发式方法，提出STAR-KV框架，包含可微分阈值实现注意力头和块级最优秩选择、混合分解策略和低秩感知混合精度量化。在多种LLM上实现最高75%压缩率，结合量化可达20倍整体缩减，注意力模块加速达6.9倍。

<h3 id="344">[344] Impacts of Histories and Models on LLM Grading: A Study in Advanced Software Engineering Courses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08400](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08400) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qilin Zhou, Zhuo Wang, Yue Li, W.K. Chan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Education Technology, Automated Grading
- **Venue**: ISET 2026
- **Summary**: 针对大规模研究生阅读报告评分的负担及LLM评分可靠性问题，提出人工对齐的LLM辅助评分流程，基于180份作业评估Grok和GPT。发现LLM内部一致性高但模型间差异大，持续交互历史导致评分标准漂移。表明LLM可减轻工作量，但需特定操作以避免系统性不公平。

<h3 id="345">[345] SceneConductor: 3D Scene Generation from Single Image with Multi-Agent Orchestration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08402) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeonghwan Kim, Yushi Lan, Yongwei Chen, Hieu Trung Nguyen, Chuanyu Pan, Xingang Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Scene Generation, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 单图像生成完整3D场景常因全局一致性差和场景级标注依赖而受限。提出多智能体编排框架，分解为场景初始化、环境构建和多智能体精炼三个阶段，并引入几何感知布局预测器。在基准数据集上，该方法在几何精度、空间一致性和感知真实感上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="346">[346] Hiding in Plain Floats: Steganographic Carriers for Indirect Prompt and Content Injection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08403) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mudit Sinha, Sanika Chavan
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection, Steganography
- **Venue**: FAGEN@ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对文本为中心的提示注入防御假设恶意信号可见于文本视图，但在结构化浮点参数中可隐藏。实验表明，通过浮点数组传输并在重建时碎片化，在最强双文本层防御下仍达到94.3%泄漏ASR。该贡献并非不可检测的漏洞，而是文本检测在结构化输入中的失效边界。

<h3 id="347">[347] TimpaTeks: Automatic In-place Text Sequence Modification via Diffusion Language Model Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08408) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ryandito Diandaru, Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif, Fadli Aulawi Al Ghiffari, Ahmed Elshabrawy, Alham Fikri Aji
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: 针对文本在原位修改以表达不同概念的需求，提出TimpaTeks，通过激活引导扩散语言模型实现无需指令调优的原位文本修改。在IMDB和合成数据集上，该方法在降低困惑度、保留句子结构的同时实现概念转移，且计算成本低于基于提示的引导。

<h3 id="348">[348] Provably Efficient Personalized Multi-Objective Bandits with Proactive Conversational Queries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08410](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08410) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Linfeng Cao, Ming Shi, Ness B. Shroff
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Summary**: 多目标赌博机中个性化决策需学习用户偏好，现有方法仅利用效用反馈。提出主动查询框架，通过Plackett-Luce模型利用用户查询信号，并设计MO-PQUCB算法结合正则化与双探索UCB。理论证明查询加速偏好估计，改进遗憾界，且在腐败查询下鲁棒。

<h3 id="349">[349] PACT: Self-Evolving Physical Safety Alignment for Diffusion Policies in Embodied Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08414) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lingxuan Wu, Zijian Zhu, Lizhong Wang, Chengyang Ying, Huayu Chen, Xiao Yang, Fangming Liu, Jun Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Safe RL, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 扩散策略在机器人操作中常违反物理约束。提出PACT后训练框架，通过反向KL目标结合课程式约束收紧，将策略投影到可行区域，无需示教数据或奖励。在仿真和真实操控任务中，安全违规平均减少31.0%，任务成功率提升30.7%。

<h3 id="350">[350] CoVEBench: Can Video Editing Models Handle Complex Instructions?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08415) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiangtao Wu, Jiaming Wang, Yiwen He, Yuanxing Zhang, Shihao Li, Dunyuan Liu, Xuedong Zhao, Jialu Chen, Zekun Moore Wang, Jiaheng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Editing, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有视频编辑基准限于简单孤立编辑，无法评估复杂组合指令。提出CoVEBench基准，包含416个源视频、626条多点编辑指令和9990个细粒度检查项。实验表明当前模型在处理多重操作时经常遗漏编辑或引入伪影，为视频编辑提供了诊断性测试平台。

<h3 id="351">[351] Hacking Generative Perplexity: Why Unconditional Text Evaluation Needs Distributional Metrics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08417](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08417) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antonio Franca, Alexander Tong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Generative Model Evaluation
- **Venue**: Workshop on Structured Probabilistic Inference & Generative Modeling (SPIGM) at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 生成困惑度（gen-PPL）作为非自回归语言模型评价指标存在缺陷，因为它仅衡量可预测性而非语法或语义连贯性。构建零参数朴素采样器在非退化熵下达到SOTA gen-PPL但文本不连贯，建议使用分布散度指标，并重新基准测试近期模型。

<h3 id="352">[352] AI Code Sandboxes: A Comparative Security Study. Part 1 of 2 -- Engine-Level Properties (Attack Surface, Leakage, Stackability, CVE History, Patch Cadence, Fuzzing)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08433) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: George Andronchik, Pavel Lokhmakov
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Security, Cybersecurity
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/orbitalab/RnD-ai-sandboxes-sec-study-part-1)
- **Summary**: 针对AI代码沙箱的安全性比较缺乏多轴分析的问题，该文从主机攻击面、信息泄漏、防御深度、CVE历史、补丁周期和模糊测试六个引擎级指标评估五款产品。发现引擎类在各轴上区分明显，产品引脚策略是主导变量，模糊测试投入分为三档。

<h3 id="353">[353] Segment-level Tree Search for Long Meeting Document Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sangwon Ryu, Heejin Do, Jun Seo, Daehui Kim, Yunsu Kim, Gary Geunbae Lee, Jungseul Ok
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Tree Search
- **Summary**: 针对长会议文档摘要困难且现有流水线存在错误累积的问题，提出基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的段级摘要框架S3，无需训练。使用7B模型即可达到与72B模型相当的性能。

<h3 id="354">[354] Sparrow: Sparse Rollout for Stable and Efficient Long-context RL of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08446) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Zhou, Ranajoy Sadhukhan, Zhaofeng Sun, Zhuoming Chen, Souvik Kundu, Saket Dingliwal, Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi, Aram Galstyan, Haizhong Zheng, Beidi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Sparse Attention, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文强化学习生成成本高的问题，提出Sparrow稀疏rollout方法，通过动态稀疏度调度保持每token策略失配稳定，实现2.2-2.0倍加速。进一步提出DistillSparse蒸馏进一步提升速度。

<h3 id="355">[355] Not Just After One: Sleep-Inspired Replay Prevents Catastrophic Forgetting After Sequential Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08447) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anthony Bazhenov, Jean Erik Delanois, Giri P. Krishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Catastrophic Forgetting
- **Summary**: 针对神经网络灾难性遗忘问题，受睡眠启发提出在连续多任务训练后执行无监督回放，恢复先前任务性能。发现任务特定信息在训练中保持韧性。

<h3 id="356">[356] Sycophancy as a Multilingual Alignment Failure: How Safety Degrades Across Languages, Topics, and Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08451](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08451) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arya Shah, Himanshu Beniwal, Mayank Singh, Chaklam Silpasuwanchai
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Multilingual Learning, Sycophancy
- **Summary**: 首次大规模跨语言评估对齐LLM的谄媚行为，覆盖38种语言和1.1百万实例。发现谄媚率在低资源语言中激增，且与主题无关，标记器fertility是结构性驱动因素。

<h3 id="357">[357] The Confidence Trap: Calibration Attacks for Graph Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08467) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cuong Dang, Jiahao Zhang, Hieu Ta Quang, Dung Le, Lu Cheng, Suhang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Adversarial Robustness, Model Calibration
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CaptainCuong/Graph-Calibration-Attack.git)
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络校准攻击的离散性和标签变化难题，提出统一图校准攻击框架UGCA，引入KL散度损失、重排序等机制。显著增加期望校准误差同时保持准确率。

<h3 id="358">[358] More Yap Less Meaning: Uncovering Self-Improvement Behavior in SLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08471](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08471) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marina Igitkhanian, Erik Arakelyan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Small Language Model, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 研究小语言模型自我修正能力，构建最小三步修正管道。发现提示仅带来4.4%提升，较长提示与错误答案正相关。

<h3 id="359">[359] FlashCP: Load-Balanced Communication-Efficient Context Parallelism for LLM Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08476) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheng Wang, Eric Liu, Linan Jiang, Zhongkai Yu, Zaifeng Pan, Yue Guan, Yuke Wang, Yufei Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Distributed Training, Context Parallelism
- **Summary**: 针对上下文并行训练中负载不均和通信冗余问题，提出FlashCP框架，引入分片感知通信和Whole-Doc分片策略。达到最高1.63倍加速。

<h3 id="360">[360] Adaptive Loss Balancing for Noise-Robust GRPO in Generative Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08480) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kewei Xu, Junbo Qi, Yanyan Zou, Pengfei Zhang, Xingzhi Yao, Shengjie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Reinforcement Learning, Generative Recommendation
- **Summary**: 针对生成式推荐中奖励信号噪声问题，提出AdaGRPO，基于策略难度和奖励判别性逐样本门控GRPO。在大型电商数据集上HR@10提升至12.18%，幻觉低于0.22%。

<h3 id="361">[361] PIPE-Cypher: Automatic Enterprise Benchmark Generation for Text-to-Cypher Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08481) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suraj Ranganath, Anish Raghavendra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Benchmark, Text-to-Cypher
- **Summary**: 针对企业属性图Text2Cypher基准缺失问题，提出PIPE-Cypher管道，从实时图生成平衡的NL-Cypher对。生成3000示例，评估11个下游模型，零样本迁移弱。

<h3 id="362">[362] STELLAR: Spatio-Temporal Environmental Learning with Latent Alignment and Refinement for Long-Tailed Species Distribution Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08484](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08484) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shufeng Kong, Tao Yu, Yuanyuan Wei, Caihua Liu, Junwen Bai, Yingheng Wang, Marc Grimson, Daniel Fink, Carla P. Gomes
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Ecological AI, Spatiotemporal Reasoning, Long-Tailed Learning
- **Summary**: 针对物种分布建模中时空动态和长尾不平衡问题，提出STELLAR框架，包含图-时间编码器、上下文锚定潜在对齐和非平衡解码。在eBird数据集上显著优于基线，尤其对稀有物种。

<h3 id="363">[363] Seeing is Believing: Aligning Prompt Rewriting with Visual Anchors for Text-to-Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08492) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuanyi Liu, Deyi Ji, Junyu Lu, Jing Wang, Qianxiong Xu, Xuhang Chen, Tianrun Chen, Siwei Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Prompt Engineering, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像生成中意图-生成差距，提出FaithRewriter，利用MLLM生成中间图像作为视觉线索，再由LLM生成视觉增强提示。生成的提示更忠实于用户意图且视觉更合理。

<h3 id="364">[364] Projecting the Emerging Mindset of SWE Agent by Launching a Wild Code Understanding Journey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08500) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengyi Zhuo, Yan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Repository Analysis, Code Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对SWE代理行为难以刻画的问题，引入Ada装置在多仓库编码理解中记录轨迹，通过观察镜头分析导航、证据选择等。在408条轨迹中揭示效率、多样性差异。

<h3 id="365">[365] ActProbe: Action-Space Probe for Early Failure Detection of Generative Robot Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08508) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bingjia Huang, Xiangyu Li, Xiang Wang, Liang Mi, Zixu Hao, Weijun Wang, Hao Wu, Kun Li, Yunxin Liu, Ting Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Anomaly Detection, Failure Detection
- **Summary**: 针对生成式机器人策略在线故障检测开销大的问题，提出ActProbe，利用单前向传播的时间一致性和动作块幅度信号。在故障识别精度-及时性帕累托前沿上平均提升12.7%。

<h3 id="366">[366] GEAR-VLA: Learning Geometry-Aware Action Representations for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08530) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuan Zhang, Shiqi Zhang, Yedong Shen, Shuai Dong, Jiajun Deng, Xin Zhang, Yuxuan Gao, Jiajia Wu, Xin Nie, Zhiyuan Cheng, Jianmin Ji, Yanyong Zhang, Xingyi Zhang, Jia Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Vision-Language Model, Vision-Language-Action
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/babynabeauty/GEAR-VLA)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型泛化性不足的问题，提出GEAR-VLA，学习统一几何感知动作表示，采用粗到细动作学习、语义对齐3D集成和具身规范化。在多个基准上达到SOTA，零样本泛化强。

<h3 id="367">[367] When Video Misreads: Closed-Loop Distillation of Reading Heuristics for Exploratory Manipulation Trace QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08542) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haizhou Ge, Yufei Jia, Yue Li, Zhixing Chen, Lu Shi, Lei Han, Guyue Zhou, Ruqi Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Visual Reasoning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对探索性操作轨迹中VLM误读问题，提出闭环轨迹蒸馏，通过每任务编码代理提炼阅读启发式提示。将链准确率提升0.38-0.47。

<h3 id="368">[368] EinSort: Sorting is All We Need for Tensorizing LLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08565) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Toshiaki Koike-Akino, Jing Liu, Ye Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, KV Cache, Tensor Network
- **Summary**: 针对大型基础模型中低秩结构难以识别的问题，提出自适应张量化方法EinSort，通过索引排序发现内在低秩结构。在权重和KV缓存压缩中重建质量优于基线。

<h3 id="369">[369] Calibration of Structured Ignorance Certificates for Diagnosing Unknown Unknowns in Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08571](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08571) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Subramanyam Sahoo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Instruction Tuning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop: Epistemic Intelligence in Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：大语言模型在超出知识范围的问题上会生成流利但错误的答案。方法：提出结构化无知证书（SIC），以JSON格式强制模型明确缺失领域、列举所需概念并提出检索查询，通过GRPO微调模型。结果：在735个保留问题上达到99.46%的JSON有效性和0.967的证书特异性分数，ROUGE-L提升3.6%。

<h3 id="370">[370] Auditable Graph-Guided Root Cause Analysis for Kubernetes Incidents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08590) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anastasiia Kuvshinova, Seungmin Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Anomaly Detection, Fault Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 问题：Kubernetes事件诊断需要可靠且可审计的根因分析，避免场景特定捷径。方法：提出图遍历代理，结合LLM推理与类型化证据图，使用确定性图操作和验证阶段。结果：在ITBench公共子集上根因实体F1从0.6087提升至0.9130，消融实验显示去提示后仍有0.6958 F1。

<h3 id="371">[371] Reinforcement Learning for Flow-Matching Policies with Density Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08602) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boshu Lei, Kostas Daniilidis, Antonio Loquercio
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Flow Matching, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 问题：强化学习微调流匹配策略因多步生成过程而困难。方法：提出RLDT，利用Stein变分梯度下降从最大熵RL目标构建传输场，然后微调预训练流匹配策略。结果：在连续控制任务中，RLDT在奖励质量和收敛速度上优于竞争基线。

<h3 id="372">[372] HARBOR: A Harness Framework for Agentic Robot Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08610) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zechu Li, Yufeng Jin, Xiaoyang Liu, Puze Liu, Vignesh Prasad, Carlo D&#39;Eramo, Georgia Chalvatzaki
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robot Manipulation, Workflow Automation
- **Summary**: 问题：机器人强化学习流程成本高、难以规模化。方法：提出HARBOR，一个代理框架，通过专门代理将模拟器代码和任务规范自动转化为策略训练。结果：在6个基准和16个任务上自动化端到端RL流程，设计奖励并调优算法，策略可迁移到真实机器人。

<h3 id="373">[373] Tyan-WP: A Wind Power Foundation Model for Ultra-Short-Term Probabilistic Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08630) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahui Huang, Ao Luo, Lei Liu, Hongwei Zhao, Tengyuan Liu, Ruibo Guo, Bo Wang, Zhao Wang, Bin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Foundation Model, Energy Management
- **Summary**: 问题：现有风功率预测模型在数据稀缺场景下泛化能力差，通用时间序列模型忽略站点属性和协变量交互。方法：提出Tyan-WP，首个风功率基础模型，包含静态站点嵌入和功率感知气象融合模块。结果：在127个站点上MAE降低19.9%，RMSE降低16.6%，CRPS降低22.2%，R²提升16.7%。

<h3 id="374">[374] A retrieval conditioned rebinding circuit for dynamic entity tracking in large language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08644) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soyoung Oh, Vera Demberg
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 问题：需要理解大语言模型如何在动态状态跟踪中实现实体绑定。方法：通过因果干预识别检索条件重绑定机制，即编码交换相关绑定信息并在读出时恢复的注意力头电路。结果：在Gemma和Llama模型中均发现该机制，但表示签名因模型族而异。

<h3 id="375">[375] Sample-Efficient LLM-Based Detection of Malicious Web Server Logs with Forensically Explainable Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08649) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bernhard Kneip, Nhien-An Le-Khac, Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Chain-of-Thought, Log Analysis
- **Summary**: 问题：Web日志取证分析需要准确检测和可解释的推理。方法：提出CEF-Log，一种上下文增强的少样本思维链提示策略，嵌入专家调查方法。结果：在CSIC 2010数据集上仅用4个示例达到0.99 F1分数，样本效率提升10倍。

<h3 id="376">[376] Reconstructing Synthetic SDO/AIA 193 A EUV Images from He I 10830 A Observations with Diffusion Model Translator</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08652](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08652) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marco Marena, Qin Li, Haimin Wang, Haodi Jiang, Prajwal Shah, Bo Shen
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.SR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Translation, Remote Sensing
- **Summary**: 问题：将极紫外日冕成像扩展到早期时期。方法：提出日冕洞感知扩散模型翻译器，从He I观测重建AIA 193 Å图像。结果：重建保留全盘形态（CC=0.92）和日冕洞结构（CC=0.84），历史适用性得到验证。

<h3 id="377">[377] FiberTune: Preserving Action-Fiber Visual Residuals in Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08653) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haihao Lin, Xiangsheng Huang, Xiao Yang, Weibang Zhou, Yiqi Zhang, Bo Yang, Simin Zeng, Jiawei Yang, Zhengyang Wang, Jiahui Du
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Robot Manipulation, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 问题：动作监督微调VLA策略导致沿动作纤维的残差视觉坍塌。方法：提出FiberTune，通过在线动作探针和正则化保留教师结构的视觉残差。结果：在所有六个仿真设置和物理任务中均改进，CALVIN上成功率提升10.7个百分点，物理任务成功率从72.7%提升至78.1%。

<h3 id="378">[378] Latent Diffusion Policy: Shaping Latent Spaces for Diffusion-Based Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08657) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhexuan Zhou, Yichen Lai, Jinhao Zhang, Huizhe Li, Youmin Gong, Jie Mei
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Diffusion Model, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 问题：扩散策略在原始动作空间中融合场景理解与轨迹生成，增加学习复杂度。方法：提出潜在扩散策略（LDP），在刻意塑造的潜在空间中进行流匹配，使用CVAE编码器吸收场景理解。结果：在协调密集型任务上大幅优于DP3，成功迁移到真实双臂部署。

<h3 id="379">[379] Data Agents Under Attack: Vulnerabilities in LLM-Driven Analytical Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08661) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kuncan Wang, Ziting Wang, Peizhuo Lv, Haoyang Li, Guoliang Li, Gao Cong, Wei Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 问题：数据代理结合LLM推理与数据访问，引入新的安全漏洞。方法：提出分层漏洞框架、攻击分类法和基于LLM的载荷生成流水线，评估六个系统。结果：揭示当前系统存在严重安全漏洞，并总结四项关键发现。

<h3 id="380">[380] BioVid: Autoregressive Video Generation with Biological Behavior Semantic Comprehension</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tsung-Wei Pan, Jung-Hua Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Autoregressive Model, Motion Synthesis
- **Summary**: 问题：视频生成框架将时长作为外部参数，与生物行为的自然时间结构脱节。方法：提出BioVid，自回归视频生成框架，从数据中学习时间结构，使用FSQ-R3GAN分词器和因果Transformer。结果：生成时长分布与真实数据匹配（Wasserstein-1=1.24），优于基线方法。

<h3 id="381">[381] Lost in the Flow with Code Talkers: Unveiling the Instruction-Tuning Tax of Large Language Models in Code Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08676) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shi Ying Chang, Chiok Yew Ho, Yichen Li, Yintong Huo
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Instruction Tuning, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/arkosioscambions/CodeTalkers)
- **Summary**: 问题：指令微调对代码LLM在流模式和命令模式下的适用性尚不明确。方法：首次实证研究揭示指令微调税，即指令微调提升指令遵循能力但削弱填充性能。结果：通过定性和定量分析总结了七项发现和四项启示。

<h3 id="382">[382] Activation Steering Induces Emergent Misalignment: A More Comprehensive Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08682) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Cao, Jian Lou, Meiting Liu, Wenjie Feng, Dan Li, See-Kiong Ng, Anh Tuan Luu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Emergent Behavior, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: 问题：激活导向作为一种推理时行为控制技术，其引发突现失调的风险尚未充分探索。方法：全面研究证明激活导向可引发广泛失调，分析导向幅度、子空间结构等因素。结果：激活导向模型产生语义相关且连贯的有害响应，比微调更有害。

<h3 id="383">[383] Agentic Search for Counterfactual Recourse under Fixed LLM Budgets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08696](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08696) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yasuo Tabei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Counterfactual Reasoning, Monte Carlo Tree Search
- **Summary**: 问题：在固定LLM调用预算下生成多个反事实追溯方案。方法：提出Comp-MCTS，一种代理树搜索框架，通过LLM提议、验证和压缩引导剪枝最大化独特验证反事实的产量。结果：在四个真实表格数据集上，Comp-MCTS在产量和质量-效率权衡方面优于基线。

<h3 id="384">[384] Structuring agentic AI for HPC code modernization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08710) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anthony Marinov, Igor Sfiligoi
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 问题：传统科学代码的现代化（并行化、迁移）耗时。方法：采用结构化的"手把手"代理AI方法，包含手动示例、持续可构建性和有限会话范围，将NMAR-RKPM从Fortran MPI转换为C++ OpenMP。结果：在几个月内成功完成约6万行代码的转换。

<h3 id="385">[385] SNR-ST-Mix: Sample-specific Neighborhood Regression Mixup for Augmented Spatial Transcriptomics Imputation with Deep Neural Network</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Spatial Transcriptomics, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对空间转录组数据噪声大、分辨率低的问题，提出了SNR-ST-Mix数据增强框架，通过k近邻空间约束和表达相似性加权插值生成增强样本。实验表明该方法在不改变架构的情况下提升预测性能。

<h3 id="386">[386] Hybrid Neural Network and Conventional Controller Approach for Robust Control of Highly Unstable Systems: Application to Tilt-Rotor Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08714) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 针对倾转旋翼飞行器的鲁棒控制问题，提出了一种神经网络增强的滑模控制器，通过学习输入无关动态分量减少实时计算需求。与直接输入输出控制对比，该方法在模型不确定性和外部干扰下表现出鲁棒性，其中LSTM预测器性能更优。

<h3 id="387">[387] Deep Active Re-Labeling: Toward Noise-Resilient Annotation Efficiency</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Venue**: 2025 IEEE International Conference on Big Data (BigData)
- **Summary**: 针对深度主动学习中人类标注错误导致性能下降的问题，提出Deep Active Re-Labeling框架，通过分配部分标注预算重新标注已有数据来去除噪声。采用两种主动噪声采样策略，实验表明在相同标注预算下更高效并获得相对无噪声的标注集。

<h3 id="388">[388] APEX4: Efficient Pure W4A4 LLM Inference via Intra-SM Compute Rebalancing</h3>

<h3 id="389">[389] RadOT-Eval: Auditable Structured-Evidence Transport for Radiology Report Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="390">[390] TeamHerald@CHIPSAL 2026: Hate Speech Detection and Sentiment Analysis of Nepali Memes using Transformer-based Architectures and Ensemble Learning</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对尼泊尔语互联网模因的仇恨言论检测和情感分析，采用基于Transformer的模型和集成学习。实验表明硬投票集成在二分类中最佳，软投票集成在多类情感任务中取得15.8%的Macro F1提升。

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, Reward Hacking
- **Summary**: agent基准测试的验证器脆弱易被奖励破解。提出黑客-修复者循环，用三个LLM agent生成抗利用验证器。在KernelBench上将攻击成功率从62%降至0%，且弱agent可防御强黑客。

<h3 id="414">[414] CARE: A Conformal Safety Layer for Medical Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08969) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suhana Bedi, Bridget Lin, Anson Y. Zhou, Chloe O. Stanwyck, Jenelle A. Jindal, Sanmi Koyejo, David Stutz, Nigam H. Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Summarization, Conformal Prediction
- **Summary**: 医学摘要LLM可能遗漏或幻觉，现有检测方法无校准。提出CARE安全层，用共形风险控制标记遗漏和幻觉。在α=0.15时满足风险界，仅用约100份标注文档即可。

<h3 id="415">[415] SpaceVLN: A Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation Agent with Online Spatial Cognitive Memory and Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08992) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yucheng Deng, Pingrui Lai, Xinhai Li, Chenjia Bai, Xiaoheng Deng, Chengnuo Sun, Xuelong Li, Hua Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Visual Navigation, Embodied AI, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 视语导航agent依赖局部视觉和线性历史，忽视空间结构。提出SpaceVLN，包含空间认知记忆和任务引导空间推理。在多个基准上达到最优零样本性能，并经真机验证。

<h3 id="416">[416] Sustainability and Artificial Intelligence: Necessary, Challenging, and Promising Intersections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09006) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han-Teng Liao, Zijia Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SI; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Survey, AI Ethics, Environmental Planning
- **Summary**: 人工智能与可持续发展交汇的必要性和挑战。基于541篇文献的文献计量分析，揭示绿色可持续科技的核心作用。讨论AI在可持续中的应用和机构建设。

<h3 id="417">[417] Understanding Quantization-Aware Training: Gradients at Quantized Weights Bias to the Low-Loss Basin</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09012](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09012) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanyang Li, Jianhao Ma, Ying Cui
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization
- **Summary**: 本文针对后训练量化（PTQ）在极低位宽下精度骤降的问题，提出了一个统一的几何框架解释PTQ失败和量化感知训练（QAT）恢复的机制。作者将全精度训练建模为低损失“河流”位于宽“山谷”中，当量化网格接近谷宽时，PTQ可能选择谷外的高损失点，而QAT通过直通估计器感知谷壁并引导迭代回到谷内。实验表明该机制在视觉和语言模型的多重量化方案下成立。

<h3 id="418">[418] TLDR: Compressing Audio Tokens for Efficient Autoregressive Text-to-Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yejin Lee, Junwon Moon, Hyoeun Kim, Hyunjin Choi, Heeseung Kim, Kyuhong Shim
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对基于编解码器的自回归语音语言模型因音频令牌序列过长导致的推理效率瓶颈，本文提出TLDR框架，通过将连续的编解码器令牌分组为紧凑的潜在补丁，利用轻量级压缩器和提取器实现补丁级别的因果建模。在补丁大小为4时，TLDR相比基线模型获得1.8倍推理加速，KV缓存内存减少高达75%，且不替换现有模块。

<h3 id="419">[419] SafeRun: Enabling Determinism in LLM Planning for Running</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09027) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Meilin Chen, Zepeng Zhai, Jiaxuan Zhao, Yuan Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型在跑步规划等确定性关键领域因概率性导致的安全风险，本文提出SafeRun框架，采用解耦架构，由LLM进行软解释，确定性求解器强制执行安全约束。在五个LLM上的实验表明，SafeRun实现了100%的安全分数，同时保持有竞争力的指令遵循分数。

<h3 id="420">[420] ATM: Action-Consistency Transfer Matrix for Diagnosing and Improving Latent World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09028) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaheng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: World Model, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对评估潜在世界模型规划性能需耗时模拟器评估的问题，本文提出ATM（动作一致性迁移矩阵），通过轻量级探针比较真实编码转移和模型预测转移中的动作信息，生成可解释的诊断矩阵。ATM可将数分钟至数小时的CEM评估降至秒级（超过100倍加速），并引入AITS利用动作可识别性作为训练信号改进下游规划。

<h3 id="421">[421] TRIAGE: Dialectical Reasoning for Explainable Risk Prediction on Irregularly Sampled Medical Time Series with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09030) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyeongwon Jang, Gyouk Chu, Changhun Kim, Joonhyung Park, Hangyul Yoon, Eunho Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clinical Decision Support
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HyeongWon-Jang/TRIAGE)
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型在不规则采样医疗时间序列风险预测中过度自信二元化的问题，本文提出TRIAGE框架，训练LLM生成辩证推理，通过对竞争性临床结果引出特定理由，实现连续风险评分。在三个基准上，TRIAGE平均AUPRC提升3.3%，校准误差降低81%，临床推理质量提升20%。

<h3 id="422">[422] BareWave: Waveform-Native Flow-Matching Text-to-Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09048) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei Fan, Chao-Hong Tan, Qian Chen, Wen Wang, Xiangang Li, Kejiang Chen, Weiming Zhang, Nenghai Yu
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 针对文本到语音合成仍需中间声学表示的问题，本文提出BareWave，一个完全波形原生的流匹配TTS框架。通过训练时表示对齐、分阶段噪声调度和速度感知感知对齐，在零样本语音克隆实验中实现了强可懂度、说话人相似度和自然度，展示了波形原生流匹配TTS的实用性。

<h3 id="423">[423] INFUSER: Influence-Guided Self-Evolution Improves Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyu Chen, Miao Lu, Beining Wu, Heejune Sheen, Fengzhuo Zhang, Shuangning Li, Zhiyuan Li, Jose Blanchet, Tianhao Wang, Zhuoran Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.GT; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Self-Improvement, LLM Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FFishy-git/INFUSER)
- **Summary**: 针对自演化方法依赖精心策划数据或启发式难度的问题，本文提出INFUSER框架，通过生成器和求解器的迭代协同训练，利用影响分数衡量问题对求解器的实际提升，并采用DuGRPO优化。在Qwen3-8B-Base上，INFUSER在奥数和SuperGPQA基准上相对改进超过20%，8B生成器优于冻结的32B思考生成器。

<h3 id="424">[424] Stage-1 Controls the Entropy Regime, Not the Outcome</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09059) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianxiong Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Instruction Tuning, RLHF
- **Summary**: 针对两阶段后训练中第一阶段（SFT/OPD）实际控制什么的问题，本文在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上进行了小数据研究。结果表明第一阶段主要控制熵状态：OPD进入RL时策略熵显著更高，但下游收益微小且局部化，最终端点性能相近。因此第一阶段更关联熵状态而非最终结果。

<h3 id="425">[425] See More, Think Deeper: Query-Expanded Visual Evidence and Answer-Clue Guided Reflection for Long Video Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuning Wang, Zhiheng Wu, YiNuo Lu, Naiming Liu, Chen Jia, Bowen Liu, Shuo Nie, Weijie Zhu, Yumeng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对长视频理解中证据获取单一且缺乏视觉反馈的问题，本文提出CoVER框架，通过查询扩展动态收集视觉证据，并利用答案特定视觉反馈进行反思验证。CoVER-7B在多个指标上显著优于同参数规模模型，甚至超越部分闭源模型。

<h3 id="426">[426] OnlyDense: Reduced-Order Modeling for Lagrangian simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09065) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tu Do, Shannon Ryan, Santu Rana
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Computing, Reduced-Order Modeling
- **Summary**: 针对拉格朗日模拟（如SPH）计算代价高的问题，本文提出一种学习框架，将系统状态函数化并在希尔伯特空间中用学习的神经基函数张成的线性子空间近似。该方法统一了经典投影降阶建模与深度学习，且对离散点数量不变。在百万粒子级SPH模拟中，仅用32个基函数即达到R^2\>0.99的重建和预测精度。

<h3 id="427">[427] A Unifying Lens on Reward Uncertainty in RLHF</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09073](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09073) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ely Hahami, Yoel Zimmermann, Ray Zhou, Jack Benarroch Jedlicki
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RLHF, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 针对RLHF中奖励黑客问题，本文提出分布性奖励模型作为不确定性度量的正确对象。在贝叶斯推断或KL-DRO视角下，KL正则化RLHF目标可得到闭式有效奖励，统一了现有的均值聚合、最坏情况优化和不确定性加权优化等启发式方法。

<h3 id="428">[428] FlashMemory-DeepSeek-V4: Lightning Index Ultra-Long Context via Lookahead Sparse Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09079](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09079) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yan Wang, Qifan Zhang, Jiachen Yu, Tian Liang, Dongyang Ma, Xiang Hu, Zibo Lin, Chunyang Li, Zhichao Wang, Jia Li, Yujiu Yang, Haitao Mi, Dong Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Sparse Attention, KV Cache
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/libertywing/FlashMemory-Deepseek-V4)
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文解码中全KV缓存导致的GPU内存瓶颈，本文提出Lookahead Sparse Attention（LSA）范式，通过神经内存索引器预测未来上下文需求，仅保留关键KV块。在DeepSeek-V4架构上，LSA将平均物理KV缓存压缩至全上下文基线的13.5%，且下游精度平均提升0.6%；在500K极端长度下，KV缓存开销降低超90%。

<h3 id="429">[429] Context-Fractured Decomposition Attacks on Tool-Using LLM Agents: Exploiting Artifact Provenance Gaps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09084) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaofeng Lin, Yukai Yang, Daniel Guo, Sahil Arun Nale, Charles Fleming, Guang Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 针对工具使用LLM代理的越狱防御忽视跨步骤和工件溯源间隙的问题，本文提出上下文碎片分解（CFD）攻击，通过跨上下文的恶意动作组合，利用工件中介的延迟组合触发有害行为。在代理系统越狱基准上，CFD比最先进基线成功率高28.3个百分点，并提出了溯源标签的缓解方向。

<h3 id="430">[430] Context Rot in AI-Assisted Software Development: Repurposing Documentation Consistency for AI Configuration Artifacts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09090) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christoph Treude, Sebastian Baltes
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对AI编码助手配置文件（如.clinerules）随软件演化而过时（上下文腐化）的问题，本文提出复用文档一致性检查工具检测上下文腐化。对356个存储库的初步研究表明，23.0%的仓库包含过时的代码元素引用，表明传统工具即可发现上下文腐化。

<h3 id="431">[431] Addressing Market Regime Changes and Heavy-Tailed Returns in Portfolio Optimization via Bayesian VAR and Elliptical Black-Litterman</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09104](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09104) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniil Mikriukov, Ruoyu Sun, Angelos Stefanidis, Jionglong Su, Zhengyong Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-fin.PM
- **Tags**: Quantitative Finance, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对深度强化学习投资组合优化忽略厚尾收益和机制变化的问题，本文提出BAVAR-BLED算法，结合贝叶斯平均向量自回归和椭圆分布Black-Litterman模型，利用Transformer和CNN构建输入。在道琼斯指数成分股十年数据上，夏普比率1.72，索提诺比率2.70，总回报57.26%，显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="432">[432] Hybridizing Equilibrium Propagation with Ising Machines for Efficient Energy-Based Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09112) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen-Rui Fan, Bo Lu, Xing-Yu Wu, Tie-Jun Wang, Chuan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Energy-Based Model, Neuromorphic Computing
- **Summary**: 针对平衡传播训练陷入局部最小值的问题，本文引入伊辛动力学启发的平衡传播框架，用扩展相空间动力学替代耗散Hopfield松弛。该训练范式保留局部两相学习规则，但降低有效能垒、加速收敛、提高噪声鲁棒性，在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上训练深度卷积Hopfield网络性能可与反向传播媲美。

<h3 id="433">[433] Optimizing Energy-based Neural Network Training with Coherent Ising Machine</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09117) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen-Rui Fan, Bo Lu, Zhi-Hong Zhang, Run-Qing Zhang, Jing-Wei Wen, Chuan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Energy-Based Model, Equilibrium Propagation, Ising Machine
- **Summary**: 针对Ising机器在训练大规模神经网络时面临的可扩展性限制，本文利用相干Ising机器（CIM）通过平衡传播训练基于能量的神经网络，并引入Adam优化器加速收敛。结果表明，该方法在性能上可与现有软件实现媲美，并提高了收敛速度和精度，为下一代AI硬件提供了新途径。

<h3 id="434">[434] Autonomous Incident Resolution at Hyperscale: An Agentic AI Architecture for Network Operations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09122) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arun Malik
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.MA; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Agent Architecture, Fault Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 针对超大规模云网络中人工事件响应无法满足需求的问题，本文提出一种多智能体AI架构，通过分层智能体协作实现网络事件的自主检测、诊断和修复。在实际部署中，该系统对常见事件类别的自主解决率超过90%，并保证了安全性。

<h3 id="435">[435] An Enhanced Geometric-Spectral Feature Learning Framework for Airborne Multispectral Point Cloud Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09123) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xian Li, Yanfeng Gu, Aleksandra Pižurica
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Point Cloud, Remote Sensing, Hyperspectral Imaging
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HITlixian/TGRS_GSFF)
- **Summary**: 针对机载多光谱点云分类中高维异构光谱-空间特征和样本不平衡等问题，本文提出一个基于注意力的双流特征融合框架，并结合联合损失函数。在两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有技术。

<h3 id="436">[436] Unveiling Privacy Risks in Multi-modal Large Language Models: Task-specific Vulnerabilities and Mitigation Challenges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09125](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09125) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tiejin Chen, Pingzhi Li, Kaixiong Zhou, Tianlong Chen, Hua Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Privacy
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在图像和文本中存在的隐私风险尚未被充分研究。本文引入MM-Privacy数据集，定义了披露风险和保留风险，并系统评估了多种MLLM的隐私泄露情况，强调了缓解策略的紧迫性。

<h3 id="437">[437] From USD Scenes to Knowledge Graphs: Zero-Shot Ontology Grounding with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09134) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiangtao Shuai, Zongxiong Chen, Manfred Hauswirth, Sonja Schimmler
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph Construction, Zero-Shot Learning, Scene Understanding
- **Venue**: IEEE ICRA 2026 International Joint Workshop on Ontologies, Semantic Maps and Autonomous Robotics Standardization (J-WOSMARS 2026)
- **Summary**: 将3D场景中的物体与本体类别自动关联的关键瓶颈在于依赖人工词典。本文利用大语言模型实现零样本的本体接地，在USD厨房场景中达到90-96%的精确匹配准确率，显著优于词典和嵌入基线。

<h3 id="438">[438] Steganography Without Modification: Hidden Communication via LLM Seeds</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09135](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09135) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Felix Mächtle, Jonas Sander, Sebastian Berndt, Ben Weimar, Nils Loose, Thomas Eisenbarth
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Steganography, LLM Security, Steganalysis
- **Venue**: International Conference on Availability, Reliability and Security (ARES 2026)
- **Summary**: 本文发现LLM推理管道中存在一个无需修改模型权重的隐写通道，利用伪随机数生成器的种子依赖性进行隐蔽通信。在已知提示词设置下，300个token内即可实现32位种子的完美恢复，准确率高达100%。

<h3 id="439">[439] Decoding Pedestrian Crossing Intention from Egocentric Vision via Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09142) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Danya Li, Xiang Su, Yan Feng, Rico Krueger
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Vision-Language Model, Eye Tracking
- **Summary**: 利用自我中心视觉解码行人过街意图的研究尚不充分。本文将其形式化为视觉问答任务，采用视觉语言模型进行零样本和微调预测，并结合眼球运动和自车运动等线索。微调后的Qwen3-VL-2B模型准确率比Transformer基线提升14.5%。

<h3 id="440">[440] SEF-CLGC at SemEval-2026 Task 11: Logical Notation Impact on Language Model Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09157) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanna Abi Akl, Fabien Gandon, Catherine Faron, Pierre Monnin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Logical Reasoning, Symbolic Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: SemEval-2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM中内容与形式推理的分离问题，本文结合形式逻辑符号与小语言模型进行推理评估。实验表明，该方法在SemEval-2026任务上取得27.80%的内容分数，显著降低了推理中的内容偏差。

<h3 id="441">[441] Unified Energy for Invariant and Independent Decoding in Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09159) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen Yan, Minkai Xu, Zaiquan Yang, Yatao Bian
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Language Modeling, Decoding Strategy
- **Summary**: 扩散语言模型在并行生成时无法充分捕获token关系，导致与自回归模型存在性能差距。本文提出统一能量（Uni-E）框架，解决模型容量、依赖性和不变性三个关键因素，且无需采样估计。实验证明Uni-E能有效修正分布偏移。

<h3 id="442">[442] Crop Recommendation and Agricultural Query Answering System Using Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks and Hybrid Retrieval Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09160) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prajwal Thapa, Yagya Raj Pandeya
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, Graph Neural Network, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 为支持精准农业，本文集成天气预测、作物推荐和问答系统。利用时空图卷积网络（STGCN）预测30天天气（MSE约0.011），并结合土壤属性推荐作物，同时通过RAG聊天机器人提供自然语言问答。系统已部署为移动应用。

<h3 id="443">[443] CANS: Accelerating Multiuser Collaborative Edge Inference via Cooperative Autodidactic NeuroSurgeon</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09175](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09175) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheshun Wu, Ziyang Zhang, Changyao Lin, Zenglin Xu, Jie Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Federated Learning, DNN Deployment
- **Summary**: 多用户协作边缘推理中，各设备在时变条件下确定最优DNN分割点具有挑战性。本文提出CANS框架，通过FedLinUCB-DW算法利用离线经验加速在线探索。在原型系统上，平均推理延迟降低高达50%。

<h3 id="444">[444] Culturally-Adapted Red-Teaming Across East and Southeast Asian Contexts: A Methodological and Comparative Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyeji Choi, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Red Teaming, LLM Safety, Cultural Alignment
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on AIWILDS
- **Summary**: 多语言LLM安全评估常依赖直译，缺乏文化适应性。本文构建了韩日泰柬四种语言的直译和文化适应数据集，发现文化适应提示的攻击成功率平均高出9.3个百分点，而直译会系统性低估风险。

<h3 id="445">[445] Pretrained, Frozen, Still Leaking: Auditing Cross-Encoder Attribute Transfer in EEG Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianwei Tai
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Foundation Models, Privacy, Membership Inference
- **Summary**: 脑电图基础模型发布时通常只审计单一端点，导致跨编码器属性泄露。本文提出联合审计框架，通过跨编码器迁移检测发现光谱属性泄露，并证明标准防御措施（如LiRA和DP-SGD）对此失效。

<h3 id="446">[446] Resource-aware Computation-Communication Overlap for multi-GPU ML Workloads</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09200](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09200) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minyu Cui, Miquel Pericas
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distributed Training, GPU Computing, High Performance Computing
- **Venue**: AI on HPC Workshop at ISC 2026
- **Summary**: 多GPU训练中通信开销成为主要瓶颈。本文通过共享内存驱动的计算内核占用调整和通信内核优先级调度，实现计算与通信的重叠。在多种GPU上测试，总执行时间最多减少25.5%。

<h3 id="447">[447] Trustworthy Smart Fabs via Professional Proxies: Scaling Safe and Sustainable by Design (SSbD) through Industrial Data Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09227) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han-Teng Liao, Chang-Yi Kao, Karen Ang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CY; cs.HC; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Industrial AI, Zero Trust, Federated Learning
- **Venue**: 32nd IEEE ICE/ITMC Conference
- **Summary**: 半导体制造面临合规性与数据隐私的冲突。本文提出零信任社会治理框架，利用“专业代理”在硬件隔离信任区执行工作流，结合虚拟计量和联邦机器学习。通过工业数据空间导出加密合规令牌，无需暴露专有配方。

<h3 id="448">[448] End-to-End Training for Discrete Token LLM based TTS System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09234) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Changfeng Gao, Yong Ren, Jun Yuan, Ye Bai, Zhao You, ShiDong Shang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Flow Matching, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 现有离散token TTS系统采用级联流水线，各组件独立训练。本文提出端到端优化框架，统一训练语音分词器、LLM、流匹配模型和奖励模型。在Seed-TTS-Eval上达到词错误率0.78%的新SOTA，验证了整体端到端优化的有效性。

<h3 id="449">[449] Self-Paced Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Superbike Racing in Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09236) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luca Ghisi, Jacopo Essenziale, Carlo D&#39;Eramo, Matteo Luperto
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Curriculum Learning
- **Venue**: 1st Workshop on Generalization in Autonomous Driving: Paradigms, Practice, and Public Road Demonstrations
- **Summary**: 针对摩托车赛车的自主驾驶问题，现有深度强化学习方法主要针对四轮车辆，难以处理摩托车需要平衡和倾斜角度的复杂性。本文提出了结合SAC和自定进度课程深度强化学习（SPDL）的框架，通过动态生成逐渐复杂任务来训练智能体。实验表明，SPDL在多个赛道和车型上训练效率、圈速和稳定性均优于单独使用SAC。

<h3 id="450">[450] EgoTactile: Learning Grasp Pressure for Everyday Objects from Egocentric Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09243](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09243) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuan Zeng, Yujia Shi, Tiao Tan, Xingting Li, Yaqi Qin, Zongqing Lu, Wenming Yang, Jing-Hao Xue, Qingmin Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Grasping, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 从第一人称视频估计全手抓取压力对沉浸式VR和机器人操作至关重要，但现有方法局限于平面或指尖接触。本文提出了EgoTactile基准数据集，并设计了EgoPressureFormer判别基线和EgoPressureDiff条件扩散框架，利用世界知识先验和物理信息特征修正层推断合理接触模式。实验表明该方法在基准和野外场景中均表现出色。

<h3 id="451">[451] Proposal Refinement for Few-Shot Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09245](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09245) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuan Zeng, Bin Song, Jie Guo, Yuwen Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Object Detection
- **Summary**: 少样本目标检测中，区域建议在新类和基类之间的分布不均是个问题。本文提出了提议细化方法，在基训练阶段设计细化损失增强对新类的敏感性，在微调阶段引入细化分支生成更多新类提议。该方法在不增加推理时间的情况下，在基准上比基线方法提升约1%–6%，达到新的最优水平。

<h3 id="452">[452] BSTabDiff: Block-Subunit Diffusion Priors for High-Dimensional Tabular Data Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09257) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib, Md Younus Ahamed, Prashnna Gyawali, Gianfranco Doretto, Donald A. Adjeroh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Diffusion Model, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Venue**: 2nd DeLTa Workshop
- **Summary**: 高维低样本（HDLSS）表格数据（如组学）存在局部相关、稀疏依赖、厚尾分布等问题，直接密度学习不稳定。本文提出BSTabDiff块-子单元生成框架，将特征划分为潜在块并通过共享低维子单元生成，支持扩散和归一化流等现代深度先验。实验证明BSTabDiff在HDLSS数据上生成的合成数据比非结构化表格生成器更真实稳定。

<h3 id="453">[453] Physics-Guided Sequence-Based Generative Framework for Acoustic Metamaterial Inverse Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09266) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yijie Li, Jiahao Xu, Ching-Chih Tsao, Lili Qiu, Jingxian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Generative Model, Inverse Problem
- **Summary**: 超材料逆设计在宽带目标响应下因色散效应而困难，现有方法受限于模板或图像表示。本文提出MetaSeq序列生成框架，将声学超材料表示为结构化序列，结合监督预训练和物理求解器引导的强化学习微调。评估表明MetaSeq将响应误差降低了45%，优于五种基线。

<h3 id="454">[454] Internalizing Geometric Law: Learning from Solver Residuals for Precision-Critical Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09278) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rafael Cabral, Pang Zixi, Ziyi Shou, Shen Xin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Geometric Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Huawei-AI4Math/PyGeoX)
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在高精度领域（如几何合成）常出现幻觉，输出需满足严格几何约束。本文发布PyGeoX可编程几何DSL和包含300个问题的基准，并提出饱和加性奖励（SAR）解决异常梯度遮蔽问题。SAR使硬难度求解率提升2.3倍，8B模型可与更大系统竞争。

<h3 id="455">[455] Brain-Prompt Injection: A Route-Safety Audit for BCI-LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09315) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianwei Tai
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, LLM Security, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 脑机接口到智能体的管道易受大脑提示注入攻击，现有监控可能失效。本文定义了路径安全审计合约，包括日志模式、分母层次和端点规范，并应用分裂共形校准在EEG确认通道上。实验展示了在60名受试者上的错误接受率前沿，表明中介和确认可降低风险但非意图证书。

<h3 id="456">[456] A Universal Dense Football Event Representation Based on TabTransformer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09327) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weiran Yang, Daniel Memmert, Maximilian Klemp-Weins
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sports Analytics, Tabular Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 足球事件数据包含异质特征，现有方法使用独热或序数嵌入忽略动作语义。本文提出基于Transformer的模型学习类别特征间的潜在依赖，生成密集事件表示。实验表明嵌入表示在下游预测任务中相比任务特定基线具有更优的概率校准（Brier分数）。

<h3 id="457">[457] Conan-embedding-v3: Fusing Modality-Specific Models for Omni-Modal Embedding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09331) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiyu Li, Zhiyuan Hu, Yifan Wang, Peiming Li, Zheng Wei, Yang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Representation Learning, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 全模态检索面临不同模态在数据分布、架构和优化上的差异。本文提出Conan-embedding-v3的“解耦-融合-恢复”框架：先独立训练模态专家并融合任务向量，再通过投影恢复修正音频模态的漂移。该模型在马里兰多模态嵌入基准上取得74.9分，音频基准上取得55.61分。

<h3 id="458">[458] Beyond Humans: Multispecies Animal Face Recognition Using Transfer Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09353) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maria De Marsico, Anil K. Jain, Annalaura Miglino
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Animal Recognition, Transfer Learning, Face Recognition
- **Summary**: 动物面部识别因数据集不足以训练深度网络而受限。本文研究迁移学习，使用FaceNet（人脸）和ViT（ImageNet）在狗、灵长类和牛的数据集上实验。ViT在狗数据集上达到96.85%的验证准确率和84.34%的Rank-1识别率，优于现有专门训练的深度学习网络。

<h3 id="459">[459] PhysScene: A Scene Graph Dataset for Scientific Visual Reasoning in Physics Experiments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09368) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minghao Zou, Qingtian Zeng, Shangkun Liu, Yanda Meng, Guanghui Yue, Baoquan Zhao, Abdulmotaleb El Saddik, Wei Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scene Graph Generation, Visual Reasoning, Scientific Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZMH-SDUST/PhysScene)
- **Summary**: 现有场景图数据集缺乏科学实验领域的专用数据。本文介绍PhysScene，首个面向物理实验的场景图数据集，包含专业仪器和功能关系，支持超越空间共现的逻辑推理。实验表明PhysScene对现有场景解析算法提出了新挑战，有助于科学视觉推理的发展。

<h3 id="460">[460] Scaling Neural Network Verification with Tensor Parallelism and Fully Sharded Data Parallelism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sergei Vorobyov, Eugene Ilyushin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Parallel Computing, Distributed Computing
- **Summary**: 形式化神经网络验证受限于GPU内存，标准边界传播算法需要所有权重和松弛系数矩阵在单个加速器上。本文将张量并行（TP）和全分片数据并行（FSDP）适配至auto_LiRPA/α,β-CROWN验证框架。FSDP在宽MLP上减少80-90%基线内存，且边界与单GPU基线逐位一致。

<h3 id="461">[461] Reasoning Arena: Trace Tournaments When Verifiable Rewards Fall Short</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han Zhou, Adam X. Yang, Laurence Aitchison, Anna Korhonen, Albert Q. Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习在组级奖励相同时缺乏梯度信号。本文提出推理竞技场，将非多样化奖励组路由至评估系统，通过轨迹锦标赛比较推理质量，并利用Bradley-Terry模型拟合排名。在数学和编程基准上平均提升7.6%，训练加速27%-41%，节省近50%生成计算。

<h3 id="462">[462] Real-time body pose non-verbal communication with a consistency-based reliability measure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09390) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alina Marcu, Dragos Costea, Cristina Lazar, Marius Leordeanu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Human-Robot Interaction, Gesture Recognition, Pose Estimation
- **Summary**: 在救援等远程场景中，身体姿势可用于实时低成本的通信，但现有资源未隔离该信号。本文发布了包含十种通信意图的全身体态数据集，在嵌入式GPU上对比多种模型，并提出基于自洽性的无监督可靠性信号及理论证明。

<h3 id="463">[463] SAILS: Surrogate-based Analysis of Interactions via Local Effect Smooths</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09404) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Timo Heiß, Julia Herbinger, Bernd Bischl, Giuseppe Casalicchio
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Surrogate Model, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 现有交互解释方法仅检测和量化特征交互，未揭示其函数形式。本文提出SAILS框架，通过拟合局部效应的GAM代理模型来分析成对交互，实现交互检测、形式分类（线性、可分离乘积、不可分离乘积）和可视化。实验验证了其有效性，但受限于强特征相关和高阶交互。

<h3 id="464">[464] Can Data Work be Reparative?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09408) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Srravya Chandhiramowuli, Ding Wang, Alex Taylor
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Fairness, Participatory AI
- **Venue**: ACM FAccT
- **Summary**: 数据工作通常忽视最受伤害的人群。本文民族志研究了一个致力于在线安全数据集的民间技术项目，他们从女性主义视角协作构建数据集。研究揭示了在推动公正报酬和集体治理方面的挑战，并主张通过修复性视角重置责任关系。

<h3 id="465">[465] AI Assurance in UK Defence: Challenges in Operationalising JSP 936</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09414) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Callum Cockburn, Sam Farrow
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, AI Ethics, AI Assurance
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了英国国防部JSP 936指令在AI保障实施中的实际挑战，通过结构化解释性审查识别出八类主题挑战，包括证据充分性、人机交互、运行环境定义等。研究认为JSP 936提供了良好的治理基础，但实施依赖于解决技术、组织和保障方面的未决问题。最终指出当前保障方法学在性能、安全、伦理等方面的权衡存在局限性，需要进一步的方法、指南和组织能力。

<h3 id="466">[466] Harness Engineering for Physical AI: Robot Middleware Is the Harness Layer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09416) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sanghoon Lee, Jiyeong Chae, Kyung-Joon Park
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Robotics, Robot Middleware, Physical AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出在物理AI时代，机器人中间件应承担‘挽具’层的角色，负责在控制、计算和通信三个维度进行中介。论文定义了投影（Projection）、隔离（Isolation）和转移（Transfer）三项缺失的强制执行功能，并设计了ROS 2 Harness Profile作为部署方案。机器人中间件是整合这三项功能的最合适层次。

<h3 id="467">[467] Context-Aware Deep Learning for Defect Classification in Atomic-Resolution STEM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiadong Dan, Cheng Zhang, Leyi Loh, Ivan Verzhbitskiy, Yuan Chen, Goki Eda, Michel Bosman, N. Duane Loh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Deep Learning, Image Classification
- **Summary**: 针对原子级扫描透射电子显微镜中仅凭图像对比度进行缺陷分类的模糊性问题，该论文提出了一种上下文感知学习框架，融合图像对比度与成分、束能量等元数据。在构建的约5500万模拟补丁数据集上，框架在模拟数据上达到98%以上准确率，在实验数据上接近人类水平，后验熵降低94%。该方法将缺陷分类从不适定问题转变为适定物理问题。

<h3 id="468">[468] LargeMonitor: Monitoring Online Task-Free Continual Learning via Large Pretrained Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09430) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingqi Yuan, Xiaoquan Sun, Shihao Luo, Jiayu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Online Learning, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对在线无任务持续学习（TFCL）中现有方法对分布漂移结构起源无感知的问题，该论文提出LargeMonitor框架，利用大型预训练模型自动编排无任务持续适应。框架包含基于大视觉模型（LVM）的解耦检测模块和基于大多模态模型（LMM）的上下文感知诊断模块。实验表明LargeMonitor能精确检测和诊断复杂数据流，并持续提升现有在线TFCL算法的性能。

<h3 id="469">[469] A Finetuned SpeechLLM for Joint Multi-Granular L2 Assessment and Natural-Language Rationales</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09470) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Kamlesh Parikh, Cristian Tejedor-Garcia, Catia Cucchiarini, Helmer Strik
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Evaluation, LLM Alignment, Interpretability
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 针对自动二语语音评估缺乏可解释性的问题，该论文提出了一种基于rubric的SpeechLLM，用于多粒度评估并生成自然语言理由。模型采用监督微调和有界直接偏好优化的混合目标，同时预测句子级标签、词/音素级准确率并生成理由。在SpeechOcean762数据集上，该方法在句子级理由上保持合理性，但词/音素级的忠实度有所下降。

<h3 id="470">[470] Memory Beyond Recall: A Dual-Process Cognitive Memory System for Self-Evolving LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09483) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianxiang Fei, Mingyang Song, Mao Zheng, Xiang Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Cognitive Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对现有LLM智能体记忆系统无法处理隐式个性化推理的问题，该论文提出DCPM双过程认知记忆系统，按认知能力层级组织记忆，包括同步日间写入器（System1）和异步夜间引擎（System2）。在LongMemEval等基准上，System2在隐式跨会话推理任务上带来显著提升（最高+5.20），而表层召回提升较小。

<h3 id="471">[471] Targeting World Models to Compromise Robot Learning Pipelines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09499](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09499) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ethan Rathbun, Ahmed Agha, Saaduddin Mahmud, Christopher Amato, Alina Oprea, Eugene Bagdasarian
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: World Model, Data Poisoning, Robot Learning, Backdoor Attack
- **Summary**: 该论文展示了世界模型在机器人学习供应链中引入了一种隐秘而有效的数据投毒入口。攻击方法通过向遥操作数据集中注入恶意提示或转换动态，在通过世界模型输入时被激活，生成危险的合成训练轨迹。实验在动作条件和文本条件世界模型上均验证了后门攻击的有效性，包括对下游DRL策略的端到端攻击和VLA场景的概念验证。

<h3 id="472">[472] Closing the Prior-Posterior Loop: Self-Reflective Molecular Design with Analysis-Driven LLM Iteration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09520](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09520) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyi Gong, Zijie Qiu, Ben Zhong Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, RAG, Self-Reflection
- **Summary**: 针对当前LLM分子设计框架使用标量反馈循环（生成-评分-拒绝）等同于试错法的问题，该工作提出将第一性原理计算得到的完整理化性质（轨道能量、原子电荷、电子密度）反馈给LLM，并耦合RAG与自反思模块，使模型进行因果推理。在HOMO-LUMO能隙设计上误差低至0.0003 eV，中等任务成功率达100%，并泛化到偶极矩设计，在多个LLM骨干上鲁棒。

<h3 id="473">[473] Emergence of Context Characteristics Sensitivity in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09525) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nadya Yuki Wangsajaya, Haeun Yu, Isabelle Augenstein
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Instruction Tuning, LLM Evaluation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文测量了大型语言模型在指令微调（IFT）各阶段（SFT、DPO、RLVR）对上下文特征敏感度的变化。在四个模型和三个数据集上的实验显示，SFT使模型更倾向于使用易理解（高长度、高相似度、高流畅度）的上下文，而后续阶段可能强化或解决这些偏好。研究揭示上下文使用在IFT各阶段被主动重塑，设计平衡的IFT数据集对确保鲁棒上下文利用至关重要。

<h3 id="474">[474] Model Poisoning Against Federated Model Adaptation with Chain of Bit-Flips</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bastien Vuillod, Kevin Hector, Pierre-Alain Moellic, Jean-Max Dutertre, Olivier Potin
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Backdoor Attack, Hardware Security
- **Venue**: ACNS/AIHWS 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种针对联邦模型适配的新型后门攻击，基于硬件故障攻击（比特翻转）进行模型投毒。攻击通过离线阶段从预训练模型中构造后门，在联邦训练期间对单个本地模型注入硬件故障。在ResNet-18上，每个恶意客户端最多10次故障、总计19次即可达到94%的攻击成功率。论文还讨论了攻击的实用性和潜在防御。

<h3 id="475">[475] SecureClaw: Clawing Back Control of LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09549](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09549) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Ma, Stefan Schmid
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Privacy
- **Summary**: 针对工具使用型LLM智能体面临的外部动作未授权和内部明文暴露双重安全失效问题，该论文提出了SecureClaw双边界架构：在效果汇点设置授权，在读边界实施明文限制。敏感读操作通过可信网关替换为不透明句柄和受限摘要，写操作遵循PREVIEW→COMMIT协议。在多个基准上，SecureClaw是唯一同时保持可用任务效用且实现0%攻击成功率（ASB）的防御方案。

<h3 id="476">[476] FuseFSS: Efficient Secure LLM Inference with Function Secret Sharing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09551](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09551) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Ma, Yong Li, Stefan Schmid
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Privacy, Function Secret Sharing
- **Venue**: 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对两方安全推理中非线性算子和辅助操作效率低下的问题，该论文提出了FuseFSS编译器，将每个标量定点算子编译为批处理FSS评估。与最先进的基于FSS的GPU安全推理相比，FuseFSS在保持精度的同时实现1.24倍至1.50倍的端到端加速，在线通信减少9%至16%，预处理密钥生成时间和大小分别降低14%至23%和20%至24%。

<h3 id="477">[477] Safe-RULE: Safe Reinforcement UnLEarning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09559) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shixiong Jiang, Taozheng Zhu, Fanxin Kong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Safe RL, Data Poisoning, Offline RL, Reinforcement Unlearning
- **Summary**: 针对离线安全强化学习（Safe RL）容易受到数据投毒攻击的问题，该论文提出了一种新的学习范式Safe-RULE（安全强化反学习），作为防御框架消除中毒数据的影响，无需从头重新训练或访问原始训练环境。该方法在多个Safe RL基准任务上有效提升了安全性能。

<h3 id="478">[478] CT-VAM: A Cerebello-Thalamic-Inspired Vision-Action Model for Efficient Visuomotor Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09572) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiacheng Li, Yize Guo, Jiabin Guo, Qingchen Liu, Jiahu Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Robot Manipulation, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在高频底层执行中重复处理语言任务意图效率低下的问题，该论文提出了类小脑-丘脑的视觉-动作模型CT-VAM，作为轻量级局部执行策略从双视图视觉和本体感觉预测动作块。模型引入TARS条件注意力解码器，仅68M参数即在LIBERO上达到与更大VLA模型相当的成功率，同时降低推理延迟。结合流一致修复实现异步执行，支持高频控制。

<h3 id="479">[479] Seeing the Hivemind: A Consensus-Aware Interaction Technique for Mitigating AI Homogenization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09587) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Haris Khan, Joel wester
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Creative Writing, Text Generation, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 针对AI辅助创意写作导致的个体创造力和整体产出同质化问题，该论文提出了语义排斥技术（SRT）。计算评估显示SRT使语义多样性提高85-167%，共识短语减少43-95%。用户研究中，SRT输出获得更高的有用性和连贯性评分，68.8%的参与者愿意在多项任务中使用强SRT。原创性和连贯性评分正相关，表明发散不需要牺牲可读性。

<h3 id="480">[480] I Was Scrolling and Then I Saw a Pregnant Strawberry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09589](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09589) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Piera Riccio
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Content, Video Generation, AI Ethics, Gender Bias
- **Summary**: 该论文分析了社交媒体上新兴的AI迷你剧（水果剧）现象，指出这些视频尽管表面无害，却再现了深层的性别化叙事结构，女性角色被系统性地关联道德越轨、性背叛和生育能力。论文认为生成式AI的柔和、圆润视觉美学作为‘美学洗白’机制，中和了叙事的意识形态重量并规避内容审核。该研究基于女性主义电影理论、批判种族理论和平台研究。

<h3 id="481">[481] Closure-Validated Circuit Discovery in Attention Heads: Co-activation Proposes, Ablation Disposes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09607) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongzhong Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Attention Head Role
- **Summary**: 该论文指出基于共激活聚类的注意力头电路发现方法缺乏因果验证。作者采用稀疏自编码器聚类注意力头，并引入闭包测试（通过消融验证）。实验表明，在密集模型中聚类结果有效，但在混合专家模型中聚类信号无法通过闭包测试，说明廉价信号仅是电路提议而非确认电路。

<h3 id="482">[482] Shape Formation for the Cooperative Transportation of Arbitrary Objects Using Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09610) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Sayed, Wolfram Burgard, Tanja Katharina Kaiser
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent Coordination
- **Summary**: 论文针对任意形状和非均匀质量分布物体的协同运输问题，提出一种基于多智能体强化学习的队形控制方法。该方法使多机器人系统自主定位到物体下方以支撑重量，并在形成过程中避开障碍物。实验表明，该方法能产生平衡的队形，并泛化到复杂几何形状和杂乱场景。

<h3 id="483">[483] AGENTSERVESIM: A Hardware-aware Simulator for Multi-Turn LLM Agent Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rakibul Hasan Rajib, Mengxin Zheng, Qian Lou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Serving, Simulation
- **Summary**: 论文指出多轮LLM agent的端到端服务受到状态化程序执行的影响，现有模拟器无法捕获其核心动态。为此提出AGENTSERVESIM，一个硬件感知的模拟器，通过程序编排器、工具模拟器等模块评估服务策略。实验表明，在实际部署和硬件配置下，该模拟器在关键性能指标上的误差低于6%。

<h3 id="484">[484] Powering the Future of AI: Navigating the Trade-offs for Europe's Energy Transition and Net-Zero Goals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09617) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Hemmati, Gbemi Oluleye, Vassilis M. Charitopoulos
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.CY; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Energy Management, AI Sustainability, Environmental Planning
- **Summary**: 论文量化了AI扩张对欧洲能源转型的影响，通过空间显式优化模型分析21种AI增长情景。结果表明，AI可能带来73-723 TWh的额外电力需求，并在2030-2050年间累积67-181 MtCO2的排放超出。研究指出，AI基础设施选址将更多取决于稳定电力和系统灵活性而非清洁能源丰度。

<h3 id="485">[485] ReCoVLA: VLM-Guided Reward Compilation for Failure Recovery in Vision-Language-Action Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09630) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haodi Hu, Chung-Ta Huang, Jing Liu, Ye Wang, Kei Suzuki, Matthew Brand, Toshiaki Koike-Akino
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Sim-to-Real, Failure Detection
- **Summary**: 论文针对视觉-语言-动作策略在非正常状态下的脆弱性，提出ReCoVLA框架。该框架冻结预训练VLA策略，利用外部VLM推断故障模式和恢复阶段，并编译结构化奖励用于残差策略训练。在仿真中，该框架将平均成功率从36.7%提升至66.7%，物理零样本迁移实验中达到61.7%。

<h3 id="486">[486] ATN3D: Density-Aware LiDAR-Radar Early 3D Object Detection Under Extreme Sparsity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09634](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09634) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Debojyoti Biswas, Xianbiao Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Object Detection, LiDAR Perception, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 论文解决极端稀疏条件下的远距离3D目标检测问题，提出ATN3D框架。该框架引入密度感知早期融合、占用门控邻域聚合和证据条件通道自注意力机制。在VoD基准上，该方法在晴朗和浓雾条件下分别提升mAP 3.55%和8.41%，远距离目标检测也取得显著提升。

<h3 id="487">[487] FMplex: Model Virtualization for Serving Extensible Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09643](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09643) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hetvi Shastri, Pragya Sharma, Walid A. Hanafy, David Irwin, Mani Srivastava, Prashant Shenoy
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.OS
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, Foundation Model, Resource Management
- **Summary**: 论文指出现有模型服务系统为每个定制任务部署独立模型，造成内存浪费。提出FMplex，将基础模型作为虚拟化基板，通过虚拟基础模型抽象和批量感知公平调度器实现部署共享。实验表明，该方法相比空间分区降低80%延迟，相比最佳协同部署降低33.3%，集群规模下可承载6倍任务。

<h3 id="488">[488] Do Video Foundation Models Understand Intuitive Physics? A Layerwise Probing Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Samuele Punzo, Niccolò Caselli, Ippokratis Pantelidis, Francesco Massafra, Salvatore Lo Sardo, Mohammadreza Salehi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Representation Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 论文探究视频基础模型是否编码直观物理知识，以及该知识在不同家族、层和探测方式下的变化。通过冻结特征探测比较V-JEPA、VideoMAE和LTX-Video，发现V-JEPA表现最强，物理相关信息在中间到深层最易获取，且时间顺序至关重要。

<h3 id="489">[489] ArtiFact: A Large-Scale Multi-Modal Cultural Heritage Dataset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09648) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luciano Duarte, Olga Ovcharenko, Sebastian Schelter
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dataset, Multi-Modal Fusion, Data Quality Assessment, Cultural Heritage
- **Summary**: 论文指出多模态数据管理领域缺乏大型真实世界数据集。因此提出ArtiFact，一个包含651045条博物馆记录的多模态文化遗产数据集。通过跨模态错误检测和语义查询处理两个下游任务，发现现有系统在检测领域特定错误和处理文化邻近性查询上仍面临挑战。

<h3 id="490">[490] Muon Learns More Robust and Transferable Features than Adam</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09658) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyu Ruan, Fengzhuo Zhang, Shuche Wang, Shihua Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Robustness, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 论文研究Muon优化器在特征学习上的优势，通过鲁棒性和迁移性评估。实验表明，Muon学习到的特征在损坏图像和文本上比Adam和SGD更鲁棒，且在下游任务中迁移效果更好。理论分析证明Muon能获得更大的边距和有效秩。

<h3 id="491">[491] End-to-End Context Compression at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09659) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ang Li, Sean McLeish, Haozhe Chen, Nimit Kalra, Zaiqian Chen, Artem Gazizov, Venkata Anoop Suhas Kumar Morisetty, Bhavya Kailkhura, Harshitha Menon, Zhuang Liu, Brian R. Bartoldson, Tom Goldstein, Sanae Lotfi, Micah Goldblum, Pavel Izmailov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Long Context
- **Summary**: 论文针对长上下文推理中KV缓存的内存瓶颈，提出端到端上下文压缩方法。通过架构搜索和持续预训练，得到一族Latent Context Language Models (LCLMs)，在压缩比1:4、1:8、1:16下优化了性能、压缩速度和内存使用的帕累托前沿。LCLMs可作为长时智能体的高效骨干。

<h3 id="492">[492] Visual Prompting Meets Feature Reconstruction-Based Anomaly Detection with Dual-Teacher Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09670) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mateo Diaz-Bone, Daniel Caraballo, Florian Scheidegger, Thomas Frick, Mattia Rigotti, Andrea Bartezzaghi, Roy Assaf, Niccolo Avogaro, Yagmur G. Cinar, Brown Ebouky, Filip M. Janicki, Piotr S. Kluska, Cezary Skura, Cristiano Malossi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Data Augmentation, Visual Prompting
- **Summary**: 论文指出现有异常检测方法在对象尺度、视角等假设被违反时失效。提出包括视觉提示流水线、教师解冻机制和扩散生成数据增强的框架。在挑战性AeBAD数据集上，基于MMR骨干的方法比之前最先进方法提升3.5个百分点。

<h3 id="493">[493] Transition-Based Digital Twin Modelling for Alzheimer's Disease under Sparse Longitudinal Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09671) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinyu Huang, Yilin Zhang, Sofia Michopoulou, Christopher Kipps, Rahman Attar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Digital Twin, Clinical Prediction
- **Venue**: International Conference on AI in Healthcare (AIiH) 2026
- **Summary**: 论文针对阿尔茨海默病进展的异质性和稀疏纵向数据，提出个性化数字孪生框架。该框架结合基于转移和序列的建模策略，利用多模态数据预测认知状态和诊断类别。在稀疏数据下，基于相邻访问的转移建模比序列模型获得更高预测精度。

<h3 id="494">[494] MeCo: One-Step MeanFlow-based Corrector for Multi-Channel Speech Separation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09677](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09677) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dohwan Kim, Jung-Woo Choi
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Separation, Generative Model, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 论文针对判别式多通道语音分离模型在人类听感上的不足，提出基于MeanFlow的单步生成式校正器MeCo。通过数据空间优化结合x_r损失和端点SI-SDR损失，在最小计算开销下实现了最先进的信号保真度和听感质量。

<h3 id="495">[495] An 84-Format Numeric Catalog with Bit-Exact Conformance Vectors: A Vendor-Neutral Reference for FP8, BF16, MXFP4, and Microscaling Formats</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dmitrii Vasilev
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.MS; cs.PF; math.NA
- **Tags**: Quantization, Floating-Point Arithmetic
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gHashTag/t27), [code](https://github.com/gHashTag/paper3-methodology)
- **Summary**: 论文指出机器学习硬件中数值格式的激增缺乏供应商中立的位精确参考。因此编制了涵盖84种格式的目录，并提供了六种位精确一致性包。所有工件均以JSON格式提供，并经过交叉验证。

<h3 id="496">[496] Observability for Delegated Execution in Agentic AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09692) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhinav Mishra, Kumar Sharad
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Traceability
- **Summary**: 论文指出标准可观测性无法识别委托范围的执行，特别是在LLM agent系统中。提出了一种agent感知的可观测性基板，包括轻量级网关和通用信息模型，在运行时绑定委托上下文。这实现了跨工具的委托范围重建和直接取证查询。

<h3 id="497">[497] Learning to Attack and Defend: Adaptive Red Teaming of Language Models via GRPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09701](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09701) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Blake Bullwinkel, Eugenia Kim, Amanda Minnich, Mark Russinovich
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Red Teaming, Adversarial Training
- **Summary**: 本文针对红队测试中攻击者和防御者不断演化的问题，提出了AdvGRPO框架，利用密集多通道奖励和去中心化优势归一化，使GRPO可行于攻击者-防御者联合优化。实验表明，联合训练的防御者在安全基准上优于基线。

<h3 id="498">[498] Hybrid Robustness Verification for Spatio-Temporal Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09746) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sherwin Varghese, Matthew Wicker, Alessio Lomuscio
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Robustness Evaluation
- **Venue**: 9th International Symposium on AI Verification (SAIV 2026)
- **Summary**: 本文针对时空神经网络（如3D CNN）的鲁棒性验证问题，提出了时空边界传播（STBP）框架，对第一卷积层进行精确闭式表征，后续层采用可扩展近似。在动作识别、自动驾驶和医学图像基准上，STBP在相同扰动预算下实现了1.7倍更高的认证鲁棒准确率。

<h3 id="499">[499] Difference-Aware Retrieval Policies for Imitation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09758) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quinn Pfeifer, Ethan Pronovost, Paarth Shah, Khimya Khetarpal, Siddhartha Srinivasa, Abhishek Gupta
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Robot Manipulation
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对行为克隆中的累积误差导致泛化差的问题，提出了DARP，一种基于检索的半参数模仿学习方法，通过k近邻和相对距离向量重新参数化模仿学习。在连续控制和机器人操作任务上，DARP比标准行为克隆提高了15-46%。

<h3 id="500">[500] Preserving Plasticity in Continual Learning via Dynamical Isometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andries Rosseau, Robert Müller, Ann Nowé
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Dynamical Isometry
- **Summary**: 本文针对持续学习中神经网络可塑性丧失的问题，识别出动力学等距作为保持可塑性的关键机制，提出了等距正则化和AdamO优化器。在监督学习和强化学习的持续学习基准上，该方法匹配或超越了现有方法。

<h3 id="501">[501] Data Synthesis and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Low-Resource NMT: A Case Study on Q'eqchi' Mayan</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09767](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09767) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Data Synthesis, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Venue**: 29th International Conference on Text, Speech and Dialogue (TSD 2026)
- **Summary**: 本文针对低资源土著语言神经机器翻译的数据极度稀缺问题，提出了利用社区词典合成大规模语料的方法，结合LoRA微调mT5-base。域内评估BLEU达42.02，但有机评估仅0.59，表明合成引导需结合真实数据进行语义优化。

<h3 id="502">[502] Who Earns the Safety? Intervention-Aware Quantum Predictive Control with Safety Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09778) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Model Predictive Control, Safety Attribution
- **Summary**: 本文针对安全过滤器掩盖上游策略不安全的问题，提出了干预感知变分量子可微预测控制，并引入安全归因协议。在建筑控制仿真中，干预感知训练显著降低了策略的原始违规和过滤器依赖，且无能量回归。

<h3 id="503">[503] FASE: Fast Adaptive Semantic Entropy for Code Quality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09800](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09800) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shizhe Lin, Ladan Tahvildari
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Uncertainty Estimation, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文针对多智能体代码生成中不确定性度量依赖昂贵LLM的问题，提出了快速自适应语义熵（FASE），利用结构相似性图的最小生成树近似功能正确性。FASE在Spearman相关和ROCAUC上分别提升25%和19%，且运行成本仅为传统方法的0.3%。

<h3 id="504">[504] Bandits for Efficient Experimentation: Adapting to Control Group, Preferences, and Context Drifts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09802) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="505">[505] Topological Neural Operators</h3>

<h3 id="506">[506] AHA-WAM:Asynchronous Horizon-Adaptive World-Action Modeling with Observation-Guided Context Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09811) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="507">[507] PTL-Diffusion: Manifold-Aware Diffusion with Periodic Terminal Laws</h3>

<h3 id="508">[508] An Agency-Transferring Model-Free Policy Enhancement Technique</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09825) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="509">[509] OmniGameArena: A Unified UE5 Benchmark for VLM Game Agents with Improvement Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09826) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="512">[512] IDEQ -- Improving Diffusion Models for the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) by Leveraging the Structure of the Solution Space</h3>

<h3 id="513">[513] HA-VLN 2.0: An Open Benchmark and Leaderboard for Human-Aware Navigation in Discrete and Continuous Environments with Dynamic Multi-Human Interactions</h3>

- **Summary**: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）研究多忽略动态拥挤环境，缺乏社交感知约束。本文提出HA-VLN 2.0基准，引入明确社交感知约束，包含HAPS 2.0数据集、模拟器及真实机器人实验。实验表明显式社交建模可提升导航鲁棒性并减少碰撞，为安全、人性化导航研究奠定基础。

<h3 id="514">[514] Can Global XAI Methods Reveal Injected Behaviours in LLMs? SHAP vs Rule Extraction vs RuleSHAP</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.11189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11189) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Francesco Sovrano
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, LLM Safety
- **Venue**: KDD'2026
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型可能放大错误信息，但其背后的信念驱动启发式难以通过全局XAI方法恢复。本文提出RuleSHAP算法，结合全局SHAP与规则归纳，在检测非线性行为触发机制上相比RuleFit平均提升82% MRR@1。

<h3 id="515">[515] FieldWorkArena: Agentic AI Benchmark for Real Field Work Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.19662](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19662) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jun Takahashi, Atsunori Moteki, Akiyoshi Uchida, Shoichi Masui, Fan Yang, Kanji Uchino, Yueqi Song, Yonatan Bisk, Graham Neubig, Ikuo Kusajima, Yasuto Watanabe, Hiroyuki Ishida, Koki Nakagawa, Shan Jiang

- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Vision-Language Model

<h3 id="516">[516] Modeling the Diachronic Evolution of Legal Norms: An LRMoo-Based, Component-Level, Event-Centric Approach to Legal Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.07853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07853) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hudson de Martim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Legal AI, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 法律规范的时间演化表示缺乏细粒度版本化模式，阻碍可靠点时间重构。本文基于LRMoo本体提出结构化时间建模模式，将规范演化建模为版本化作品链，并以巴西宪法为例验证了任意历史日期的精确重构能力。

<h3 id="517">[517] Sound and Complete Neurosymbolic Reasoning with LLM-Grounded Interpretations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.09751](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09751) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bradley P. Allen, Prateek Chhikara, Thomas Macaulay Ferguson, Filip Ilievski, Paul Groth
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, LLM Reasoning, Formal Methods

- **Summary**: 大型语言模型输出缺乏逻辑一致性，难以直接用于形式推理。本文提出将LLM集成到超协调逻辑的语义解释函数中，实现神经符号推理，在事实性基准上提升宏F1约6个百分点，并在药物安全知识库中定位92个医学错误。

<h3 id="518">[518] Discovering heuristics in a complex SAT solver with large language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.22876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22876) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiwen Sun, Furong Ye, Zhihan Chen, Ke Wei, Shaowei Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: SAT Solving, Automated Heuristic Design
- **Summary**: 优化复杂SAT求解器因架构复杂而极具挑战。本文提出AutoModSAT框架，利用LLM自动生成启发式，结合无监督提示优化和进化搜索，在多个数据集上相比基线求解器提升40%性能，相比最优求解器提升30%。

<h3 id="519">[519] CLPO: Curriculum Learning meets Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.25004](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25004) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Curriculum Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 在线强化学习优化LLM推理时，静态问题集浪费预算。本文提出CLPO，一种自演化课程学习框架，根据模型能力动态调整问题难度和结构，无需额外人工标注。在数学推理和通用推理基准上，CLPO显著优于GRPO和DAPO。

<h3 id="520">[520] MixReasoning: Switching Modes to Think</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.06052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06052) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haiquan Lu, Gongfan Fang, Xinyin Ma, Qi Li, Xinchao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 推理模型对所有步骤采用相同长链推理会导致冗余。本文提出MixReasoning，动态调整推理深度，对困难步骤详细推理，简单步骤简洁推断。在GSM8K、MATH-500和AIME上，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著缩短推理长度并提高效率。

<h3 id="521">[521] MatSciBench: Benchmarking the Reasoning Ability of Large Language Models in Materials Science</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Material Discovery, Domain-Specific Evaluation
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型在材料科学推理方面的能力尚未充分研究。本文提出MatSciBench基准，包含1340个大学级材料科学问题，细粒度分类和三级难度划分。评估显示DeepSeek-R1在纯文本问题上达到75.22%准确率，GPT-5在图像问题上为53.02%，工具增强可提升非推理模型性能。

<h3 id="523">[523] TempoBench: Evaluating Temporal Causal Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>
