- **Tags**: Human-Robot Interaction, Preference Optimization, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MIT-CLEAR-Lab/QuickLAP)
- **Summary**: 机器人的物理纠正和语言反馈各自不完整。本文提出QuickLAP，一个贝叶斯框架，将语言和物理反馈融合以实时推断奖励函数。在自动驾驶模拟中，奖励学习误差降低超过70%；用户研究显示参与者认为该方法更易理解和协作。

<h3 id="525">[525] Knowing How to Edit: Reliable Evaluation Signals for Diagnosing and Optimizing Prompts at Query Level</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.19829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19829) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ke Chen, Yifeng Wang, Hassan Almosapeeh, Haohan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 提示评估与优化脱节限制了优化效果。本文提出评估指导的提示优化方法，集成多个提示质量指标并训练无执行评估器，准确率83.7%。在八个基准和三种骨干模型上，该方法持续超越现有优化基线。

<h3 id="526">[526] A Geometric Unification of Concept Learning with Concept Cones</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.07355](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07355) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexandre Rocchi, Thomas Fel, Gianni Franchi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder, Concept Learning
- **Summary**: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）和稀疏自编码器（SAE）分别代表有监督和无监督概念发现，但缺乏统一理解。本文证明二者均学习概念锥的几何结构，并提出包含框架来量化SAE与CBM的对齐。实验发现稀疏性和扩展因子的“甜蜜点”可最大化几何和语义对齐。

<h3 id="527">[527] A Geometric Theory of Cognition for Machine Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.12225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12225) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Laha Ale
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Representation Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 统一表示、记忆、适应和预测的智能体仍是挑战。本文提出黎曼梯度流框架，潜在流形上的几何自然地产生多时间尺度行为，无需显式记忆模块。在部分可观测RL环境中，该方法优于前馈基线，鲁棒性可与循环架构媲美。

<h3 id="528">[528] MAR:Multi-Agent Reflexion Improves Reasoning Abilities in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.20845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20845) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Onat Ozer, Yuchen Wang, Grace Wu, Daniel Dosti, Honghao Zhang, Vivi De La Rue
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Multi-Agent System, Self-Correction
- **Summary**: 单一LLM的自我反思可能导致思维退化。本文提出多智能体多角色辩论方法生成反思，提升多样性。在HotPot QA上达到47% EM，HumanEval上82.7%，均超越单LLM反思性能。

<h3 id="529">[529] ReTreVal: Reasoning Tree with Validation and Cross-Problem Memory for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.02880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02880) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Abhishek HS, Pavan C Shekar, Arpit Jain, Ashwanth Krishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Improvement, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 现有推理框架在问题边界丢弃所有失败上下文，限制了跨问题学习。提出ReTreVal，一种无需训练的自适应树探索框架，结合带验证的节点细化、类型化失败回溯和跨问题自重写记忆。在MATH-500上达到85.8% pass@1（比Zero-Shot CoT提升8.6个百分点），在MMLU-Pro上达到54.4%（比Self-Refine提升15.3个百分点），胜败比3.4:1。

<h3 id="530">[530] Thinking-Based Non-Thinking: Solving the Reward Hacking Problem in Training Hybrid Reasoning Models via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.04805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04805) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Siyuan Gan, Jiaheng Liu, Boyan Wang, Tianpei Yang, Runqing Miao, Yuyao Zhang, Fanyu Meng, Junlan Feng, Linjian Meng, Jing Huo, Yang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Reward Hacking
- **Summary**: 大型推理模型的长思维链导致计算开销大，用强化学习训练混合推理模型会出现奖励破解问题。提出TNT（Thinking-Based Non-Thinking），利用思维回答的组件信息为不同查询设置非思维回答的最大令牌数，无需监督微调。在五个数学基准上使令牌使用减少约50%，同时提高准确率，实现最优的精度-效率权衡。

<h3 id="531">[531] Dynamic Distributed Constraint Optimization and Metareasoning for Continual, Large-Scale Satellite Operations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06188) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Itai Zilberstein, Steve Chien
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Autonomous Systems, Resource Allocation
- **Summary**: 处理动态、大规模卫星星座观测调度问题，涉及数百万变量。提出DCOSP问题形式化，引入元推理框架控制资源消耗，以及D-NSS算法。在真实模拟中，D-NSS收敛到近优解，在解质量、计算时间和消息量上优于基线，元推理框架平衡了资源保存与效用。

<h3 id="532">[532] Payoff scaling shapes cooperation in LLM agents across languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19082](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19082) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Trung-Kiet Huynh, Dao-Sy Duy-Minh, Thanh-Bang Cao, Phong-Hao Le, Hong-Dan Nguyen, Phu-Quy Nguyen-Lam, Minh-Luan Nguyen-Vo, Hong-Phat Pham, Phu-Hoa Pham, Thien-Kim Than, Chi-Nguyen Tran, Huy Tran, Gia-Thoai Tran-Le, Alessio Buscemi, Le Hong Trang, Anh Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.GT; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Game Theory, Emergent Behavior
- **Summary**: 研究利益大小和语言如何影响LLM智能体在重复囚徒困境中的策略。训练分类器识别经典策略，并与演化博弈论基线比较。发现随着利益增大，LLM变得更具合作性，与演化预测相反，且在多个开源模型中一致。

<h3 id="533">[533] Language-based Trial and Error Falls Behind in the Era of Experience</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21754) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoyu Wang, Guozheng Ma, Shugang Cui, Yilun Kong, Haotian Luo, Li Shen, Mengya Gao, Yichao Wu, Xiaogang Wang, Dacheng Tao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Exploration Strategy
- **Summary**: LLM在未见过的非语言环境中因探索成本过高而表现受限。提出SCOUT，用轻量级“侦察兵”探索环境，通过监督微调和多轮强化学习引导LLM。在非语言任务中使Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct平均分达0.86，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro（0.60），节省约60% GPU时间。

<h3 id="534">[534] TAME: A Trustworthy Test-Time Evolution of Agent Memory with Systematic Benchmarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03224) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu Cheng, Yongkang Hu, Jiuan Zhou, Yushuo Zhang, Yihang Chen, Huichi Zhou, Mingang Chen, Zhizhong Zhang, Kun Shao, Yuan Xie, Zhaoxia Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 良性任务演化中智能体记忆可能差错，导致可信度下降。提出TAME，一种可信感知的记忆演化框架，由执行器和评估器协同管理共享记忆库。在GPT-5.2 AIME基准上准确率提升14.6个百分点，同时保持可信度。

<h3 id="535">[535] Weak-Driven Learning: How Weak Agents make Strong Agents Stronger</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08222) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zehao Chen, Gongxun Li, Tianxiang Ai, Zixuan Huang, Xiaodong Liu, Yifei Li, Wang Zhou, Fuzhen Zhuang, Xianglong Liu, Jianxin Li, Deqing Wang, Yikun Ban
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Distillation, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 后训练优化中模型高度自信后收益递减。提出WMSS，利用弱检查点通过熵动态识别可恢复的学习差距并进行补偿学习。在数学推理和代码生成上实现有效改进，且无额外推理成本。

<h3 id="536">[536] Web Agents Should Use Typed Actions Instead of Click-Based Browsing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17245](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17245) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Linxi Jiang, Rui Xi, Zhijie Liu, Shuo Chen, Zhiqiang Lin, Suman Nath
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Web Agent, GUI Automation, Agent-Tool Interface
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Position Paper Track
- **Summary**: 基于点击的低级交互原语导致Web智能体行为脆弱、成本高且难以审计。提出web verbs，作为带结构化输入输出、前置/后置条件和策略标签的类型化函数。通过案例展示动词级组合能产生正确、可重复的结果。

<h3 id="537">[537] NoRD: A Data-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model that Drives without Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21172) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ishaan Rawal, Shubh Gupta, Yihan Hu, Wei Zhan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, End-to-End Driving, Vision-Language-Action
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Applied-Open-Source/nord)
- **Summary**: 当前VLA模型需要大量数据和推理标注。提出NORD，在\<60%数据且无推理标注下微调，采用Dr. GRPO缓解难度偏差。在Waymo和NAVSIM上取得竞争性能，令牌量减少3倍。

<h3 id="538">[538] 2-Step Agent: A Framework for the Interaction of a Decision Maker with AI Decision Support</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21889) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Otto Nyberg, Fausto Carcassi, Davide Tugnoli, Giovanni Cinà
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, Causal Inference, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 缺乏对决策者如何从ML决策支持中学习的理解。提出2-Step Agent框架，建模贝叶斯智能体从ML预测更新信念并影响因果估计和决策。识别出ML-DS有益的条件，并发现单个错配先验信念就能使ML-DS造成更差结果。

<h3 id="539">[539] An Alternative Trajectory for Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14147) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Margarita Belova, Yuval Kansal, Yihao Liang, Jiaxin Xiao, Niraj K. Jha
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Sustainability, Small Language Model, Symbolic Reasoning
- **Summary**: 生成式AI面临可持续性危机，推理能耗高且收益递减。提出基于领域专用超智能（DSS）的替代路径，构建显式符号抽象（知识图谱、本体、形式逻辑）用于合成课程，使小语言模型掌握领域推理。设想“DSS模型社会”通过编排智能体路由任务。

<h3 id="540">[540] IRAM-Omega-Q: A Computational Framework for Uncertainty Regulation in Adaptive Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.16020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16020) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Veronique Ziegler
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Control Theory, Agent Architecture
- **Summary**: 自适应智能体需在噪声和扰动下维持内部状态。提出IRAM-Omega-Q，结合量子态表示和熵信号闭环自适应控制。比较了两种因果控制顺序，发现顺序影响调节需求和阈值位置。

<h3 id="541">[541] Reflection in the Dark: Exposing and Escaping the Black Box in Reflective Prompt Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18388) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shiyan Liu, Qifeng Xia, Qiyun Xia, Yisheng Liu, Xinyu Yu, Rui Qu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, Multi-Agent System, Prompt Optimization
- **Venue**: ACL SRW 2026
- **Summary**: 反射式提示优化方法如GEPA存在黑箱优化、不可解释轨迹和性能退化问题。提出VISTA，多智能体框架将假设生成与提示改写解耦，结合随机重启和epsilon-贪心采样。在缺陷种子下将准确率从13.50%恢复至87.57%。

<h3 id="542">[542] Counterfactual Credit Policy Optimization for Multi-Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.21563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21563) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhongyi Li, Wan Tian, Yikun Ban, Jinju Chen, Huiming Zhang, Yang Liu, Fuzhen Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, Counterfactual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 多智能体LLM中共享奖励掩盖个体贡献，导致信用分配问题。提出CCPO，一种优化器无关的信用分配层，通过反事实信用和验证器锚定的自评估分配器提供代理特定学习信号。在MATH500和多个OOD设置上提升双智能体推理性能。

<h3 id="543">[543] Signals Are Not States: Neuro-Symbolic Safeguards for Culturally Aware Classroom AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22793](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22793) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sina Bagheri Nezhad
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuro-Symbolic AI, Bias Mitigation, Education Technology
- **Venue**: Workshop on Stereotypes Across Cultures in Language Technologies @ ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 课堂AI系统可能将文化差异行为误解为刻板状态（如沉默=不投入）。提出NSCR，文化感知神经符号框架，将多模态信号转化为带不确定性、来源和文化范围的类型化事实，通过可执行推理和政策约束构图。定义了刻板印象推理的分类法和基准议程。

<h3 id="544">[544] MC-CPO: Mastery-Conditioned Constrained Policy Optimization for Pedagogically Safe Intelligent Tutoring Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.04251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04251) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Oluseyi Olukola, Nick Rahimi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Education Technology, Safe RL
- **Summary**: 智能导学系统中优化参与信号可能导致学习与实际知识获取脱节，观察到26.5%和3.1%的差距。提出MC-CPO，将可行动作空间条件限制在掌握状态上，并通过构造强制执行教学安全约束。在Junyi Academy和XES3G5M上平均每回合掌握增益分别提高18.3%和54.0%，同时维持竞争性参与表现。

<h3 id="545">[545] EvoMaster: A Foundational Evolving Agent Framework for Agentic Science at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17406) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Zhu, Yuzhu Cai, Zexi Liu, Cheng Wang, Fengyang Li, Wenkai Jin, Wanxu Liu, Zehao Bing, Bingyang Zheng, Jingyi Chai, Shuo Tang, Rui Ye, Yuwen Du, Xianghe Pang, Yaxin Du, Tingjia Miao, Yuzhi Zhang, Ruoxue Liao, Zhaohan Ding, Linfeng Zhang, Yanfeng Wang, Weinan E, Siheng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Research Agent, Self-Improvement
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sjtu-sai-agents/EvoMaster)
- **Summary**: 问题:现有智能体框架静态且缺乏自我进化能力，难以支撑大规模科学发现。方法:提出EvoMaster，一种基础演化智能体框架，支持假设迭代精炼、自我批评和知识积累，仅需约100行代码即可构建领域无关的自演化科学智能体。结果:在四个权威基准上取得SOTA成绩（Humanity's Last Exam 41.1%，MLE-Bench Lite 75.8%，BrowseComp 73.3%，FrontierScience 53.3%），相对OpenClaw提升159%至316%。

<h3 id="546">[546] The Topological Dual of a Dataset: A Logic-to-Topology Encoding for AlphaGeometry-Style Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18050](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18050) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anthony Bordg
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Geometric Reasoning, Reasoning Structure
- **Summary**: 问题:AlphaGeometry的符号推理引擎存在对数线性扩展瓶颈，且当前表示方法表面化。方法:提出基于观测逻辑的逻辑到拓扑编码，利用可证明性与拓扑之间的对偶性，引入数据集拓扑对偶概念，连接形式逻辑、拓扑和神经处理。结果:概念性论文，无定量结果。

<h3 id="547">[547] Explainable AML Triage with LLMs: Evidence Retrieval and Counterfactual Checks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.19755](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19755) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dorothy Torres, Wei Cheng, Ke Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Counterfactual Reasoning, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 问题:LLM在监管严格的反洗钱工作流中存在幻觉和缺乏来源等风险。方法:提出可解释反洗钱分流框架，结合检索增强证据捆绑、结构化输出合同和反事实检查。结果:取得最佳分流性能（PR-AUC 0.75，升级F1 0.62），引文有效性0.98，证据支持0.88，反事实忠实度0.76，有效减少幻觉。

<h3 id="548">[548] Deconstructing Superintelligence: Identity, Self-Modification and Différance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.19845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19845) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Elija Perrier
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AGI Architecture, Self-Modification
- **Venue**: AGI-2026
- **Summary**: 问题:自我修改被视为超级智能的构成特性，但存在悖论。方法:在算子代数上形式化自我修改，识别补充为交换子，证明传播定理，关联说谎者悖论和德里达的延异。结果:理论证明了强自我修改可能破坏系统持久的同一性。

<h3 id="549">[549] Agentic World Modeling: Foundations, Capabilities, Laws, and Beyond</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.22748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22748) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Meng Chu, Xuan Billy Zhang, Kevin Qinghong Lin, Lingdong Kong, Jize Zhang, Teng Tu, Weijian Ma, Ziqi Huang, Senqiao Yang, Wei Huang, Yeying Jin, Zhefan Rao, Jinhui Ye, Xinyu Lin, Xichen Zhang, Qisheng Hu, Shuai Yang, Leyang Shen, Wei Chow, Yifei Dong, Fengyi Wu, Quanyu Long, Bin Xia, Shaozuo Yu, Mingkang Zhu, Wenhu Zhang, Jiehui Huang, Haokun Gui, Runyi Li, Shiyi Du, Xu Huang, Dong Huang, Rui Liu, Chenyu Tang, Xuhang Chen, Chengzu Li, Haoxuan Che, Long Chen, Qifeng Chen, Wenxuan Zhang, Wenya Wang, Xiaojuan Qi, Yang Deng, Yanwei Li, Mike Zheng Shou, Zhi-Qi Cheng, See-Kiong Ng, Ziwei Liu, Philip Torr, Jiaya Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, LLM Agent, Simulation
- **Summary**: 问题:世界模型在不同社区含义不同，缺乏统一分类。方法:提出"层次×法则"分类法，定义三个能力层次（预测器、模拟器、演化器）和四个法则领域（物理、数字、社会、科学），综述超过400项工作。结果:提供了路线图、评估原则和可复现评估包。

<h3 id="550">[550] Correct Is Not Enough: Training Reasoning Planners with Executor-Grounded Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03862) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianyang Han, Hengyu Shi, Junjie Hu, Xu Yang, Zhiling Wang, Junhao Su
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reward Model, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MasaiahHan/TraceLift)
- **Summary**: 问题:仅以最终答案正确性作为奖励信号会强化不忠实的推理链，导致捷径和错误中间状态。方法:提出TraceLift，一种规划器-执行器训练框架，使用执行器接地奖励（推理奖励模型得分乘以执行器性能提升），并构建TRACELIFT-GROUPS数据集。结果:在代码和数学基准上，执行器接地理性奖励优于仅执行训练。

<h3 id="551">[551] Executable World Models for ARC-AGI-3 in the Era of Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05138) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sergey Rodionov
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, World Model, Code Execution
- **Venue**: AGI-2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/astroseger/arc-3-agents-baseline1)
- **Summary**: 问题:ARC-AGI-3基准需要强抽象和推理能力，编码智能体需要世界模型。方法:构建编码智能体系统，维护可执行的Python世界模型，通过验证、重构和规划来操作，无手工编码的游戏逻辑。结果:GPT-5.5高推理努力下完全解决15/25个游戏，平均RHAE 58.12%；GPT-5.4下完全解决8个游戏，平均RHAE 41.29%。

<h3 id="552">[552] Engagement Process: Rethinking the Temporal Interface of Action and Observation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11484](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11484) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jialian Li, Yuchen Cao, Junhong Liu, Weiran Guo, Xutao Wang, Jiaming Song, Jiahao Zhang, Jie Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: POMDP, Temporal Reasoning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 问题:任务完成涉及复杂时间交互，固定决策步长的接口无法捕捉。方法:提出参与过程（EP），将动作和观测解耦为时间上的事件流，泛化POMDP。结果:在玩具、LLM智能体和学习实验中，EP揭示了基于步长接口掩盖的时间行为。

<h3 id="553">[553] Goal-Oriented Reasoning for RAG-based Memory in Conversational Agentic LLM Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12213](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12213) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiazhou Liang, Armin Toroghi, Yifan Simon Liu, Faeze Moradi Kalarde, Liam Gallagher, Scott Sanner
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Reasoning, Multi-Hop Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题:基于RAG的记忆检索常返回不相关证据，缺乏对缺失事实的推理。方法:提出Goal-Mem，一种面向目标推理框架，从用户话语作为目标进行反向链式推理，分解为原子子目标并定向检索，用自然语言逻辑形式化。结果:在多跳推理和隐式推理任务上持续优于九个强基线。

<h3 id="554">[554] ASH: Agents that Self-Hone via Embodied Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14211) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Benjamin Schneider, Xavier Schneider, Victor Zhong, Sun Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Self-Improvement, Long-Horizon Planning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Scalable Learning and Optimization for Efficient Multimodal AI Agents
- **Summary**: 问题:长周期具身任务依赖手工奖励或演示，不可扩展。方法:提出ASH，一种通过逆向动力学模型从无标注互联网视频学习具身策略的自我改进系统，无监督识别关键时刻并保留为长期记忆。结果:在《宝可梦绿宝石》和《塞尔达传说缩小帽》中，ASH平均达到11.2/12和9.9/12里程碑，最佳基线仅6.5和6.0。

<h3 id="555">[555] ANNEAL: Adapting LLM Agents via Governed Symbolic Patch Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.16309](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16309) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Safayat Bin Hakim, Keyan Guo, Wenkai Tan, Alvaro Velasquez, Shouhuai Xu, Houbing Herbert Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Knowledge Graph, Neuro-Symbolic AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sbhakim/anneal-agents)
- **Summary**: 问题:LLM智能体常在同一故障上重复失败，因为底层过程知识未修复。方法:提出ANNEAL，一种神经符号智能体，将重复失败转化为受控的符号编辑到过程知识图谱中，核心机制FDKA包括定位、综合补丁、验证和回滚。结果:唯一进行持久结构修复的系统，在重复故障场景中将坚持失败率从72-100%降至0%。

<h3 id="556">[556] CatalyticMLLM: A Graph-Text Multimodal Large Language Model for Catalytic Materials</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17254) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanjie Li, Jian Xu, Xu-Yao Zhang, Shiming Xiang, Nian Ran, Weijun Li, Cheng-Lin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, Graph Neural Network, Material Discovery
- **Summary**: 问题:催化材料的性质预测和逆设计通常解耦，导致数据分布偏移和评估偏差。方法:提出CatalyticMLLM，一个统一的图-文本多模态大模型，在相同模型和表示空间中整合性质预测和逆设计，形成闭环优化。结果:在催化弛豫能预测和逆设计任务上优于解耦基线。

<h3 id="557">[557] When Tabular Foundation Models Meet Strategic Tabular Data: A Prior Alignment Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19662](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19662) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Lv, Yunxin Mao, Renzhe Xu, Chunyuan Zheng, Yikai Chen, Haoxuan Li, Jinxuan Yang, Kun Kuang, Yuanlong Chen, Mingyang Geng, Wanrong Huang, Shixuan Liu, Shaowu Yang, Wenjing Yang, Zhouchen Lin, Haotian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Strategic Classification, Foundation Model
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Summary**: 问题:表格基础模型假设非策略性数据，而现实中被操作后产生分布偏移。方法:提出策略先验数据拟合网络（SPN），一种推理时策略感知框架，通过构建策略性上下文示例适应后操作分布，无需重新训练。结果:在真实和合成数据集上，SPN在策略性操作下持续提升鲁棒性和预测性能。

<h3 id="558">[558] Beyond Rational Illusion: Behaviorally Realistic Strategic Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19674) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Lv, Yunxin Mao, Renzhe Xu, Chunyuan Zheng, Yikai Chen, Haoxuan Li, Yang Shi, Jinxuan Yang, Zhouchen Lin, Yuanlong Chen, Yuanxing Zhang, Shaowu Yang, Wenjing Yang, Haotian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Strategic Classification, Behavioral Modeling
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Summary**: 问题:现有策略性分类假设智能体完全理性，忽略认知偏差。方法:提出前景引导策略框架（Pro-SF），基于前景理论建模行为真实的策略性响应（包含收益/成本不对称、主观参考点、概率扭曲）。结果:在合成和真实数据集上优于基准，实现行为学基础化。

<h3 id="559">[559] Playing Devil's Advocate: Off-the-Shelf Persona Vectors Rival Targeted Steering for Sycophancy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.21006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21006) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ishaan Kelkar, Nebras Alam, Vikram Kakaria, Madhur Panwar, Vasu Sharma, Maheep Chaudhary
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, Persona, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 问题:LLM的谄媚问题（同意错误用户），标准缓解方法CAA需要标注数据。方法:评估现成的角色扮演人格向量对减轻谄媚的效果。结果:引导怀疑/审视人格可将谄媚减少至CAA效果的68%和98%，且在用户正确时保持准确率；人格向量与谄媚方向在激活空间中独立。

<h3 id="560">[560] MBABench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomson Yen, Julian Poeltl, Harshith Srinivas Gear, Yilin Meng, Joshua Fan, Adam Shen, Yili Liu, Ali Bauyrzhan, Siri Du, Haoyang Liu, Daniel Guetta, Hongseok Namkoong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 问题:现有电子表格基准聚焦于问答或单公式编辑，无法评估端到端工作流。方法:提出MBABench，专注于金融领域端到端电子表格任务的基准，包含准确性、公式、格式三维评价体系。结果:Claude系列领先，但即使最强智能体也常未达专业标准，且随难度增加性能显著下降。

<h3 id="561">[561] Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763) (replaced)
- **Authors**: George Tsoukalas, Anton Kovsharov, Sergey Shirobokov, Anja Surina, Moritz Firsching, Gergely Bérczi, Francisco J. R. Ruiz, Arun Suggala, Adam Zsolt Wagner, Eric Wieser, Lei Yu, Aja Huang, Miklós Z. Horváth, Andrew Ferraiuolo, Henryk Michalewski, Edward Lockhart, Codrut Grosu, Thomas Hubert, Matej Balog, Pushmeet Kohli, Swarat Chaudhuri
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Automated Theorem Proving
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在数学推理中不可靠，该论文提出使用LLM生成Lean形式化证明的方法来解决开放数学问题。其最强大的代理以几百美元的成本自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并已部署在多个数学领域。结果表明AI辅助形式化证明搜索的强大能力，并揭示了实现这一能力的代理设计。

<h3 id="562">[562] LGMT: Logic-Grounded Metamorphic Testing for Evaluating the Reasoning Reliability of LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23965](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23965) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zenghui Zhou, Man Li, Xiaoke Fang, Xinyi Zhou, Weibin Lin, Zheng Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Metamorphic Testing
- **Summary**: 现有LLM推理可靠性评估依赖静态基准，无法测试逻辑等价变换下的鲁棒性。本文提出LGMT框架，基于一阶逻辑推导不变关系生成测试用例，通过跨案例一致性检查检测缺陷。在六个模型上实验表明，LGMT暴露了大量传统评估忽略的缺陷，且模型对符号和结论层面变化敏感。

<h3 id="563">[563] CUA-Gym: Scaling Verifiable Training Environments and Tasks for Computer-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25624](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25624) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bowen Wang, Dunjie Lu, Junli Wang, Tianyi Bai, Shixuan Liu, Zhipeng Zhang, Haiquan Wang, Hao Hu, Tianbao Xie, Shuai Bai, Dayiheng Liu, Que Shen, Junyang Lin, Tao Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Computer-Use Agent, RLVR, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 计算机使用代理的强化学习因缺乏可验证奖励的可扩展训练数据而受阻。本文提出CUA-Gym流水线，协同生成任务指令、环境状态和奖励函数，并构建了32,112个验证训练元组的数据集。训练后的模型在OSWorld-Verified上达到62.1%和72.6%的准确率，优于开源CUA，且性能随数据量和环境多样性平滑提升。

<h3 id="564">[564] Neural Scalable Symbolic Search Framework for Complex Logical Queries with Multiple Free Variables</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25985) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weizhi Fei, Hang Yin, Zihao Wang, Shukai Zhao, Wei Zhang, Yangqiu Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Complex Query Answering, Knowledge Graph
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/NS3_KDD2026)
- **Summary**: 知识图谱上的复杂查询回答面临多自由变量联合排序的难题。本文提出NS3框架，通过预算约束的神经符号搜索近似联合排序，逐步将多变量查询简化为单变量查询。在三个数据集上显著提升了联合排序性能，并发布了k=3的基准。

<h3 id="565">[565] Quantifying and Optimizing Simplicity via Polynomial Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29823) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianren Zhang, Xiangxin Li, Minghao Xiao, Guanyu Chen, Feng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Simplicity Bias, Regularization
- **Summary**: 深度学习缺乏通用的简化性量化指标，本文提出基于正交多项式基的函数表示，以有效度数作为简化性度量，能预测泛化性能且优于现有的尖锐度等代理。进一步引入可微简化正则化器，在图像分类、文本分类、对比学习微调和强化学习中持续提升泛化。

<h3 id="566">[566] VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00384) (replaced)
- **Authors**: William Rudman, Abhishek Divekar, Kanishk Jain, Sebastian Joseph, Stella S. R. Offner, Matthew Lease, Kyle Mahowald, Greg Durrett, Junyi Jessy Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.LG; stat.CO
- **Tags**: Automated Research, Tool Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 科学工作流中拟合定量模型的自动化程度低，本文提出VESTA框架，赋予视觉语言模型动态扩展的工具集，通过数据变换、可视化和统计检验指导模型细化。在DAWN基准上，VESTA的动态工具创建超越了现有流水线，尤其在复杂和领域特定任务上提升显著。

<h3 id="567">[567] ReSkill: Reconciling Skill Creation with Policy Optimization in Agentic RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01619) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zelin He, Haotian Lin, Boran Han, Wei Zhu, Haoyang Fang, Bernie Wang, Xuan Zhu, Runze Li, Matthew Reimherr
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Skill Learning, Thompson Sampling, Agentic RL
- **Summary**: 现有技能增强强化学习将技能创建与策略优化分离，导致冲突。本文提出ReSkill框架，将技能创建嵌入策略学习，利用GRPO的组结构实现断言驱动的技能创建、组内采样和自适应Thompson采样。在多个领域超越现有方法，并展示了技能与策略的自适应共演化。

<h3 id="568">[568] WorldCoder-Bench: Benchmarking Physically Grounded 3D World Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01869) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuo Lu, Yinuo Xu, Kecheng Yu, Siru Jiang, Yongcan Yu, Yubin Wang, Haitao Yang, Yuxiang Zhang, Bin Wang, Ran He, Jian Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D World Synthesis, Benchmark, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: LLM被要求从自然语言构建可执行的交互式3D世界，但现有评估仅关注像素或DOM。本文提出WorldCoder-Bench基准，含2026个任务和StateProbe执行协议，验证隐藏行为合约。九个前沿模型中最佳系统仅达27.8%验证覆盖率，失败主要由状态模式漂移和交互链中断引起。

<h3 id="569">[569] ChatHealthAI: Aligning Electronic Health Record Representations with Large Language Models for Grounded Clinical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02802) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bo-Hong Wang, Baicheng Peng, Ruilin Wang, Jun Bai, Ziyang Song, Yue Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clinical NLP, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: LLM在临床决策支持中难以有效建模结构化EHR，本文提出ChatHealthAI框架，通过任务感知重采样器将EHR基础模型表示与冻结LLM语义空间对齐。在EHRSHOT基准的三个任务上，ChatHealthAI改善了推理质量和可解释性，同时保持竞争性预测性能。

<h3 id="570">[570] The Shadow Price of Reasoning: Economic Perspective on Optimal Budget Allocation for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03092) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xu Wan, Speed Zhu, Jianwei Cai, Guang Chen, XiMing Huang, Wiggin Zhou, Mingyang Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Resource Allocation, Cost Optimization
- **Summary**: 推理时扩展提升LLM性能但受计算预算约束，本文将推理预算分配建模为全局优化问题，基于移位激增函数推导出最优分配策略CLEAR，实现资源从低效查询向高效查询的再分配。在资源稀缺场景下，CLEAR相比均匀分配将全局准确率提升高达3倍。

<h3 id="571">[571] Trivium: Temporal Regret as a First-Class Objective for Causal-Memory Controllers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04421) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Edward Y. Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Reasoning, Regret Minimization, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 当前智能体系统通过结果奖励纠正错误却忽略错误时机和原因，本文提出长时域时间遗憾作为一等目标，与结果遗憾和认知遗憾共同构成完整归因。在CausalBench-Seq上，Trivium遵循对数包络线，而基线线性增长，验证了理论有效性。

<h3 id="572">[572] MIRAGE: Mobile Agents with Implicit Reasoning and Generative World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04627) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhichao Yang, Yuanze Hu, Haojie Hao, Longkun Hao, Dongshuo Huang, Hongyu Lin, Gen Li, Lanqing Hong, Yihang Lou, Yan Bai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mobile AI, GUI Automation, World Model
- **Summary**: 移动代理使用长文本推理链导致交互缓慢，本文提出MIRAGE框架，学习连续潜在推理表示将显式推理转为隐式状态，并整合生成世界模型使潜在向量与未来屏幕对齐。在AndroidWorld上匹配显式推理但解码令牌减少3-5倍，在AndroidControl上提升动作基础。

<h3 id="573">[573] Entropy-Based Evaluation of AI Agents: A Lightweight Framework for Measuring Behavioral Patterns</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05872) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Olasimbo Ayodeji Arigbabu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Agent Evaluation, Information Theory, Behavioral Modeling
- **Summary**: 现有AI代理评估忽略探索、重复性等行为模式，本文提出基于熵的评估框架EEA，引入动作熵、轨迹熵等指标，并提供Python实现。该框架旨在补充传统任务成功率等指标，可集成于LangChain等框架。

<h3 id="574">[574] A Pre-Registered Causal Partition of Self-Consistency Elicitation and Reward Design in RLVR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05932) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuze Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RLVR, Self-Consistency, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: RLVR中朴素估计混淆了自一致性启发和奖励设计效应，本文推导出精确分解并通过控制实验量化各分量。弱先验下奖励设计效应占13.9%，强先验下占5%。对两个已发表结果重审计，分别得出启发主导和奖励设计主导的结论。

<h3 id="575">[575] Learning Visual Spatial Planning from Symbolic State via Modality-Gap-Aware Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06076) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haocheng Luo, Jiahui Liu, Ruicheng Zhang, Zhizhou Zhong, Jiaqi Huang, Zunnan Xu, Quan Shi, Jun Zhou, Xiu Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Visual Reasoning, Spatial Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Oranger-l/MGSD)
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型在视觉空间规划上因感知-推理模态差距而表现不足，本文提出MGSD两阶段自蒸馏框架：冷启动阶段建立可靠状态表示，特权教师通过策略蒸馏转移规划能力。在视觉规划基准上，4B和8B骨干的宏平均分别提升19.3%和18.4%，缩小了与符号输入的差距。

<h3 id="576">[576] Towards Healthy Evolution: Exploring the Role and Mechanisms of Human-Agent Interaction in Self-Evolving Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06114) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dianxing Shi, Bowen Wang, Junqi He, Junhao Chen, Yuta Nakashima
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Improvement, Human-AI Collaboration, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 自进化智能体通过自博弈改进但可能能力退化，本文提出ANCHOR框架模拟人类监督在自进化各阶段提供反馈。实验表明有限监督显著缓解安全退化，输出验证阶段的监督最有效，增加监督频率收益递减。

<h3 id="577">[577] An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yonchanok Khaokaew, Ruochen Kong, Andreas Zufle, Hao Xue, Taylor Anderson, Chandini Raina MacIntyre, Matthew Scotch, Flora D. Salim, David J Heslop
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent-Based Model, Epidemic Forecasting
- **Summary**: 针对疫情中建模个体决策行为的需求，提出一个基于LLM决策、空间锚定的智能体模拟框架，结合人口普查数据实现地理多样化。实验发现收入和学历是报告率变化的主要驱动因素，地理、LLM模型选择和消息框架也有影响。该框架能生成包含社会和地理异质性的合成数据。

<h3 id="578">[578] Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaju Chen, Yuxuan Lu, Jiayi Su, Chaoran Chen, Songlin Xiao, Zheng Zhang, Yun Wang, Yunyao Li, Jian Zhao, Tongshuang Wu, Toby Jia-Jun Li, Dakuo Wang, Bingsheng Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 针对当前智能体缺乏协作过程中心理模型对齐能力的问题，构建了包含2987个协作动作及其心理模型标注的数据集，并评估了6个LLM预测下一轮行为和心理模型的能力。结果表明该数据集可用于评估模型模拟人类协作行为的能力。

<h3 id="579">[579] A Study of Parallel Continuous Local Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06656](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06656) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cody J Christopher, Charles Gretton
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, Continuous Local Search
- **Summary**: 研究并行连续局部搜索（CLS）解决布尔可满足性问题。发现冗余约束会抑制收敛，CLS在混合设置中作为子求解器有潜力，局部搜索由于鞍点密集的目标快速收敛到稳定解质量分布。这些发现为在加速器上使用CLS解决SAT提供指导。

<h3 id="580">[580] Front-to-Attractors: Modifying the Front-to-Front Heuristic in Bidirectional Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07047](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07047) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alvin Zou, Muhammad Suhail Saleem, Maxim Likhachev
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Planning, Bidirectional Search
- **Summary**: 针对双向搜索中前向-前向启发式计算开销大的问题，提出前向-吸引子启发式（F2A），用少量动态吸引子代替整个前沿。实验表明相比前向-前向启发式，成对评估减少11.2倍，节点扩展比前向-终端启发式平均减少4.8倍，同时保持最优性保证。

<h3 id="581">[581] DyCon: Dynamic Reasoning Control via Evolving Difficulty Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07108](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07108) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tengyao Tu, Yulin Li, Hui-Ling Zhen, Libo Qin, Zhoujun Wei, Jinghua Piao, Zhuotao Tian, Yong Li, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Overthinking
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yu-lin-li/DyCon)
- **Summary**: 针对大型推理模型因冗余推理导致的“过度思考”问题，提出DyCon框架，利用潜在步骤级表示显式建模任务难度动态变化，从而控制推理深度。在多模型多基准实验上，该方法在保持准确率的同时显著减少冗余步骤，提升推理效率。

<h3 id="582">[582] Large Models for Time Series and Spatio-Temporal Data: A Survey and Outlook</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2310.10196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.10196) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ming Jin, Yaxuan Kong, Yuxuan Liang, Chaoli Zhang, Siqiao Xue, Xue Wang, James Zhang, Yi Wang, Haifeng Chen, Xiaoli Li, Vincent S. Tseng, Yu Zheng, Lei Chen, Hui Xiong, Shirui Pan, Qingsong Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Survey, Time Series Forecasting, Spatiotemporal Reasoning
- **Venue**: ACM Computing Surveys
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/qingsongedu/Awesome-TimeSeries-SpatioTemporal-LM-LLM)
- **Summary**: 综述针对时间序列和时空数据的大模型，沿数据类别、模型类别、模型范围和应用领域四个维度组织现有工作，分为LM4TS和LM4STD两类，并区分通用与领域特定模型。还整理了相关资源，指出了未来研究方向。

<h3 id="583">[583] Toward autocorrection of chemical process flowsheets using large language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2312.02873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.02873) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lukas Schulze Balhorn, Marc Caballero, Artur M. Schweidtmann
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI-Assisted Design, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 针对流程图中错误导致安全隐患的问题，提出基于LLM的自动修正模型，以有错流程图输入输出修正建议。在合成数据集上达到80%的top-1准确率和84%的top-5准确率，表明模型能学习修正合成流程图。

<h3 id="584">[584] Investigating the Histogram Loss in Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.13425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13425) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ehsan Imani, Kai Luedemann, Sam Scholnick-Hughes, Esraa Elelimy, Martha White
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Training Dynamics, Optimization
- **Summary**: 探讨在回归中学习分布（如直方图损失）为何能提升性能。理论和实证分析表明性能提升主要来源于优化改进而非额外信息建模。该损失在常见深度学习应用中无需昂贵超参数调优即可使用。

<h3 id="585">[585] Strategic Integration of Artificial Intelligence in the C-Suite: The Role of the Chief AI Officer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.10247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10247) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Marc Schmitt
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG; econ.GN
- **Tags**: AI Governance, Chief AI Officer
- **Summary**: 针对企业将AI融入战略时现有高管角色不完全适配的问题，提出角色设计理论，识别AI的三种属性及组织应对的三种配置，并建立CAIO框架。给出何时设立CAIO、组织反应形式、有效性及演化规律的四项命题。

<h3 id="586">[586] Discovering Data Structures: Nearest Neighbor Search and Beyond</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.03253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03253) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Omar Salemohamed, Laurent Charlin, Shivam Garg, Vatsal Sharan, Gregory Valiant
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DS
- **Tags**: Approximate Nearest Neighbor, Learned Data Structure
- **Summary**: 提出端到端学习数据结构的通用框架，无需精心初始化，可适应数据分布。在最近邻搜索中，一维发现最优算法（如二分搜索），高维学习类似k-d树或LSH的解决方案。也适用于频率估计。

<h3 id="587">[587] Graph-to-SFILES: Control structure prediction from process topologies using generative artificial intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.00508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00508) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lukas Schulze Balhorn, Kevin Degens, Artur M. Schweidtmann
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Generative AI, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 针对控制结构设计繁琐的问题，提出Graph-to-SFILES模型，以流程图拓扑图输入预测控制扩展流程图序列。在10K流程图数据集上top-5准确率73.2%，提出的GNN编码器表现最佳；在少数据场景下优于纯序列方法。

<h3 id="588">[588] Complement or substitute? How AI increases the demand for human skills</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.19754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19754) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Elina Mäkelä, Matthew Bone, Mareike Sehrer, Farah Nanji, Fabian Stephany
- **Subjects**: econ.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Economics, Skill Demand
- **Summary**: 实证研究AI采纳如何影响技能需求，分析美国、英国、澳大利亚近3000万条招聘数据。发现AI密集型岗位更需互补性非技术技能（如分析思维），这些技能带来工资溢价；AI扩散增加非AI岗位对互补技能的需求，减少可替代技能需求。

<h3 id="589">[589] FIT-Print: Towards False-claim-resistant Model Ownership Verification via Targeted Fingerprint</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.15509](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15509) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuo Shao, Haozhe Zhu, Yiming Li, Hongwei Yao, Tianwei Zhang, Zhan Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Fingerprinting, Model Security, IP Protection
- **Venue**: IEEE Transactions on Information Forensics and Security
- **Summary**: 针对现有模型指纹技术易受虚假主权攻击的问题，提出FIT-Print目标指纹范式和两种黑盒方法。在基准模型和数据集上，完美防御虚假主权攻击（100%），零误报，100%所有权验证率。

<h3 id="590">[590] Dealing with Annotator Disagreement in Hate Speech Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.08266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08266) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Somaiyeh Dehghan, Mehmet Umut Sen, Berrin Yanikoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Hate Speech Detection, Annotator Disagreement
- **Summary**: 针对仇恨言论标注主观性强导致标注者分歧的问题，评估多种聚合方法（多数投票、序数策略等），并利用感知仇恨强度进行回归/混合建模。发现过滤非共识样本导致过于乐观结果，感知强度能提升性能，在土耳其推文上取得新最优结果。

<h3 id="591">[591] Deep Tree Tensor Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.09928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09928) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chang Nie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tensor Network, Image Classification, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/NieCha/deep_tree_tensor_network)
- **Summary**: 针对张量网络如MPS在自然图像识别中效果不佳的问题，提出深度树张量网络（DTTN），通过多线性操作捕获2^L阶特征交互，具有树状拓扑和参数共享。在多个基准上表现优于同类方法和先进架构。

<h3 id="592">[592] Audio-FLAN: An Instruction-Following Dataset for Unified Audio Understanding and Generation of Speech, Music, and Sound</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.16584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16584) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liumeng Xue, Ziya Zhou, Jiahao Pan, Zixuan Li, Shuai Fan, Yinghao Ma, Sitong Cheng, Dongchao Yang, Haohan Guo, Yujia Xiao, Xinsheng Wang, Zixuan Shen, Chuanbo Zhu, Xinshen Zhang, Tianchi Liu, Ruibin Yuan, Zeyue Tian, Haohe Liu, Xingjian Du, Emmanouil Benetos, Ge Zhang, Yike Guo, Wei Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MM; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Instruction Tuning, Audio-Language Model, Audio Generation
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏统一音频理解和生成指令微调数据集的问题，构建Audio-FLAN，涵盖语音、音乐、声音领域80个任务，超过1亿实例。该数据集为统一的音频语言模型在零样本下处理理解和生成任务奠定基础。

<h3 id="593">[593] Rule-based autocorrection of Piping and Instrumentation Diagrams (P&IDs) on graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.18493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18493) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lukas Schulze Balhorn, Niels Seijsener, Kevin Dao, Minji Kim, Dominik P. Goldstein, Ge H. M. Driessen, Artur M. Schweidtmann
- **Subjects**: cs.CE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: P&ID Autocorrection, Document Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对P&ID图纸人工审查工作量大、效率低的问题，提出一种基于图表示的规则自动校正方法。该方法利用pyDEXPI将P&ID转换为图，并通过33条化工知识与启发式规则实现错误检测与修正。案例研究表明方法可靠有效。

<h3 id="594">[594] LoTUS: Large-Scale Machine Unlearning with a Taste of Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.18314](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18314) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Christoforos N. Spartalis, Theodoros Semertzidis, Petros Daras, Efstratios Gavves
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning
- **Venue**: CVPR 2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cspartalis/LoTUS)
- **Summary**: 为解决机器学习中需重训练才能移除样本影响的问题，提出LoTUS去学习方法。该方法通过平滑预测概率至信息论上界来减轻模型过度自信，无需重训练。在Transformer和ResNet18模型上，LoTUS在多个数据集（包括ImageNet1k）上优于现有方法，并引入新指标RF-JSD。

<h3 id="595">[595] Brain2Text Decoding Model Reveals the Neural Mechanisms of Visual Semantic Processing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.22697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22697) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Feihan Feng, Jingxin Nie
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Computational Neuroscience, Brain-Computer Interface
- **Summary**: 针对fMRI解码视觉语义内容缺乏与神经科学理论结合的问题，提出直接解码fMRI为文本的深度学习模型。模型在无视觉信息训练下达到最优语义解码性能，并揭示了高级视觉皮层在语义处理中的作用。类别分析显示对生命性、运动等维度的细粒度神经表征。

<h3 id="596">[596] Hyperflux: Pruning Reveals Importance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.05349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05349) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Eugen Barbulescu, Antonio Alexoaie, Lucian Busoniu
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Sparse Training
- **Summary**: 针对网络剪枝过程理解不足的问题，提出基于L0范数的Hyperflux方法。该方法将剪枝建模为由通量（flux）和压力（pressure）驱动的连续演化系统，并引入新的压力调度器。在ResNet-50、VGG-19、DeiT等模型上取得有竞争力的结果，同时提供微观和宏观层面的可解释性。

<h3 id="597">[597] Robust Renal Mass Segmentation on CT: A Validation Study of an AI-Based Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.07573](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07573) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sarah de Boer, Hartmut Häntze, Kiran Vaidhya Venkadesh, Myrthe A. D. Buser, Gabriel E. Humpire Mamani, Lina Xu, Lisa C. Adams, Jawed Nawabi, Keno K. Bressem, Bram van Ginneken, Mathias Prokop, Alessa Hering
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation
- **Venue**: Journal of Machine Learning for Biomedical Imaging (MELBA)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DIAGNijmegen/oncology-kidney-abnormality-segmentation)
- **Summary**: 针对肾肿块分割在临床评估中的需求，提出基于nnU-Net的Renal-Net分割算法。该方法仅使用公开数据训练，在多个内部和外部测试集上优于现有模型，并且在不同性别、年龄、CT时相和肿瘤亚型中表现稳健。

<h3 id="598">[598] Harmonia: End-to-End RAG Serving Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.07833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07833) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Saurabh Agarwal, Bodun Hu, Luis Pabon, Myungjin Lee, Jayanth Srinivasa, Aditya Akella
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.OS
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Serving
- **Summary**: 针对RAG服务中异构组件（LLM、数据库、CPU处理）效率低下的问题，提出端到端优化框架Harmonia。该框架通过灵活的流水线接口、异构感知部署和闭环运行时控制器，实现吞吐量提升2.04倍以上，SLO违规减少78.4%。

<h3 id="599">[599] ePC: Fast and Deep Predictive Coding in Digital Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.20137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20137) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cédric Goemaere, Gaspard Oliviers, Rafal Bogacz, Thomas Demeester
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Predictive Coding
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cgoemaere/error_based_PC)
- **Summary**: 针对预测编码（PC）在数字仿真中信号衰减导致效率低下的问题，提出误差基PC（ePC）重新参数化方法。ePC避免了信号衰减，计算精确梯度，运行速度比状态基PC快数个数量级。实验表明ePC性能与反向传播相当，且能扩展到更深架构。

<h3 id="600">[600] ACTIVE-o3: Empowering MLLMs with Active Perception via Pure Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.21457](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21457) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Muzhi Zhu, Hao Zhong, Canyu Zhao, Zongze Du, Mingyu Liu, Zheng Huang, Anzhou Li, Hao Chen, Cheng Zou, Jingdong Chen, Ming Yang, Chunhua Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Reinforcement Learning, Active Perception
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型缺乏主动感知能力的问题，提出基于GRPO强化学习的ACTIVE-o3框架。通过模块化感知-动作设计和双形式奖励，模型自主学会高效区域选择策略。在开放世界和领域特定任务上显著提升主动感知能力，并保持通用理解能力。

<h3 id="601">[601] dLLM-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Models with Adaptive Caching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.06295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06295) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Liu, Yicun Yang, Yaojie Zhang, Junjie Chen, Chang Zou, Qingyan Wei, Shaobo Wang, Yichen Zhu, Linfeng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/maomaocun/dLLM-cache)
- **Summary**: 针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）推理延迟高、KV缓存不兼容的问题，提出无训练自适应缓存框架dLLM-Cache。该方法结合长间隔提示缓存和基于特征相似度的部分响应更新，实现高达9.1倍的FLOPs降低，推理延迟接近自回归模型。

<h3 id="602">[602] Failure by Interference: Language Models Make Balanced Parentheses Errors When Faulty Mechanisms Overshadow Sound Ones</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.00322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00322) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daking Rai, Samuel Miller, Kevin Moran, Ziyu Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Code Generation
- **Venue**: NeurIPS 2025
- **Summary**: 针对语言模型在括号生成等简单语法任务上持续出错的问题，机制研究发现模型存在可靠组件和故障组件，故障组件压倒可靠组件时出错。提出RASteer方法增强可靠组件贡献，使括号正确率从0%提升至约100%，且不影响通用编码能力。

<h3 id="603">[603] AMix-1: A Pathway to Test-Time Scalable Protein Foundation Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.08920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08920) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Changze Lv, Jiang Zhou, Siyu Long, Lihao Wang, Jiangtao Feng, Dongyu Xue, Yu Pei, Hao Wang, Zherui Zhang, Yuchen Cai, Zhiqiang Gao, Ziyuan Ma, Jiakai Hu, Chaochen Gao, Jingjing Gong, Yuxuan Song, Shuyi Zhang, Xiaoqing Zheng, Deyi Xiong, Lei Bai, Wanli Ouyang, Ya-Qin Zhang, Wei-Ying Ma, Bowen Zhou, Hao Zhou
- **Subjects**: q-bio.BM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Language Model, Molecular Generation, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: 针对蛋白质基础模型可扩展性问题，提出基于贝叶斯流网络的AMix-1模型。通过预训练缩放定律、上下文学习策略和测试时缩放算法，构建了17亿参数的强大模型。利用MSA上下文学习成功设计出活性提高50倍的AmeR变体。

<h3 id="604">[604] Learning Task Mixtures from Task Affinities: A Probabilistic Graphical Model for Supervised Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.12612](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12612) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Prateek Chanda, Saral Sureka, Parth Pratim Chatterjee, Krishnateja Killamsetty, Nikhil Shivakumar Nayak, Ganesh Ramakrishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Task Learning, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对监督微调中任务混合启发式方法忽视任务交互的问题，提出基于能量模型的任务混合框架TaskPGM。通过任务间行为散度（JS散度、点互信息）构建无向图，优化得到平衡覆盖与冗余的混合比例。在LLaMA-7B和Qwen2-7B上优于标准策略。

<h3 id="605">[605] CLONE: A 3DGS-Based Closed-Loop Differentiable Optimization Framework for Single-Image Normal Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.05950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05950) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanxing Liang, Yinghui Wang, Wei Li, Tao Yan, Jiaxing Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, 3D Reconstruction, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 针对单图像法线估计方法缺乏跨域几何约束或可微优化路径的问题，提出基于3D高斯泼溅的闭环可微优化框架CLONE。通过参数化场景、可微光照模型和扩散精化网络，在无需真实法线监督下实现端到端优化，提高了几何一致性。

<h3 id="606">[606] Unsupervised Partner Design Enables Robust Ad-hoc Teamwork</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.06336](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06336) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Constantin Ruhdorfer, Matteo Bortoletto, Victor Oei, Anna Penzkofer, Andreas Bulling
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 针对无预设伙伴的临时团队合作中需要鲁棒多智能体强化学习的问题，提出无监督伙伴设计方法UPD。该方法在线生成训练伙伴并基于可学习性自适应选择，无需预先训练的伙伴库。在多个环境和人类-AI实验中表现优于基线，被评为更具适应性、更类人、更少挫败。

<h3 id="607">[607] In-Context Reinforcement Learning via Communicative World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.06659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06659) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fernando Martinez-Lopez, Tao Li, Yingdong Lu, Juntao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Emergent Communication, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习代理难以泛化到新任务且需更新参数的问题，提出通信式世界模型CORAL框架。将上下文RL转化为双智能体涌现通信问题：信息代理作为世界模型提供简洁消息，控制代理利用消息学习任务。实验证明零样本适应能力和样本效率提升。

<h3 id="608">[608] Discovering Expert-Level Nash Equilibrium Algorithms with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.11874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11874) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanyu Li, Dongchen Li, Xiaotie Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.DS; cs.LO; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Game Theory, LLM Reasoning, Algorithm Design
- **Venue**: Nature Communications
- **Summary**: 针对设计具有最坏情况保证的近似纳什均衡多项式时间算法这一开放问题，提出LegoNE框架。该框架将专家证明策略编码为符号语言，自动验证算法保证。结合推理LLM，重新发现了双人博弈中最优多项式时间算法，并将三人博弈保证从0.6+δ提升到0.5+δ，超越了扩展技术范式。

<h3 id="609">[609] Video Understanding by Design: How Datasets Shape Video Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.09151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09151) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lei Wang, Syuan-Hao Li, Piotr Koniusz, Yongsheng Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文指出目前视频理解综述缺乏从数据集角度解释模型演变的视角。提出以数据集为中心的框架，统一数据集结构、归纳偏置和架构设计，认为双流网络、3D CNN、Transformer等架构是对数据集挑战的响应。系统分析了数据集特征如何驱动创新，并讨论不同数据 regime 的表示偏置。

<h3 id="610">[610] I-Segmenter: Integer-Only Vision Transformer for Efficient Semantic Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.10334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10334) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jordan Sassoon, Michal Szczepanski, Martyna Poreba
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Semantic Segmentation, Quantization, Vision Transformer
- **Venue**: Journal of Systems Architecture
- **Summary**: 视觉Transformer在语义分割中效果良好但部署困难。提出I-Segmenter，第一个完全整数化的ViT分割框架，通过λ-ShiftGELU等改进实现全整数计算。精度仅下降5.1%，模型大小减少3.8倍，推理加速1.2倍。

<h3 id="611">[611] Decoupling the "What" and "Where" With Polar Coordinate Positional Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.10534](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10534) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anand Gopalakrishnan, Robert Csordás, Jürgen Schmidhuber, Michael C. Mozer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Position Encoding, Transformer Architecture
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 分析RoPE位置编码中内容与位置纠缠问题，提出PoPE（极坐标位置嵌入）消除混淆。在诊断任务上显著优于RoPE，在自回归序列建模中降低困惑度，并展示强零样本长度外推能力。

<h3 id="612">[612] MVCL-DAF++: Enhancing Multimodal Intent Recognition via Prototype-Aware Contrastive Alignment and Coarse-to-Fine Dynamic Attention Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.17446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17446) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haofeng Huang, Yifei Han, Long Zhang, Bin Li, Yangfan He, Yaxin Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Intent Recognition, Contrastive Learning
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/chr1s623/MVCL-DAF-PlusPlus)
- **Summary**: 多模态意图识别面临语义基础弱和噪声鲁棒性差问题。提出MVCL-DAF++，包含原型感知对比对齐和粗到细注意力融合。在MIntRec和MIntRec2.0上达到SOTA，稀有类识别提升1.05%和4.18% WF1。

<h3 id="613">[613] Understanding Benchmark Language Under Weakened Formal Semantics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.17455](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17455) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoyang Chen, Kumiko Tanaka-Ishii
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Code Generation, Natural Language Understanding
- **Venue**: Transactions of the Association for Computational Linguistics (TACL)
- **Summary**: NLP基准测试需要解释自然语言，但构建完整语义表示困难。本文通过提取可计算表示（computables）提供可检查的语义证据。在数学推理、因果推理、法律和生物医学基准上，该方法持续优于纯文本推理和一次性代码执行。

<h3 id="614">[614] Generation Properties of Stochastic Interpolation under Finite Training Set</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.21925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21925) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunchen Li, Shaohui Lin, Zhou Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Model, Diffusion Model, Stochastic Interpolation
- **Summary**: 研究有限训练样本下生成模型的理论行为。推导最优速度场和得分函数的闭式表达式，证明确定性生成精确恢复训练样本，随机生成则加高斯噪声。考虑估计误差后，生成样本为训练样本的凸组合加噪声。

<h3 id="615">[615] SecureVibeBench: Benchmarking Secure Vibe Coding of AI Agents via Reconstructing Vulnerability-Introducing Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.22097](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22097) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junkai Chen, Huihui Huang, Yunbo Lyu, Junwen An, Jieke Shi, Chengran Yang, Ting Zhang, Haoye Tian, Yikun Li, Zhenhao Li, Xin Zhou, Xing Hu, David Lo
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, LLM Security
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/iCSawyer/SecureVibeBench)
- **Summary**: LLM代码代理存在安全风险，现有基准未捕获人类引入漏洞场景。提出SecureVibeBench，含105个C/C++安全编码任务，评估5个代码代理和5个LLM。最佳代理仅产生23.8%正确且安全的解决方案。

<h3 id="616">[616] VFEM: Visual Feature Empowered Multivariate Time Series Forecasting with Cross-Modal Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.03244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03244) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanlong Wang, Hang Yu, Jian Xu, Fei Ma, Hongkang Zhang, Tongtong Feng, Zijian Zhang, Shao-Lun Huang, Danny Dongning Sun, Xiao-Ping Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Multimodal Learning, Cross-Modal Fusion
- **Summary**: 时间序列基础模型忽略跨变量依赖，现有跨模态方法多基于文本。提出VFEM，利用预训练大视觉模型捕捉跨变量模式，通过双分支结构和交叉模态注意力融合。冻结视觉模型仅训练7.45%参数，取得竞争力性能。

<h3 id="617">[617] Projection and Quantisation: A Unifying View of Learning to Hash, from Random Projections to the RAG Era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.04127](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04127) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sean Moran
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Approximate Nearest Neighbor, Quantization, RAG
- **Summary**: 近似最近邻搜索支撑大规模检索和RAG。提出投影-量化-组织（PQO）视角，统一多种哈希方法。测量表明内存节省主要在量化轴，监督下8字节码质量超过2KB浮点。发布BitBudget基准。

<h3 id="618">[618] Large Language Models for Imbalanced Classification: Diversity makes the difference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.09783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09783) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dang Nguyen, Sunil Gupta, Kien Do, Thin Nguyen, Taylor Braund, Alexis Whitton, Svetha Venkatesh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Class Imbalance, Data Synthesis, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 过采样处理不平衡分类，但现有LLM方法生成样本多样性有限。提出新LLM过采样方法，通过条件生成、新排列策略和插值训练增强多样性。在10个表格数据集上超越8个基线。

<h3 id="619">[619] Efficient Onboard Vision-Language Inference in UAV-Enabled Low-Altitude Economy Networks via LLM-Enhanced Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.10028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10028) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Li, Ruichen Zhang, Yinqiu Liu, Guangyuan Liu, Abbas Jamalipour, Xianbin Wang, Dong In Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Reinforcement Learning, UAV
- **Summary**: 无人机搭载视觉语言模型进行实时多模态推理面临挑战。提出层次优化框架，含ARPO资源分配算法和LLaRA轨迹优化（LLM增强强化学习）。数值结果证明有效性。

<h3 id="620">[620] TAO: Tolerance-Aware Optimistic Verification for Floating-Point Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.16028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16028) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jianzhu Yao, Hongxu Su, Taobo Liao, Zerui Cheng, Huan Zhang, Xuechao Wang, Pramod Viswanath
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Model Verification, Hardware Security
- **Summary**: ML即服务中用户无法验证浮点网络输出。提出TAO验证协议，结合最坏情况界和经验百分位阈值，实现操作符级别可接受区域。PyTorch运行时和以太坊合约，开销仅0.3%。

<h3 id="621">[621] PLAGUE: Plug-and-play framework for Lifelong Adaptive Generation of Multi-turn Exploits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.17947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17947) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Neeladri Bhuiya, Madhav Aggarwal, Diptanshu Purwar
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Red Teaming
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Summary**: LLM多轮对话易受越狱攻击。提出PLAGUE框架，基于终身学习代理设计多轮攻击。在o3和Opus 4.1上分别达81.4%和67.3%攻击成功率。

<h3 id="622">[622] SmartMixed: A Two-Phase Training Strategy for Adaptive Activation Function Learning in Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.22450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22450) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Amin Omidvar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Activation Function, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 大多数网络使用固定激活函数。提出SmartMixed两阶段训练策略，让神经元自适应选择激活函数池，然后固定选择。在MNIST上分析显示不同层偏好不同。

<h3 id="623">[623] Learning Quantized Continuous Controllers for Integer Hardware</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.07046](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07046) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fabian Kresse, Christoph H. Lampert
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Quantization, FPGA
- **Summary**: 连续控制RL策略部署到FPGA需低延迟低功耗。研究量化感知训练，自动选择低比特策略并在Artix-7 FPGA上综合。在5个MuJoCo任务中，策略与全精度相当，推理微秒级。

<h3 id="624">[624] Correcting Mean Bias in Text Embeddings: A Refined Renormalization with Training-Free Improvements on MMTEB</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.11041](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11041) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Ren, Youran Sun, Haoyu Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text Embedding, Representation Learning, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 发现句子嵌入存在一致均值偏差。提出两种免训练修正方法R1和R2，R2通过投影消除均值方向。在38个模型上R2持续改进分类性能，与全PCA白化对比。

<h3 id="625">[625] SMART: Shot-Aware Multimodal Video Moment Retrieval with Audio-Enhanced MLLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.14143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14143) (replaced)
- **Authors**: An Yu, Weiheng Lu, Jian Li, Zhenfei Zhang, Yunhang Shen, Felix X.-F. Ye, Ming-Ching Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Moment Retrieval, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 现有视频时刻检索方法依赖粗略的时间理解和单一视觉模态，在复杂视频上性能有限。本文提出SMART框架，结合音频线索和镜头级时间结构，通过镜头感知令牌压缩保留细粒度细节。在Charades-STA和QVHighlights上，SMART取得了显著提升，R1@0.5提高1.61%，R1@0.7提高2.59%。

<h3 id="626">[626] AttnRegDeepLab: A Two-Stage Decoupled Framework for Interpretable Embryo Fragmentation Grading</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.18454](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18454) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ming-Jhe Lee, Chang-Hong Wu, Jung-Hua Wang, Ming-Jer Chen, Yu-Chiao Yi, Tsung-Hsien Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Segmentation, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: 胚胎碎片分级人工评估主观，现有AI模型可解释性差且分割错误多。本文提出AttnRegDeepLab多任务学习框架，通过注意力门控和多尺度回归头增强分割与分级。该模型在保持高分级精度的同时，Dice系数达0.729，避免了轮廓完整性与分级精度的权衡。

<h3 id="627">[627] SAGE: Shape-Adapting Gated Experts for Adaptive Histopathology Image Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.18493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18493) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gia Huy Thai, Hoang-Nguyen Vu, Anh-Minh Phan, Quang-Thinh Ly, Thi-Ngoc-Truc Nguyen, Nhat Ho
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Segmentation, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 (Findings Track)
- **Summary**: 细胞大小和形状的显著变异阻碍了计算机辅助癌症检测，现有混合网络使用静态计算图难以适应输入变化。本文提出SAGE框架，通过动态专家路由和形状自适应枢纽实现输入自适应。在多项医学图像分割基准上，SAGE的Dice评分最高达95.23%，并展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

<h3 id="628">[628] MedVision: Benchmarking Quantitative Medical Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.18676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18676) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yongcheng Yao, Yongshuo Zong, Raman Dutt, Yongxin Yang, Sotirios A Tsaftaris, Timothy Hospedales
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Benchmark, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有医学视觉语言模型仅支持分类问答或定性描述，缺乏定量分析能力。本文引入MedVision数据集和基准，涵盖22个公共数据集和30.8百万图像-标注对，评估检测、尺寸估计和角度测量任务。实验表明，现有VLM性能不佳，但在MedVision上微调后显著提升。

<h3 id="629">[629] SVRG and Beyond via Posterior Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.01930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01930) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nico Daheim, Thomas Möllenhoff, Ming Liang Ang, Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: SVRG及其变体与贝叶斯方法缺乏联系。本文证明SVRG是后验校正的特例，并利用更灵活的指数族后验自动衍生出牛顿型变体和Adam型变体。这些新扩展加速了训练过程。

<h3 id="630">[630] Developing Distance-Aware Physics-Constrained Probabilistic Frameworks for Industrial Prognostics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.08499](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08499) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Waleed Razzaq, Yun-Bo Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Physics-Informed Learning, Prognostics and Health Management
- **Summary**: 现有工业预测的概率框架在输入远离训练流形时缺乏敏感性。本文开发了两种距离感知、物理约束的概率框架PC-SNGP和PC-SNER，采用谱归一化和动态加权策略。在轴承预测基准上，这些方法提高了预测精度和校准的不确定性，并具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="631">[631] Unambiguous Representations in Neural Networks: An Information-Theoretic Approach to Intentionality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.11000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11000) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Francesco Lässig
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Interpretability, Information Theory
- **Venue**: Models of Consciousness 6 (MoC6)
- **Summary**: 意识体验的表示具有内在确定性，但现有表示通常需要外部解码器。本文用信息论定义表示模糊度，在MNIST分类网络上实验。结果表明，网络连接中的关系结构可以明确编码表示内容，且模糊度与准确性正交。

<h3 id="632">[632] FADTI: Fourier and Attention Driven Diffusion for Multivariate Time Series Imputation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.15116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15116) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Runze Li, Hanchen Wang, Wenjie Zhang, Binghao Li, Yu Zhang, Xuemin Lin, Ying Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Diffusion Model, Data Imputation
- **Summary**: 现有Transformer和扩散模型缺乏频率显式偏置，泛化能力受限。本文提出FADTI，通过可学习傅里叶偏置投影注入频率信息，结合自注意力和门控卷积。在多个基准上，特别是高缺失率下，FADTI优于现有方法。

<h3 id="633">[633] Collaborative Edge-to-Server Inference for Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.16349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16349) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Soochang Song, Yongjune Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 边缘设备传输全分辨率图像给服务器进行VLM推理通信成本高。本文提出两阶段协作框架：服务器先用缩略图推理，若输出最小熵超过阈值则请求局部细节。实验表明，该方法在保持推理精度的同时大幅降低通信开销。

<h3 id="634">[634] Exploring the Effect of Basis Rotation on NQS Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.17893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17893) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sven Benjamin Kožić, Vinko Zlatić, Fabio Franchini, Salvatore Marco Giampaolo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Representation Learning, Optimization
- **Summary**: 神经量子态的性能依赖于基选择。本文使用精确可解Ising模型，证明局部基旋转不改变最小化景观但移动基态位置。即使旋转后的目标状态仍可表示，优化失败仍可能发生。这揭示了NQS基依赖的几何机制。

<h3 id="635">[635] GenTSE: Enhancing Target Speaker Extraction via a Coarse-to-Fine Generative Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.20978](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20978) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoyang Li, Xuyi Zhuang, Azmat Adnan, Ye Ni, Wei Rao, Shreyas Gopal, Eng Siong Chng, Boon Siew Han, Yuanjin Zheng
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Generative Model, Target Speaker Extraction
- **Venue**: Interspeech2026
- **Summary**: 生成式语言模型用于目标说话人提取有潜力但存在暴露偏差。本文提出GenTSE，两阶段解码器：粗语义标记和细声学标记。采用冻结LM条件和DPO对齐。在Libri2Mix上，GenTSE在语音质量、可懂度和说话人一致性上超越先前LM系统。

<h3 id="636">[636] Supracompetitive Pricing Under AI Monoculture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.01279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01279) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shengyu Cao, Ming Hu
- **Subjects**: econ.TH; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CL; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Algorithmic Pricing, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 当竞争卖家共享AI模型定价时，相关推荐可能导致超竞争定价。本文构建双寡头模型，分析AI的参数（倾向性和输出保真度）。发现高于临界保真度时，系统出现双稳态：竞争和超竞争定价共存。小批量训练时，超竞争概率接近1。任何降低倾向与定价对齐的因素都会推动市场竞争。

<h3 id="637">[637] Adversarial Instance Generation and Robust Training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Multiple Objectives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.01665](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01665) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wei Liu, Yaoxin Wu, Yingqian Zhang, Thomas Bäck, Yingjie Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Combinatorial Optimization, Adversarial Robustness, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Summary**: 深度强化学习求解多目标组合优化问题的鲁棒性尚未充分探索。本文提出统一鲁棒框架，包括基于偏好的对抗攻击生成困难实例，以及硬度感知偏好选择防御策略。在多目标TSP、CVRP和KP上，攻击成功暴露脆弱性，防御显著增强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

<h3 id="638">[638] Vision-Based Early Fault Diagnosis and Self-Recovery for Strawberry Harvesting Robots</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.02085](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02085) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Meili Sun, Chunjiang Zhao, Lichao Yang, Hao Liu, Shimin Hu, Ya Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Computer Vision, Fault Diagnosis
- **Venue**: Artificial Intelligence in Agriculture
- **Summary**: 草莓采摘机器人存在视觉感知差、夹爪不对准、空抓和滑移等问题。本文提出视觉故障诊断与自恢复框架，包括SRR-Net统一感知、相对误差补偿、微光相机和LSTM滑移预测。实验将定位误差从~10mm降至~3-4mm，滑移处理成功率88.89%，恢复率81.25%。

<h3 id="639">[639] A large-scale nanocrystal database with aligned synthesis and properties enabling generative inverse design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.02424](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02424) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kai Gu, Yingping Liang, Senliang Peng, Aotian Guo, Haizheng Zhong, Ying Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Materials Science, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 纳米晶合成依赖试错法，缺乏高质量数据集。本文构建大规模纳米晶合成-属性对齐数据库NSP，使用带增强策略的LLM NanoExtractor从文献中提取结构化数据。NanoDesigner成功设计了PbSe和MgF2纳米晶的合成路线，并发现反直觉的非化学计量前驱体比例。

<h3 id="640">[640] One if by Land, Two if by Sea, Three if by Four Seas, and More to Come -- Values of Perception, Prediction, Communication, and Common Sense in Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06077) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aolin Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.OC
- **Tags**: Decision Making, Information Theory, Autonomous Systems
- **Summary**: 需要严格定义感知、预测、通信和常识在决策中的价值。本文提出决策论定义，具有信息论类比。有趣发现：无预测的感知价值可能为负。这些定义有助于回答自主决策系统设计中的实际问题，并为认知科学提供见解。

<h3 id="641">[641] How Context Shapes Truth: Geometric Transformations of Statement-level Truth Representations in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06599](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06599) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shivam Adarsh, Maria Maistro, Christina Lioma
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了上下文如何改变大语言模型中真理向量的几何特征。通过测量有无上下文时真理向量的方向变化和相对大小，发现早期层向量正交，中间层收敛，上下文会放大真假表征分离；大模型通过方向变化区分相关与无关上下文，小模型通过大小变化。这是首次在激活空间中对上下文变换真理向量进行几何刻画。

<h3 id="642">[642] Revisiting Training Scale: An Empirical Study of Token Count, Power Consumption, and Parameter Efficiency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06649) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joe Dwyer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Training Efficiency, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 该论文实证研究了在固定硬件条件下增加训练token数量对大型语言模型训练效率的影响。使用1.1B参数TinyLlama模型在三个token量级上训练，发现传统性能指标收益递减，而引入功耗和执行时间后，训练效率随token增加严格单调下降。强调了效率感知评估的重要性。

<h3 id="643">[643] DYCP: Dynamic Context Pruning for Long-Form Dialogue with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07994) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nayoung Choi, Jonathan Zhang, Jinho D. Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Context Selection, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 该论文提出DyCP，一种轻量级上下文管理方法，用于长对话中动态选择相关对话片段，无需离线记忆构建。在三个长对话基准上，DyCP实现了有竞争力的答案质量和更高效的推理。

<h3 id="644">[644] MMR-GRPO: Accelerating GRPO-Style Training through Diversity-Aware Reward Reweighting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.09085](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09085) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kangda Wei, Ruihong Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/WeiKangda/MMR-GRPO)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出MMR-GRPO，通过最大边际相关性对奖励进行重加权，基于完成多样性优先选择多样化解决方案，加速GRPO训练收敛。在三种模型大小和五个数学推理基准上，平均减少47.9%训练步骤和70.2%时间。

<h3 id="645">[645] A Comparative Study of Student Perspectives on Technical Writing Feedback Quality: Evaluating LLMs, SLMs, and Humans in Computer Science Topics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.11541](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11541) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Suqing Liu, Runlong Ye, Christopher Eaton, Bogdan Simion, Michael Liut
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Small Language Model
- **Venue**: AIED 26
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了本地小语言模型在计算机科学课程中提供技术写作反馈的质量。与GPT-4和人工导师比较，发现本地SLM在可读性和可操作性上评分更高，而人类反馈在专业写作任务中更受青睐。表明SLM可作为保护隐私、零边际成本的替代方案。

<h3 id="646">[646] Multimodal Generative Engine Optimization: Rank Manipulation for Vision-Language Model Rankers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.12263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12263) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yixuan Du, Chenxiao Yu, Haoyan Xu, Ziyi Wang, Yue Zhao, Xiyang Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Adversarial Robustness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/glad-lab/MGEO)
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示视觉语言模型在多模态产品排序中的漏洞，提出多模态生成引擎优化方法，通过联合优化图像扰动和文本后缀来操纵VLM的排序决策。攻击显著优于单模态攻击和基线，表明需要对齐模型内部知识利用机制。

<h3 id="647">[647] XCR-Bench: Benchmarking Cross-Cultural Reasoning in LLMs via Culture-Specific Items and Hall's Triad</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14063) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohsinul Kabir, Tasnim Ahmed, Md Mezbaur Rahman, Shaoxiong Ji, Hassan Alhuzali, Yuechen Jiang, Jimin Huang, Sophia Ananiadou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Cultural Alignment, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出XCR-Bench，一个跨文化推理基准，包含4.1k平行句和1098个文化特定项，结合Newmark框架和Hall文化三元组。在8个多语言LLM上实验，发现模型在识别和适应特定文化类别上存在弱点，性能在深层文化维度显著下降。

<h3 id="648">[648] The Flexibility Trap: Rethinking the Value of Arbitrary Order in Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15165](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15165) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zanlin Ni, Shenzhi Wang, Yang Yue, Tianyu Yu, Weilin Zhao, Yeguo Hua, Tianyi Chen, Jun Song, Cheng Yu, Bo Zheng, Gao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/JustGRPO)
- **Summary**: 该论文发现扩散语言模型的任意顺序生成能力可能限制推理潜力，模型倾向于绕过高不确定性token导致解覆盖坍塌。提出JustGRPO方法，放弃任意顺序，使用标准GRPO，在GSM8K上达89.1%准确率，同时保留并行解码能力。

<h3 id="649">[649] CURE: Curriculum-guided Multi-task Training for Reliable Anatomy Grounded Report Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15408) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pablo Messina, Andrés Villa, Juan León Alcázar, Karen Sánchez, Carlos Hinojosa, Denis Parra, Álvaro Soto, Bernard Ghanem
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Report Generation, Curriculum Learning
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PabloMessina/CURE)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出CURE，一个错误感知的课程学习框架，用于提高放射学报告生成的视觉接地和事实一致性。通过多任务微调，在公开数据集上仅使用少量数据，将接地准确率提高+0.35 IoU，报告质量提高+0.192 CXRFEScore，幻觉减少18.6%。

<h3 id="650">[650] Just-In-Time Reinforcement Learning: Continual Learning in LLM Agents Without Gradient Updates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.18510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yibo Li, Zijie Lin, Ailin Deng, Xuan Zhang, Yufei He, Shuo Ji, Tri Cao, Bryan Hooi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Test-Time Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/liushiliushi/JitRL)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出JitRL，一种无需梯度更新的测试时策略优化框架，通过动态非参数记忆和在线优势估计直接调制LLM的logits。理论上证明是KL约束策略优化的闭式解。在WebArena和Jericho上达到训练无关方法的最佳性能，成本降低30倍以上。

<h3 id="651">[651] Meeting SLOs, Slashing Hours: Automated Enterprise LLM Optimization with OptiKIT</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20408) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nicholas Santavas, Kareem Eissa, Patrycja Cieplicka, Piotr Florek, Matteo Nulli, Stefan Vasilev, Seyyed Hadi Hashemi, Antonios Gasteratos, Shahram Khadivi
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Deployment, Model Compression, LLM Serving
- **Venue**: MLSys 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出OPTIKIT，一个分布式LLM优化框架，通过自动化模型压缩和调优工作流，降低专家门槛。在生产中实现超过2倍GPU吞吐量提升，并开源系统以促进可重复性。

<h3 id="652">[652] Comparative evaluation of training strategies using partially labelled datasets for segmentation of white matter hyperintensities and stroke lesions in FLAIR MRI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20503) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jesse Phitidis, Alison Q. Smithard, William N. Whiteley, Joanna M. Wardlaw, Miguel O. Bernabeu, Maria Valdés Hernández
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Semi-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文系统评估了六种使用部分标注数据训练白质高信号和缺血性卒中病变分割模型的策略。在2052个MRI体积上实验，发现伪标签方法最有效，能够一致地分割WMH并检测FLAIR阳性ISL，支持大规模临床研究。

<h3 id="653">[653] Mobility-Embedded POIs: Learning What A Place Is and How It Is Used from Human Movement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21149](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21149) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maria Despoina Siampou, Shushman Choudhury, Shang-Ling Hsu, Neha Arora, Cyrus Shahabi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Geospatial Representation, Representation Learning, Human Mobility
- **Summary**: 该论文提出ME-POIs框架，利用大规模人类移动数据增强POI嵌入，学习基于真实使用模式的表示。通过对比学习对齐访问嵌入与POI表示，并引入长尾稀疏性传播机制。在五个地图丰富任务上，融合移动数据的嵌入显著优于纯文本或纯移动基线。

<h3 id="654">[654] More Bang for the Buck: Improving the Inference of Large Language Models at a Fixed Budget using Reset and Discard (ReD)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21522) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sagi Meir, Tommer D. Keidar, Noam Levi, Shlomi Reuveni, Barak Hirshberg
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Inference Cost Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文提出Reset-and-Discard (ReD)方法，在固定预算下提高LLM推理的覆盖率。通过重置和丢弃查询，显著减少所需尝试次数和成本。在三个基准上实验，证明ReD优于其他分配基线。

<h3 id="655">[655] Mechanistic Data Attribution: Tracing the Training Origins of Interpretable LLM Units</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21996](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21996) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jianhui Chen, Yuzhang Luo, Liangming Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Data Attribution
- **Summary**: 该论文提出机械数据归因（MDA）框架，利用影响函数将可解释单元追溯至训练样本。实验证明去除高影响样本会显著调节可解释头的出现，并发现重复结构数据（如LaTeX）作为机械催化剂。还提出数据增强流水线加速电路收敛。

<h3 id="656">[656] UA-DCM: Uncertainty-aware Causal Decision Making via Effect Bound Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22736) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Md Musfiqur Rahman, Ziwei Jiang, Hilaf Hasson, Murat Kocaoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Uncertainty Estimation, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 该论文提出不确定性感知因果决策框架，通过效应界分解区分可消除与不可消除的因果效应不确定性。利用神经因果模型近似分解，在合成和真实数据上实验，帮助实践者决定何时需要非观测研究。

<h3 id="657">[657] MEnvAgent: Scalable Polyglot Environment Construction for Verifiable Software Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22859](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22859) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chuanzhe Guo, Jingjing Wu, Sijun He, Yang Chen, Zhaoqi Kuang, Shilong Fan, Bingjin Chen, Siqi Bao, Jing Liu, Hua Wu, Qingfu Zhu, Wanxiang Che, Haifeng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Code Generation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ernie-research/MEnvAgent)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多语言软件工程任务中可验证数据集稀缺的问题，提出MEnvAgent框架，采用多智能体规划-执行-验证架构自动构建可执行环境，并结合环境复用机制降低开销。在MEnvBench上，F2P率提升8.6%，时间成本降低43%。

<h3 id="658">[658] VideoGPA: Distilling Geometry Priors for 3D-Consistent Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.23286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23286) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongyang Du, Junjie Ye, Xiaoyan Cong, Runhao Li, Jingcheng Ni, Aman Agarwal, Zeqi Zhou, Zekun Li, Randall Balestriero, Yue Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 针对视频扩散模型存在3D结构不一致的问题，提出VideoGPA框架，利用几何基础模型自动获取密集偏好信号并通过DPO引导生成，无需人工标注。实验表明，该方法显著提升了时间稳定性、几何合理性和运动连贯性。

<h3 id="659">[659] How Hyper-Datafication Impacts the Sustainability Costs in Frontier AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00056](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00056) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sophia N. Wilson, Sebastian Mair, Mophat Okinyi, Erik B. Dam, Janin Koch, Raghavendra Selvan
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了前沿AI中大规模数据带来的环境、社会和经济效益成本，通过分析Hugging Face上的约55万个数据集，揭示了超数据化驱动的成本增长和全球不平等。提出了Data PROOFS建议以缓解这些成本。

<h3 id="660">[660] DIVERGE: Diversity-Enhanced RAG for Open-Ended Information Seeking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00238) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Hu, Niket Tandon, Akhil Arora
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 针对RAG系统在开放查询中多样性不足的问题，提出DIVERGE框架，通过反思引导的探索和多样性感知检索提升生成多样性，同时保持质量。实验表明，多样性提升约2倍且质量无明显下降。

<h3 id="661">[661] Reward Shaping for (Inference-Time) Alignment: A Stackelberg Game Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02572) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="662">[662] Performative Learning Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.04402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04402) (replaced)

<h3 id="663">[663] Variational Speculative Decoding: Rethinking Draft Training from Token Likelihood to Sequence Acceptance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05774) (replaced)
