<h3 id="664">[664] Implementing Grassroots Logic Programs with Multiagent Transition Systems and AI (Full Version)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06934) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ehud Shapiro
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LO; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Formal Methods, Logic Programming
- **Summary**: 该论文为Grassroots逻辑程序（GLP）推导出确定性版本dGLP和多智能体确定性版本madGLP，并证明其正确性。madGLP通过异步消息传递实现基层多智能体通信，并形式化地实现了AI驱动的实现方法。

<h3 id="665">[665] Generative Reasoning Re-ranker</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07774) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingfu Liang, Yufei Li, Jay Xu, Kavosh Asadi, Xi Liu, Shuo Gu, Kaushik Rangadurai, Frank Shyu, Shuaiwen Wang, Song Yang, Zhijing Li, Jiang Liu, Mengying Sun, Fei Tian, Xiaohan Wei, Chonglin Sun, Jacob Tao, Shike Mei, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Sandeep Pandey, Hamed Firooz, Luke Simon
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在推荐系统重排序阶段的局限性，提出GR2框架，采用三阶段训练流程：语义ID编码、监督微调推理轨迹、以及DAPO强化学习。在真实数据集上，Recall@5和NDCG@5分别提升2.4%和1.3%。

<h3 id="666">[666] When Benign Inputs Lead to Severe Harms: Eliciting Unsafe Unintended Behaviors of Computer-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08235) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaylen Jones, Zhehao Zhang, Yuting Ning, Eric Fosler-Lussier, Pierre-Luc St-Charles, Yoshua Bengio, Dawn Song, Yu Su, Huan Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Security, AI Safety, Computer-Use Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文首次系统地研究了计算机使用代理（CUA）在良性输入下出现不安全意外行为的问题，提出AutoElicit框架自动触发此类行为。在多个SOTA CUA上发现数百个有害行为，并验证了扰动在不同CUA间的可迁移性。

<h3 id="667">[667] Kunlun: Establishing Scaling Laws for Massive-Scale Recommendation Systems through Unified Architecture Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.10016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10016) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bojian Hou, Xiaolong Liu, Xiaoyi Liu, Jiaqi Xu, Yasmine Badr, Mengyue Hang, Sudhanshu Chanpuriya, Junqing Zhou, Yuhang Yang, Han Xu, Qiuling Suo, Laming Chen, Yuxi Hu, Jiasheng Zhang, Huaqing Xiong, Yuzhen Huang, Chao Chen, Yue Dong, Yi Yang, Shuo Chang, Xiaorui Gan, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Darren Liu, Jade Nie, Chunzhi Yang, Ellie Wen, Jiyan Yang, Huayu Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 针对大规模推荐系统难以获得可预测缩放定律的问题，提出Kunlun架构，通过GDPA、HSP、CompSkip等优化，将MFU从17%提升至37%，缩放效率翻倍。该方法已在Meta Ads模型中部署。

<h3 id="668">[668] Cosmo3DFlow: Wavelet Flow Matching for Spatial-to-Spectral Compression in Reconstructing the Early Universe</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.10172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10172) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Md. Khairul Islam, Zeyu Xia, Ryan Goudjil, Jialu Wang, Arya Farahi, Judy Fox
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, 3D Reconstruction, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 针对宇宙早期结构重建中的高维和稀疏问题，提出Cosmo3DFlow框架，将3D离散小波变换与流匹配结合，实现46倍采样加速。在128^3分辨率的N体模拟中效果显著。

<h3 id="669">[669] Transforming Police-Car Swerving for Mitigating Isolated Stop-and-Go Traffic Waves: A Practice-Oriented Jam-Absorption Driving Strategy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.10234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10234) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhengbing He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Traffic Wave Mitigation, Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对高速公路上的停走交通波问题，受警车摆尾行为启发，提出SD-JAD策略，定义了JAD三角形，通过双检测器参数测量实现波动抑制。SUMO仿真验证了策略有效性。

<h3 id="670">[670] On the Complexity of Offline Reinforcement Learning with $Q^\star$-Approximation and Partial Coverage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.12107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12107) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haolin Liu, Braham Snyder, Chen-Yu Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了离线强化学习中Q*近似和部分覆盖的复杂性，给出了信息论下界，并引入决策-估计框架统一和改进了现有结果。首次分析了CQL在函数逼近下的表现。

<h3 id="671">[671] Know More, Know Clearer: A Meta-Cognitive Framework for Knowledge Augmentation in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.12996](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12996) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hao Chen, Ye He, Yuchun Fan, Yukun Yan, Zhenghao Liu, Qingfu Zhu, Maosong Sun, Wanxiang Che
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Metacognition, Knowledge Augmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AI9Stars/Know-More-Know-Clearer)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM知识增强中忽略知识-置信度差距的问题，提出元认知框架，利用内部认知信号划分知识空间并针对性增强，同时引入认知一致性机制。实验表明，该方法不仅提升知识能力，还使模型更好区分已知与未知。

<h3 id="672">[672] Prescriptive Scaling Reveals the Evolution of Language Model Capabilities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.15327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15327) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanlin Zhang, Jikai Jin, Vasilis Syrgkanis, Sham Kakade
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Scaling Laws, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了规定性缩放定律，通过平滑分位数回归估计预训练计算预算下的下游准确率，并随时间监控能力边界变化。在六个基准上验证了可靠性，并发布了Proteus-2k数据集。

<h3 id="673">[673] Condition-Gated Reasoning for Context-Dependent Biomedical Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17911) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jash Rajesh Parekh, Wonbin Kweon, Joey Chan, Rezarta Islamaj, Robert Leaman, Pengcheng Jiang, Chih-Hsuan Wei, Zhizheng Wang, Zhiyong Lu, Jiawei Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Conditional Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对现有生物医学问答系统未考虑患者特定条件的问题，提出CondMedQA基准和条件门控推理（CGR）框架，通过构建条件感知知识图谱并选择性激活推理路径。实验表明CGR在生物医学QA基准上达到或超越最先进性能，并更可靠地选择条件适应该答案。

<h3 id="674">[674] Training-Free Intelligibility-Guided Observation Addition for Noisy ASR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.20967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20967) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoyang Li, Changsong Liu, Wei Rao, Hao Shi, Sakriani Sakti, Eng Siong Chng
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Speech Enhancement
- **Venue**: Interspeech2026
- **Summary**: 针对语音增强导致ASR识别下降的问题，提出基于可懂度引导的观察相加方法，融合权重来自后端ASR的可懂度估计，无需训练。实验表明该方法在多种SE-ASR组合和数据集上表现鲁棒，优于现有OA基线。

<h3 id="675">[675] Scaling Search Relevance: Augmenting App Store Ranking with LLM-Generated Judgments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23234) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Evangelia Christakopoulou, Vivekkumar Patel, Hemanth Velaga, Sandip Gaikwad, Sean Suchter, Venkat Sundaranatha
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Search Relevance, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对应用商店搜索中专家文本相关性标签稀缺的问题，提出利用LLM生成数百万文本相关性标签，并增强生产排序模型。离线NDCG改进和全球A/B测试表明转化率显著提升+0.24%，尤其在长尾查询中效果显著。

<h3 id="676">[676] A Mixed Diet Makes DINO An Omnivorous Vision Encoder</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.24181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24181) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rishabh Kabra, Maks Ovsjanikov, Drew A. Hudson, Ye Xia, Skanda Koppula, Andre Araujo, Joao Carreira, Niloy J. Mitra
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Foundation Model, Multimodal Learning, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/google-deepmind/representations4d)
- **Summary**: 针对DINOv2等视觉编码器在不同模态特征对齐差的问题，提出后训练框架Omnivorous Vision Encoder，通过双目标微调学习模态无关特征空间。在多种模态上产生更一致的嵌入，保持判别语义，实现跨模态理解。

<h3 id="677">[677] From Conflict to Consensus: Boosting Medical Reasoning via Multi-Round Agentic RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03292) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenhao Wu, Zhentao Tang, Yafu Li, Shixiong Kai, Mingxuan Yuan, Zhenhong Sun, Chunlin Chen, Zhi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Medical AI, RAG, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/NJU-RL/MA-RAG)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在医疗问答中产生幻觉和知识过时的问题，提出多轮智能体RAG框架MA-RAG，通过迭代进化外部证据和内部推理历史，利用语义冲突作为主动检索信号。在7个医疗QA基准上平均准确率提升6.8个百分点。

<h3 id="678">[678] CodeTaste: Can LLMs Generate Human-Level Code Refactorings?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04177) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alex Thillen, Niels Mündler, Veselin Raychev, Martin Vechev
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Software Engineering, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM编码智能体生成的代码存在复杂度和架构债务问题，提出CodeTaste基准，评估智能体执行重构和识别人类重构选择的能力。实验表明智能体在详细指定重构时表现良好，但发现人类重构选择有困难，提出先提议后实现的分解方法可提升对齐。

<h3 id="679">[679] POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.05500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05500) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zeju Qiu, Lixin Liu, Adrian Weller, Han Shi, Weiyang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对POET框架内存消耗和计算开销大的问题，提出POET-X，一种可扩展且内存高效的变体，通过正交等价变换减少计算成本。实验表明POET-X能在单个H100 GPU上预训练十亿参数LLM，而AdamW在相同设置下内存不足。

<h3 id="680">[680] Context Over Compute Human-in-the-Loop Outperforms Iterative Chain-of-Thought Prompting in Interview Answer Quality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.09995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kewen Zhu, Zixi Liu, Yanjing Li, Jing Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, Human-Computer Interaction, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对行为面试评估中结构化评估和真实模拟的需求，对比人机循环与自动思维链改进方法。实验表明人机循环在置信度（3.16→4.16）、真实性（2.94→4.53）方面显著提升，迭代次数更少（1.0 vs 5.0），成功率更高。

<h3 id="681">[681] RetroReasoner: A Reasoning LLM for Strategic Retrosynthesis Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.12666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12666) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanbum Ko, Chanhui Lee, Ye Rin Kim, Rodrigo Hormazabal, Sehui Han, Sungbin Lim, Sungwoong Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Molecular Generation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对逆合成预测中缺乏键断开策略显式推理的问题，提出RetroReasoner，通过监督微调和强化学习训练，生成结构化断开理由和反应物预测，并采用往返奖励。在多步逆合成规划中结合并行化MCTS，减少搜索时间，增加有效合成路径。

<h3 id="682">[682] How Transformers Reject Wrong Answers: Rotational Dynamics of Factual Constraint Processing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.13259](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13259) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Javier Marín
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Transformer Architecture, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对解码器仅Transformer处理正确与错误续接时的内部机制，发现位移向量在中期层旋转分离，后期层产生不对称承诺。该模式在多个模型（1B-13B）上一致，表明事实约束处理是分布式轨迹过程，而非单层定位。

<h3 id="683">[683] CHIMERA-Bench: A Benchmark Dataset for Epitope-Specific Antibody Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.13431](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13431) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mansoor Ahmed, Nadeem Taj, Imdad Ullah Khan, Hemanth Venkateswara, Murray Patterson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Protein Engineering, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 针对计算抗体设计缺乏标准化基准的问题，提出CHIMERA-Bench，包含2922个抗体-抗原复合物数据集，三种生物学动机的划分，以及包括表位特异性指标的综合评估协议。对六种生成范式的11种方法进行基准测试。

<h3 id="684">[684] AgroOmni: A Large-Scale Multi-view Agricultural Dataset for Cross-Scale Multimodal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14342) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiarui Zhang, Junqi Hu, Zurong Mai, Yang Liu, Yuhang Chen, Shuohong Lou, Henglian Huang, Hong Cheng, Lingyuan Zhao, Jianxi Huang, Yutong Lu, Haohuan Fu, Juepeng Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, Multimodal Learning, Dataset
- **Summary**: 针对农业多模态推理中缺乏多视角基准导致尺度混淆的问题，提出AgroOmni大规模多视角训练语料（288K VQA对）和AgroNVILA模型，在AgroMind基准上达到62.32%（+15.03%），有效缓解多视角跨尺度鸿沟。

<h3 id="685">[685] Component Ablation for Efficient Hybrid Language Model Architectures: Performance, Resilience, and Compression Implications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22473) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hector Borobia, Elies Seguí-Mas, Guillermina Tormo-Carbó
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Transformer Architecture, Architecture Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对混合语言模型中注意力机制与线性时间机制的功能贡献未充分表征的问题，通过组件消融实验分析两个子1B混合模型。发现两种组件都重要，但重要性随位置和任务变化，为高效模型设计、压缩和部署提供证据。

<h3 id="686">[686] DecepGPT: Schema-Driven Deception Detection with Multicultural Datasets and Robust Multimodal Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23916) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiajian Huang, Dongliang Zhu, Zitong YU, Hui Ma, Jiayu Zhang, Chunmei Zhu, Xiaochun Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Deception Detection, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态欺骗检测中缺乏推理线索和数据集小的问题，提出结构化线索级描述和推理链，发布大型多文化数据集T4-Deception（1695样本），并提出SICS和DMC模块实现鲁棒学习。在多个基准上取得最先进性能，并展现跨文化迁移性。

<h3 id="687">[687] Vision Hopfield Memory Networks for Image Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.25157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25157) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jianfeng Wang, Amine M&#39;Charrak, Luk Koska, Xiangtao Wang, Daniel Petriceanu, Ruizhi Wang, Michael Bumbar, Luca Pinchetti, Thomas Lukasiewicz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Image Classification, Memory Architecture, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对现有视觉骨干网络缺乏人脑计算原理和可解释性的问题，提出视觉Hopfield记忆网络（V-HMN），集成局部和全局Hopfield模块以及预测编码启发式细化规则。在小型和中型基准上表现强，ImageNet上具竞争力，且提供基于原型的可解释性和数据效率。

<h3 id="688">[688] Train at Moving Edge: Online-Verified Prompt Selection for Efficient RL Training of Large Reasoning Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.25184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25184) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Wu, Ning Lu, Shengcai Liu, Kun Wang, Yanting Yang, Bailong Lin, Chen Jason Zhang, Li Qing, Ke Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习训练大语言模型时多rollout的高计算开销，提出HIVE（历史信息和在线验证的提示选择）框架，利用历史奖励轨迹和提示熵选择高效用提示。在多个数学推理基准上显著提高rollout效率而不牺牲性能。

<h3 id="689">[689] UnWeaving the knots of GraphRAG -- turns out VectorRAG is almost enough</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.29875](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29875) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ryszard Tuora, Mateusz Galiński, Michał Godziszewski, Michał Karpowicz, Mateusz Czyżnikiewicz, Adam Kozakiewicz, Tomasz Ziętkiewicz
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 问题：基于块的检索增强生成（RAG）系统处理多跳问题时缺乏机制，而图RAG系统虽以知识图谱建模但存在组件复杂度高和依赖启发式检索的问题。方法：提出UnWeaver框架，通过LLM将文档内容分解为跨多个块的实体，检索时以实体为中间表示恢复原文。结果：实验表明VectorRAG在端到端QA中性能优于标准图RAG，且达到当前图方法最佳效果，成本更低。

<h3 id="690">[690] Automated Framework to Evaluate and Harden LLM System Instructions against Encoding Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.01039](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01039) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anubhab Sahu, Diptisha Samanta, Reza Soosahabi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM系统指令可能含敏感信息，现有基于拒绝的指令无法防御通过编码或结构化输出任务的提取攻击。方法：提出自动化评估框架测试系统指令保密性，并展示一种基于思维链推理模型的一次性指令重塑缓解策略。结果：在4个常见模型和46个系统指令上结构化攻击成功率\>0.7，所提缓解策略可显著降低成功率。

<h3 id="691">[691] Distributional Open-Ended Evaluation of LLM Cultural Value Alignment Based on Value Codebook</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.06210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06210) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaehyeok Lee, Xiaoyuan Yi, Jing Yao, Hyunjin Hwang, Roy Ka-Wei Lee, Xing Xie, JinYeong Bak
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Evaluation, Cultural Alignment
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：现有LLM文化价值对齐基准面临构建-组合-上下文挑战，依赖判别式多选题评估价值知识而非真实取向，且忽略子文化异质性。方法：提出DOVE分布评估框架，利用率失真变分优化构建价值码本，通过不平衡最优传输衡量对齐。结果：在12个LLM上DOVE与下游任务相关性达31.56%，仅需500样本即可保持高可靠性。

<h3 id="692">[692] Securing Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Taxonomy of Attacks, Defenses, and Future Directions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.08304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08304) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuming Xu, Mingtao Zhang, Zhuohan Ge, Haoyang Li, Nicole Hu, Yongqi Zhang, Zhiyuan Wen, Jason Chen Zhang, Qing Li, Lei Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Security, Survey
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TreeAI-Lab/Awesome-RAG-Security)
- **Summary**: 问题：检索增强生成（RAG）引入外部知识时带来安全风险，现有工作常混淆于LLM固有缺陷。方法：提出SLOT分类法，沿攻击面、防御层、目标与对象四轴组织文献，映射到六阶段知识访问管道。结果：揭示结构不匹配问题，并讨论鲁棒防御、多模态及智能体RAG等方向。

<h3 id="693">[693] SatIR: Scalable High-Recall Constraint-Satisfaction-Based Information Retrieval for Clinical Trials Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.08849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08849) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cyrus Zhou, Yufei Jin, Yilin Xu, Yu-Chiang Wang, Chieh-Ju Chao, Monica S. Lam
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.MA; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Medical AI, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 问题：许多检索问题是约束满足问题（如临床试验匹配需满足复杂资格标准），而非单纯语义相似性。方法：提出SatIR方法，结合可满足性模理论、关系代数、医学本体和LLM，将试验标准转换为形式约束进行检索。结果：在SIGIR2016和TREC-2022上，SatIR召回率比基线高32%-72%和1.8-3.2倍，每患者检索仅146毫秒。

<h3 id="694">[694] Muon$^2$: Boosting Muon via Adaptive Second-Moment Preconditioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.09967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09967) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyue Liu, Ruijie Zhang, Zhengyang Wang, Yequan Zhao, Yupeng Su, Zi Yang, Zheng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Pre-training, Training Efficiency
- **Summary**: 问题：Muon优化器在预训练中通过迭代正交化利用矩阵结构，但正交化质量依赖牛顿-舒尔茨迭代次数，计算与通信成本高。方法：提出Muon²，在正交化前应用Adam风格自适应二阶矩预条件，改善动量矩阵条件数。结果：在GPT、LLaMA等预训练中，Muon²减少40%迭代次数，一致优于Muon，节省最多1/4训练时间。

<h3 id="695">[695] Resilient Write: A Six-Layer Durable Write Surface for LLM Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10842) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Justice Owusu Agyemang, Jerry John Kponyo, Elliot Amponsah, Godfred Manu Addo Boakye, Kwame Opuni-Boachie Obour Agyekum
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Engineering, Error Recovery
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM编码智能体依赖工具写入文件时，写入失败（如内容过滤、截断）常无结构化信号，导致重试浪费。方法：提出Resilient Write MCP服务器，在智能体和文件系统间插入六层持久写入层，包括预检风险评分、事务性原子写入等。结果：测试显示恢复时间减少5倍，自我纠正率提升13倍。

<h3 id="696">[696] Capacity-Controlled Global Attention for Graph Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17324) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Liu, Dongxin Guo, Tom Zheng, Siu Ming Yiu, Liam Ning, Jikun Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 问题：图Transformer中softmax注意力导致节点表示随深度坍塌（过平滑）、每头输出低秩瓶颈和优化脆弱。方法：提出SigGate-GT，在GraphGPS框架中对注意力输出应用可学习sigmoid门控，放松注意力守恒约束。结果：在ZINC等五个基准上达到最佳或竞争结果，过平滑减少30%，训练稳定性提升。

<h3 id="697">[697] Multilingual Training and Evaluation Resources for Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18347](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18347) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daniela Baiamonte, Elena Fano, Matteo Gabburo, Stefano Simonazzi, Leonardo Rigutini, Andrea Zugarini
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multilingual Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉语言模型发展以英语为核心，缺乏多语言多模态训练数据和评测基准。方法：采用再生-翻译范式构建多语言训练语料Multi-PixMo及翻译评测基准。结果：通过人类评估和消融实验证明多语言数据训练对非英语和英语基准均有正向效果。

<h3 id="698">[698] Watts-per-Intelligence Part II: Algorithmic Catalysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.20897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20897) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Elija Perrier
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Theory, Algorithm Design, Complexity Theory
- **Venue**: AGI-2026
- **Summary**: 问题：智能计算中可重用结构如何减少任务类别的不可逆操作，并受热力学约束。方法：提出算法催化的热力学理论，证明类别特定加速受算法互信息上界约束，编码该信息需Landauer擦除最小热力学成本。结果：导出部署时间下界，并在仿射SAT类上验证。

<h3 id="699">[699] Knee-xRAI: An Explainable AI Framework for Automatic Kellgren-Lawrence Grading of Knee Osteoarthritis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23435) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Azmul A. Irfan, Nur Ahmad Khatim, Alfan Alfian Irfan, Achmad Zaki, Erike A. Suwarsono, Mansur M. Arief
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Explainable AI, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 问题：膝关节骨关节炎KL分级可重复性差，深度学习模型虽性能优异但缺乏可解释性。方法：提出Knee-xRAI流水线，模拟临床工作流独立测量关节间隙狭窄、骨赘和硬化，组合为可解释KL等级。结果：在8260张放射片上，JSN模块Dice=0.8909，Path B达QWK=0.8436、AUC=0.9017，SHAP分析显示JSN为主特征。

<h3 id="700">[700] Speech Enhancement Based on Drifting Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.24199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24199) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liang Xu, Diego Caviedes-Nozal, W. Bastiaan Kleijn, Longfei Felix Yan, Rasmus Kongsgaard Olsson
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Speech Enhancement, Diffusion Model, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 问题：传统语音增强依赖迭代采样，计算成本高。方法：提出DriftSE生成框架，将去噪建模为平衡问题，通过漂移场一步推理匹配干净语音分布，支持非配对数据训练。结果：在VoiceBank-DEMAND基准上，单步增强优于多步扩散基线。

<h3 id="701">[701] RAS: a Reliability Oriented Metric for Automatic Speech Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.24278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24278) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenbin Huang, Yuhang Qiu, Bohan Li, Yiwei Guo, Jing Peng, Hankun Wang, Xie Chen, Kai Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Uncertainty Estimation, Model Evaluation
- **Venue**: InterSpeech 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：ASR系统在噪声下常产生自信但错误的转录，标准WER指标无法捕获可靠性。方法：提出放弃感知转录框架，允许模型对不确定段放弃，引入可靠性指标RAS，通过强化学习训练放弃感知模型。结果：在保持竞争性准确率的同时，显著提高转录可靠性。

<h3 id="702">[702] Skill Retrieval Augmentation for Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.24594](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24594) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weihang Su, Jianming Long, Qingyao Ai, Qiaozhi He, Yichen Tang, Changyue Wang, Yiteng Tu, Yingbo Wang, Yiqun Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Skill Retrieval
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM智能体依赖显式列举技能的方式难以扩展，技能库增大导致上下文预算耗尽且识别准确率下降。方法：提出技能检索增强范式，动态检索外部技能，并构建SRA-Bench基准（5400测试实例、636黄金技能）。结果：检索增强显著提升性能，但发现技能融合存在瓶颈，模型对是否需要加载技能的决定不敏感。

<h3 id="703">[703] Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michael Cardei, Huu Binh Ta, Ferdinando Fioretto
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Self-Conditioning
- **Summary**: 问题：标准掩码扩散模型中仍掩码位置必须重复从掩码标记推断，限制跨步细化。方法：提出后训练适应方法SCMDM，每步以模型自身先前干净预测为条件，无需额外训练或参考模型。结果：在多个领域改进基线，生成困惑度降低约50%（42.89→23.72），图像、分子和基因组分布建模均有提升。

<h3 id="704">[704] When Do Diffusion Models learn to Generate Multiple Objects?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00273](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00273) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yujin Jeong, Arnas Uselis, Iro Laina, Seong Joon Oh, Anna Rohrbach
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Compositional Generalization
- **Summary**: 问题：文本到图像扩散模型在多对象生成中不可靠，但原因不明。方法：通过控制数据集研究概念泛化与组合泛化，引入Mosaic框架。结果：场景复杂性起主导作用而非概念不平衡，计数在低数据条件下特别困难，组合泛化随概念组合缺失而崩溃。

<h3 id="705">[705] DynamicPO: Dynamic Preference Optimization for Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00327) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Hu, Kai Zhang, Jiancan Wu, Shuli Wang, Chi Wang, Wenshuai Chen, Yinhua Zhu, Haitao Wang, Xingxing Wang, Xiang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Preference Optimization, Recommender System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xingyuHuxingyu/DynamicPO)
- **Summary**: 该论文发现基于大语言模型的推荐系统中，直接偏好优化在使用多负样本时会出现“偏好优化崩溃”现象，即增加负样本数量导致性能下降。提出DynamicPO框架，包含动态边界负样本选择和双边缘动态beta调整机制，有效缓解梯度抑制。实验表明DynamicPO能防止优化崩溃并提升推荐准确率，计算开销可忽略。

<h3 id="706">[706] Learning Behavioral Signals from Encrypted Smartphone Network Traffic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01616) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rameen Mahmood, Omar El Shahawy, Souptik Barua, Zachary Beattie, Jeffrey Kaye, Xuhai &#34;Orson&#39;&#39; Xu, Chao-Yi Wu, Danny Yuxing Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Network Traffic Analysis, Mental Health, Behavioral Modeling
- **Summary**: 该论文研究是否可从加密智能手机网络流量中被动感知睡眠障碍、压力和孤独等行为状态。使用Transformer模型和用户特定适配器学习网络活动表示，并通过稀疏表示学习解释。分析发现三种状态具有不同时间动态：压力主要与个体间差异相关，孤独更多与个体内波动相关。学习到的表示优于传统手工特征。

<h3 id="707">[707] Kernel Affine Hull Machines as Compute-Efficient Encoders for Frozen Semantic Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02950) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohit Kumar, Somayeh Kargaran, Bernhard A. Moser, Manuela Geiß
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Kernel Method
- **Summary**: 该论文研究在固定教师表示空间和语料索引下，能否用轻量估计器替代神经查询编码。提出核仿射包机（KAHM）作为后向传播免费的编码器，从廉价词汇特征估计语义向量。在奥地利法律检索基准上，KAHM在重建和检索性能上最优，在线查询时间减少8.53倍。

<h3 id="708">[708] Neuron-Anchored Rule Extraction for Large Language Models via Contrastive Hierarchical Ablation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03058) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Francesco Sovrano, Gabriele Dominici, Marc Langheinrich
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Safety, Rule Extraction
- **Venue**: KDD'2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出MechaRule管道，通过定位稀疏激活的神经元来提取大语言模型中的规则，这些神经元的消融会破坏规则相关行为。基于自适应组测试，在算术和越狱任务上以仅2.14%的穷举消融成本召回97%的高效应神经元，消融后规则相关行为消除率达97.6-100%。

<h3 id="709">[709] Self-Mined Hardness for Safety Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03226) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Prakhar Gupta, Garv Shah, Donghua Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Jailbreak Defense, Safety Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出自我挖掘难度的方法用于语言模型安全微调：根据模型自身生成被判定为有害的频率来评分提示难度，然后在最难的提示上微调。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上将WildJailbreak攻击成功率从11.5%降至1-3%，但导致对类似越狱的良性提示拒绝率上升，通过混合良性提示可平衡。

<h3 id="710">[710] APEX: Large-scale Multi-task Aesthetic-Informed Popularity Prediction for AI-Generated Music</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.03395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03395) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaavid Aktar Husain, Dorien Herremans
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Music Generation, AI-Generated Content, Aesthetic Assessment
- **Summary**: 该论文针对AI生成音乐平台，提出APEX，第一个大规模多任务学习框架，在21.1万首歌曲上联合预测基于参与度的流行度信号和五维感知美学质量。在Music Arena数据集上，加入美学特征持续提升偏好预测性能，展现了跨生成架构的泛化能力。

<h3 id="711">[711] NavOne: One-Step Global Planning for Vision-Language Navigation on Top-Down Maps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06317) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dijia Zhan, Jinyi Li, Chenxi Zheng, Shaoyu Huang, Yong Li, Jie Tang, Xuemiao Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Navigation, Visual Navigation, Path Planning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有视觉语言导航方法逐步决策导致错误累积和效率低的问题，提出Top-Down VLN框架，将导航重构为在预构建俯视图上的单步全局路径规划。NavOne直接预测密集路径概率，在R2R-TopDown上达到最优，规划速度比基于地图的方法快8倍，比自回归方法快80倍。

<h3 id="712">[712] MinMax Recurrent Neural Cascades</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06384) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alessandro Ronca
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.FL
- **Tags**: Recurrent Neural Network, Sequence Modeling, MinMax RNN
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/minmaxrnc/model)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出MinMax循环神经级联（MinMax RNC），基于MinMax代数的新型循环神经网络。具有强形式表达能力（对应正则语言）、高效评估（可并行扫描）、稳定动态和非消失状态梯度。在合成任务上完美解决并泛化到长序列，在1.12亿参数语言模型上取得有竞争力性能。

<h3 id="713">[713] From Detection to Recovery: Operational Analysis on LLM Pre-training with 504 GPUs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09370) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daemyung Kang, Eunjin Hwang, Hanjeong Lee, HyeokJin Kim, Hyunhoi Koo, Jeongkyu Shin, Jeongseok Kang, Jihyun Kang, Joongi Kim, Junbum Lee, Jungseung Yang, Kyujin Cho, Youngsook Song
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Distributed Training, Fault Tolerance
- **Summary**: 该论文对63节点NVIDIA B200生产集群（504 GPU）进行实证分析，利用55天时间序列数据和73天操作日志。发现跨组织环境的存储I/O瓶颈，并进行了GPU故障检测（10/10检出率）、检查点事件剖析（揭示带宽悖论）以及多节点故障恢复分析（自动重试成功率33.3%）。

<h3 id="714">[714] CalBench: Evaluating Coordination-Privacy Trade-offs in Multi-Agent LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09823) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chelsea Zou, Yiheng Yao, Selena She, Noah Goodman, Robert D. Hawkins
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文针对个人AI助手在日程协调中需平衡协调与隐私的问题，提出CalBench基准，用于评估多代理系统在隐私信息下的日程安排。基准使用CP-SAT最优解和参考协议，评估任务成功、额外成本、通信效率、公平性和隐私泄露。实验发现完成率无法揭示重要失败，通信量与遗憾无关。

<h3 id="715">[715] Quantifying Rodda and Graham Gait Classification from 3D Markerless Kinematics derived from a Single-view Video in a Heterogeneous Pediatric Clinical Cohort</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11314](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11314) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lauhitya Reddy, Seth Donahue, Jeremy Bauer, Susan Sienko, Anita Bagley, Joseph Krzak, Maura Eveld, Karen Kruger, Ross Chafetz, Vedant Kulkarni, Hyeokhyen Kwon
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Gait Analysis, Markerless Motion Capture
- **Summary**: 该论文针对脑瘫儿童步态评估中三维仪器步态分析昂贵且受限的问题，开发无标记步态分析管道，从单视角临床视频量化Rodda和Graham分类系统的膝关节和踝关节z分数。在152名儿童数据上，膝关节z分数R²=0.80，踝关节z分数R²=0.57，二分类筛查AUROC=0.88。

<h3 id="716">[716] Improving the Performance and Learning Stability of Parallelizable RNNs Designed for Ultra-Low Power Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11855](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11855) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Julien Brandoit, Arthur Fyon, Damien Ernst, Guillaume Drion
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.AR
- **Tags**: Recurrent Neural Network, Energy Efficiency, Hardware-Software Co-design
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对超低功耗应用的BMRU在复杂任务上性能落后，发现梯度阻塞问题并提出累积更新公式，导出CMRU。CMRU在保持量化状态和噪声鲁棒性同时，在小模型尺寸下匹配或超越LRU和minGRU，具有更好收敛稳定性。

<h3 id="717">[717] High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Or Ordentlich, Yury Polyanskiy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 该论文研究利用协方差矩阵进行加权均方误差源编码的量化矩阵乘法，应用于LLM权重后训练量化。展示水填充法改进GPTQ，分析WaterSIC方案的高速率性能，证明其与信息论失真限差距在0.25 bit/项以内，且GPTQ在随机旋转下接近最优。

<h3 id="718">[718] Margin-Adaptive Confidence Ranking for Reliable LLM Judgement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15416) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gaojie Jin, Yong Tao, Lijia Yu, Tianjin Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM-as-a-Judge, Confidence Calibration
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM作为判断者时启发式置信度与不一致风险单调性可能被违反的问题，提出学习专门的置信度估计器。利用模拟标注者多样性和边际排序，显式建模置信度。在多个数据集上提高排序准确率并强化单调关系，提升满足目标一致水平的成功率。

<h3 id="719">[719] Ghosted Layers: Unconstrained Activation Alignment for Recovering Layer-Pruned LLMs</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PF

- **Summary**: 该论文针对层剪枝后后续存活层隐藏状态分布不匹配导致性能下降的问题，提出Ghosted Layers，一个无需训练的重建模块，通过闭式最优线性算子填补剪枝层引起的激活差异。在多个LLM和剪枝策略上持续优于无训练基线，保持剪枝效率。

<h3 id="720">[720] Evaluating Design Video Generation: Metrics for Compositional Fidelity</h3>

<h3 id="721">[721] Full Attention Strikes Back: Transferring Full Attention into Sparse within Hundred Training Steps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.16928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16928) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanke Zhou, Yiduo Li, Hanlin Tang, Maohua Li, Kan Liu, Tao Lan, Lin Qu, Yuan Yao, Xiaoxing Ma

<h3 id="722">[722] WhiteTesseract: Reframing the Interpretation of Cultural Heritage through XR and Conversational AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.16972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16972) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对文化遗产展览缺乏适应性和参与度问题，提出WhiteTesseract系统，结合XR和对话AI实现现场解读。用户研究显示平均观看时长从35.3秒增至98.3秒，60%的交互超越事实性查询。

<h3 id="723">[723] LEAP: Learnable End-to-End Adaptive Pruning of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17289](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17289) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对非结构化LLM剪枝中逐层方法精度损失问题，提出LEAP框架，通过每权重的Bernoulli-Gumbel-sigmoid松弛实现端到端可学习剪枝。在50%和60%稀疏度下，六任务平均零样本准确率比ADMM提高2.59个百分点。

<h3 id="724">[724] ConflictRAG: Detecting and Resolving Knowledge Conflicts in Retrieval Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17301](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17301) (replaced)

- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Conflict
- **Summary**: 针对RAG系统中检索文档间的知识冲突问题，提出ConflictRAG框架，包含两阶段冲突检测、Entropy-TOPSIS源可信度评估和CARS诊断指标。冲突检测F1达88.7%，正确率比最强基线提高5.3-6.1%，API成本降低62%。

<h3 id="725">[725] Post-Trained MoE Can Skip Half Experts via Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18643](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18643) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingtai Lv, Li Sheng, Kaiyan Zhang, Yichen You, Siyan Gao, Xueheng Luo, Yuxin Zuo, Yuchen Fan, Junlin Yang, Ganqu Cui, Bingning Wang, Fan Yang, Youbang Sun, Ning Ding, Bowen Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Self-Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对训练后静态MoE模型推理成本高问题，提出ZEDA框架，通过零专家注入和两阶段自蒸馏将静态MoE转换为动态MoE。消除超过50%专家FLOPs，精度损失微小，在两种模型上分别比最强动态MoE基线高6.1和4.0分，实现约1.20倍端到端加速。

<h3 id="726">[726] Diagnosing Multi-step Reasoning Failures in Black-box LLMs via Stepwise Confidence Attribution</h3>

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对黑盒LLM多步推理失败诊断困难问题，提出SCA框架，利用信息瓶颈原理分配步级置信度。能可靠识别低置信度步骤，基于步级置信度的自纠正使纠正成功率比回答级反馈提高13.5%。

<h3 id="727">[727] FormalASR: End-to-End Spoken Chinese to Formal Text</h3>

<h3 id="728">[728] Causal Unlearning in Collaborative Optimization: Exact and Approximate Influence Reversal under Adversarial Contributions</h3>

<h3 id="729">[729] DySink: Dynamic Frame Sinks for Autoregressive Long Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.21028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21028) (replaced)

<h3 id="730">[730] CrossVLA: Cross-Paradigm Post-Training and Inference Optimization for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对连续动作视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中偏好对齐缺失问题，提出CrossVLA，包含连续动作流匹配DPO估计器、LoRA与DoRA比较及推理时分析。DoRA在LIBERO套件上平均优于OpenVLA SFT 10.4个百分点，目标套件零方差。

<h3 id="731">[731] DeltaBox: Scaling Stateful AI Agents with Millisecond-Level Sandbox Checkpoint/Rollback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22781](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22781) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对AI智能体沙盒检查点/回滚延迟高问题，提出DeltaBox，通过DeltaFS（基于变化的文件系统C/R）和DeltaCR（基于变化的进程状态C/R）实现毫秒级C/R。检查点14ms、回滚5ms，支持固定时间预算内探索更多节点。

<h3 id="732">[732] Learning to Evaluate: Cost-Effective Model Evaluation on Unlabeled Data with Meta-Learning</h3>

<h3 id="733">[733] How Many Tools Should an LLM Agent See? A Chance-Corrected Answer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24660) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vyzantinos Repantis, Ameya Gawde, Harshvardhan Singh, Joey Blackwell II
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体显示工具数量选择问题，提出基于Bits-over-Random（BoR）的度量及RL自适应选择策略。在BFCL上，学习策略平均仅显示7个工具即达到显示50个工具的覆盖率（90.3% vs 90.8%），下游工具选择准确率提升至93.1%。

<h3 id="734">[734] Riemannian-Manifold Steering: Geometry-Aware Generative Autoencoders for Label-Free Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24942](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24942) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对现有流形引导方法需要标注质心问题，提出基于黎曼测地线计算的激活引导框架，使用在概念-标记模式上训练的编码器。在标准任务上可靠驱动模型进入目标类，轨迹更自然。

<h3 id="735">[735] Polynomial Context-Truncation Sensitivity in Autoregressive Language Models: Sequential Wyner-Ziv Bounds for KV Cache Compression</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对自回归语言模型在线KV缓存压缩的率失真极限问题，建模为顺序Wyner-Ziv信源编码，发现上下文截断敏感性呈多项式衰减。经验验证滑动窗口方案可达O(ε^{-1/α})失真，并在四个模型上验证。

<h3 id="736">[736] The Strongest Teacher Is Not Always the Best Teacher: Student-Centric Answer Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26872) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhengyu Hu, Zheyuan Xiao, Linxin Song, Fengqing Jiang, Yuetai Li, Zhengyu Chen, Zhihan Xiong, Yue Liu, Junhao Lin, Yao Su, Lijie Hu, Kaize Ding, Teng Xiao, Radha Poovendran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Data Selection, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 针对最强教师未必是最佳教师监督假设问题，提出学生中心答案采样（SCAS），根据估计的学生中心学习成本从验证过的教师答案中选择。在30个教师、6个学生模型和6个任务上持续提升学生性能。

<h3 id="737">[737] Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vincent-Daniel Yun, Youngrae Kim, Woosang Lim, YoungJin Heo, Minkyu Kim, Sunwoo Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Pruning, LLM Inference

- **Summary**: 针对现有一次性剪枝方法依赖局部层重要性或固定冗余假设的问题，提出LoRP（位置感知冗余剪枝），一种无训练的一次性深度剪枝框架，通过表示位置性得分（RLS）量化层间相似性并聚类剪枝。实验表明，该方法在多个LLM家族上提升了困惑度和下游任务准确率。

<h3 id="738">[738] Pruning and Distilling Mixture-of-Experts into Dense Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28207) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junhyuck Kim, Jihun Yun, Haechan Kim, Gyeongman Kim, Joonghyun Bae, Jaewoong Cho
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Knowledge Distillation, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 针对MoE模型内存占用大的部署问题，提出首个将训练好的MoE转换为标准密集架构的系统框架，通过专家评分、选择、分组拼接及知识蒸馏实现。实验表明，多样性感知评分方法最优，在相同参数量下，MoE转密集相比密集剪枝平均下游准确率提升6.3个百分点，蒸馏速度快1.6倍。

<h3 id="739">[739] Efficient and Scalable Provenance Tracking for LLM-Generated Code Snippets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrea Gurioli, Davide D&#39;Ascenzo, Federico Pennino, Maurizio Gabbrielli, Stefano Zacchiroli
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Code Search, Code Provenance
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成代码可能逐字复现训练样例而缺乏归属标注的问题，提出SOURCETRACKER编码器及混合两阶段溯源管道HST，结合向量搜索与指纹匹配。在10万片段搜索空间中，混合方法对30词元片段达到与Winnowing相当的MRR，对≥60词元片段性能提升达5.4%，且保持对数级查询复杂度。

<h3 id="740">[740] S3Mem: Structured Spatiotemporal Scene-Event Memory for Long-Horizon Interactive Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28831) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Encheng Su, Jianyu Wu, Jinouwen Zhang, Qiucheng Yu, Chen Tang, Pengze Li, Lintao Wang, Aoran Wang, Xinzhu Ma, Shixiang Tang, Yizhou Wang, Houqiang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Memory Architecture, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对长时记忆问答中稀疏异构证据选择困难的问题，提出S3Mem，一种查询时记忆接口，将文本、视觉和代理使用历史写入结构化场景-事件单元，并通过路由选择紧凑证据包。在LoCoMo上，使用每问题约1073个证据词元达到0.48 F1，比参考少15.8倍，在EMemBench可视化游戏中以189词元获得最佳F1。

<h3 id="741">[741] Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28860](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jeanmely Rojas Nunez, Viraj Sawant, Nathan Allen, Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar, Yannis Zongo, Vasu Sharma, Maheep Chaudhary
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Reinforcement Learning, Catastrophic Forgetting
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability)
- **Summary**: 针对微调导致灾难性遗忘的问题，从机制层面引入差分电路脆弱性指标，比较RL与SFT对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct内部电路的影响。发现SFT适应更快但造成更大电路破坏和遗忘，RL保留更多基础电路但适应较慢。

<h3 id="742">[742] MOOSE-Copilot: A Web-Based Interactive Assistant for Unified Exploratory and Fine-Grained Scientific Hypothesis Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29475) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongran An, Zonglin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Human-Computer Interaction, Hypothesis Generation
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 (System Demonstrations)
- **Summary**: 针对现有LLM假设发现中探索与精化隔离且缺乏人工引导的问题，提出MOOSE-Copilot统一框架，通过初始蓝图、阶段间路由和阶段内反馈三种信号实现人机交互。实验表明结构化信号显著优于纯自主基线，并提供无代码网页界面。

<h3 id="743">[743] BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Reinforcement Learning, Dexterous Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对灵巧操作中VLA策略的高维控制与执行误差问题，提出BORA，一种离线到在线RL后训练框架。离线阶段构建动作条件值指导，在线阶段冻结VLA基础并引入人机协同的块级残差适应。在五项灵巧任务上，平均成功率绝对提升33%，未见物体泛化提升43%。

<h3 id="744">[744] Exploring Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering for Model Specialization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30407](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30407) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yujie Luo, Xiangyuan Ru, Jingsheng Zheng, Jingjing Wang, Yuqi Zhu, Jintian Zhang, Runnan Fang, Kewei Xu, Ye Liu, Zheng Wei, Jiang Bian, Zang Li, Shumin Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Data Synthesis, Model Specialization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/DataAgent)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在专业领域缺乏高质量数据的问题，形式化自主智能体数据工程任务，让LLM自主规划、生成并迭代优化训练数据。实验表明，GPT-5.2构建的训练课程使学生模型性能提升57.29%，完全通过智能体驱动的数据适应实现。

<h3 id="745">[745] Diffusion Image Generation with Explicit Modeling of Data Manifold Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00094](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00094) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Duoduo Xue, Zhiyu Zhu, Junhui Hou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Generation, Manifold Learning
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型缺乏显式数据流形几何建模的问题，提出MIND框架，将离散块标记化集成到连续扩散得分函数中，并引入软top-k聚合和双分支高频嵌入。在ImageNet 256×256上，基础模型80轮训练FID为22.73（相比DiT-B/2的43.47），130M参数的MIND-B在引导下FID达2.06，超越3.1B参数的LlamaGen-3B。

<h3 id="746">[746] Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00229) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yueh-Hua Wu, Tatsuya Matsushima, Kei Ota
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Continuous Control, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对自然语言推理与VLA连续控制粒度不匹配的问题，提出连续推理方法，预测结构化连续思维作为共享上下文用于动作生成。通过共享高斯潜在接口和自验证目标训练。在真实机器人上，平均子任务成功率相比π0.5提升40.4%和26.3%。

<h3 id="747">[747] Beyond Independent Manipulation: Individual Fairness-aware Strategic Classification with Peer Imitation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00827) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Lv, Chunyuan Zheng, Yunxin Mao, Renzhe Xu, Jinxuan Yang, Yuanlong Chen, Wangrong Huang, Shaowu Yang, Wenjing Yang, Xinwang Liu, Peng Cui, Haotian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Individual Fairness, Strategic Classification
- **Venue**: SIGKDD2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有公平感知策略分类中假设代理独立操纵而忽略个体公平性问题，提出IFSC框架，建模基于同伴模仿的驱动操纵。通过随机扰动鲁棒学习处理不确定性。实验表明，IFSC提升了个体公平一致性并减少模仿引起的失真。

<h3 id="748">[748] MENTIS: What Belief Changes Under Alignment? Measuring Multi-Scale Latent Torsion in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01060](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01060) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Partha Pratim Saha, Samarth Raina, Mayur Parvatikar, Amit Dhanda, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha, Amitava Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Mechanistic Interpretability, Representation Geometry
- **Summary**: 针对偏好对齐后内部机制变化不明的问题，提出MENTIS几何优先框架，通过协方差扭转范数、谱扭转诊断和ERA定位测量对齐引起的内部重组。发现规范性概念扭转变化大于事实性概念，扭转与上下文熵负相关，峰值位于中后层。

<h3 id="749">[749] Crazyflow: An Accurate, GPU-Accelerated, Differentiable Drone Simulator in JAX</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01478) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Martin Schuck, Marcel P. Rath, Yufei Hua, Abhishek Goudar, SiQi Zhou, Angela P. Schoellig
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.MA; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Simulation, Robotics, Differentiable Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对空中机器人模拟器无法兼顾保真度、可微性和集群的问题，提出Crazyflow，基于JAX的GPU加速可微无人机模拟器。单机速度比现有最优快一个数量级，可模拟数千个4000架无人机集群。支持解析梯度策略学习（亚厘米精度）和飞行中RL训练（0.38秒内从零学习恢复策略）。

<h3 id="750">[750] Defenses & Enablers For Skill Injection Attacks on Terminal Based Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01567) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yoshinari Fujinuma, Varun Gangal, Traian Rebedea, Makesh Narsimhan Sreedhar, Prasoon Varshney, Rebecca Qian, Anand Kannappan
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Agent Safety
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理使用可复用技能带来的新攻击面，评估基于守卫的防御机制。动态守卫（中介LLM）和静态守卫（预重写）在保持任务效用的同时将攻击成功率降低一半以上。攻击重定向使无守卫场景ASR升至81.4%，但动态守卫降至18.6%。

<h3 id="751">[751] Easier to Mislead Than to Correct: Harmful and Beneficial Revision in LLM Conformity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01637](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01637) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaming Qu, Lucheng Fu, Yibo Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Conformity
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体系统中LLM从众现象，研究同伴一致性和权威标签对有益/有害修订的影响。发现同伴一致性更易误导原本正确的模型而非纠正错误模型，权威标签增加对答案的采纳不论对错。通用推理干预不能可靠减少有害修订。

<h3 id="752">[752] Argument Collapse: LLMs Flatten Long-Form Public Debate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01736) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yekyung Kim, Yapei Chang, Chau Minh Pham, Mohit Iyyer
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Text Generation, Argument Collapse
- **Summary**: 针对LLM起草论证可能扁平化公共辩论的问题，研究论证坍缩现象。比较人类与LLM生成辩论文章，发现65.3%的人类主论点在辩论中唯一，而LLM仅3.4%。LLM仅恢复约一半的人类独特主论点，且子论点多样性更低，结构更固定。

<h3 id="753">[753] See Less, Specify More: Visual Evidence Budgets for Generalizable VLAs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02735](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02735) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yueh-Hua Wu, Tatsuya Matsushima, Kei Ota
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在干扰物和外观变化下泛化能力差的问题，提出S2框架，通过视觉证据预算和细化语言指令来改进执行器学习。在多个真实机器人任务上，平均子任务成功率从54.2%提升至79.0%。

<h3 id="754">[754] Cosmos 3: Omnimodal World Models for Physical AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02800](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02800) (replaced)
- **Authors**: NVIDIA, Aditi, Niket Agarwal, Arslan Ali, Jon Allen, Martin Antolini, Adeline Aubame, Alisson Azzolini, Junjie Bai, Maciej Bala, Yogesh Balaji, Josh Bapst, Aarti Basant, Mukesh Beladiya, Mohammad Qazim Bhat, Zaid Pervaiz Bhat, Dan Blick, Vanni Brighella, Han Cai, Tiffany Cai, Eric Cameracci, Jiaxin Cao, Yulong Cao, Mark Carlson, Carlos Casanova, Ting-Yun Chang, Yan Chang, Yu-Wei Chao, Prithvijit Chattopadhyay, Roshan Chaudhari, Chieh-Yun Chen, Junyu Chen, Ke Chen, Qizhi Chen, Wenkai Chen, Xiaotong Chen, Yu Chen, An-Chieh Cheng, Click Cheng, Xiu Chia, Jeana Choi, Chaeyeon Chung, Wenyan Cong, Yin Cui, Magdalena Dadela, Nalin Dadhich, Wenliang Dai, Joyjit Daw, Alperen Degirmenci, Rodrigo Vieira Del Monte, Robert Denomme, Sameer Dharur, Marco Di Lucca, Ke Ding, Wenhao Ding, Yifan Ding, Yuzhu Dong, Nicole Drumheller, Yilun Du, Aigul Dzhumamuratova, Aleksandr Efitorov, Hamid Eghbalzadeh, Naomi Eigbe, Imad El Hanafi, Hassan Eslami, Benedikt Falk, Jiaojiao Fan, Jim Fan, Amol Fasale, Sergiy Fefilatyev, Liang Feng, Francesco Ferroni, Sanja Fidler, Xiao Fu, Vikram Fugro, Prashant Gaikwad, TJ Galda, Katelyn Gao, Yihuai Gao, Wenhang Ge, Sreyan Ghosh, Arushi Goel, Vivek Goel, Akash Gokul, Rama Govindaraju, Jinwei Gu, Miguel Guerrero, Elfie Guo, Aryaman Gupta, Siddharth Gururani, Hugo Hadfield, Song Han, Ankur Handa, Zekun Hao, Mohammad Harrim, Ali Hassani, Nathan Hayes-Roth, Yufan He, Chris Helvig, Cyrus Hogg
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM; cs.RO

<h3 id="755">[755] Calibration Data Trade-offs Across Capability Dimensions: Why Multi-Source Mixing Matters for High-Sparsity LLM Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03328) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hu Xu, Zhaolong Xing, Congcong Liu, Jiaxing Wang, Zhida Jiang, Junshi Huang, Zhen Chen, Jianfeng Xu

<h3 id="756">[756] Optimizing Explicit Unit-Distance Lower-Bound Certificates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03419) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对Erdős单位距离猜测中参数未优化的问题，开发了非线性整数优化流水线。通过定制进化策略获得改进的证书，支持u(n)\>n^{1.0152}，并展示了扩大素数范围的重要性。

<h3 id="757">[757] Position: Deployed Reinforcement Learning should be Continual</h3>

<h3 id="758">[758] Incremental Sheaf Cohomology on Cellular Complexes: O(1)-in-n Lazy Edit Processing under Bounded Local Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04227) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jason L. Volk
- **Subjects**: cs.DS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Topological Data Analysis, Graph Theory

- **Summary**: 针对动态细胞复形上第一上同调群的高效计算问题，提出基于局部几何有界假设的增量算法。每次编辑延迟中位数35微秒，同步时零漂移，并讨论了非平凡层无法局部化的障碍。

<h3 id="759">[759] An Empirical Study of Data Scale, Model Complexity, and Input Modalities in Visual Generalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04409](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04409) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yidi Zhouluo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Computer Vision, Generalization Analysis
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YidiZhouluo/DeepLearning-Empirical-Studies/tree/main/Exp_01)
- **Summary**: 针对深度神经网络泛化性能来源难以解释的问题，通过实验分析数据规模、模型复杂度和输入模态的影响。结果表明增加数据规模持续提升泛化，而模型复杂度提升不稳定，颜色信息重要但先验特征效果不一致。

<h3 id="760">[760] Multi-SPIN: Multi-Access Speculative Inference for Cooperative Token Generation at the Edge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04581) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Speculative Decoding, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 针对异构边缘系统中大语言模型推理加速的问题，提出Multi-SPIN架构，利用设备端小模型生成草稿并由服务器并行验证。优化草稿长度和带宽分配，在异构场景下吞吐量提升最高88%。

<h3 id="761">[761] An Empirical Audit of Input Encoders for Multi-Channel Signal Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04752](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04752) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ossi Lehtinen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Representation Learning, Signal Processing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/OssiLehtinen/channel-encoder-audit)
- **Summary**: 针对多通道标量信号输入Transformer的编码器选择问题，审计8种编码器。发现标准每通道线性投影与其他方法实际表现接近，源于自发正交化；共享标量编码器因信息原因崩溃。

<h3 id="762">[762] GOTabPFN: From Feature Ordering to Compact Tokenization for Tabular Foundation Models on High-Dimensional Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05441) (replaced)

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zadid6pretam/GOTabPFN)
- **Summary**: 针对高维小样本表格预测中基础模型效果差的问题，提出GOTabPFN，通过图引导特征排序和神经启发子单元压缩实现紧凑表示。在表格基准上提升了稳定性和准确率。

<h3 id="763">[763] Emotion-Aware Image Generation from Korean Diary Text via LLM-based Prompt Translation and LoRA Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05816) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jihun Cho, Soo-Yeon Jeong, Sun-Young Ihm
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Emotion Recognition, LoRA
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像模型难以捕捉文本情感的问题，提出情感感知图像生成流水线，使用Qwen3识别情感并用LoRA微调SD3.5生成儿童画风格图像。实验验证了情感触发词的效果并讨论了CLIP Score的局限性。

<h3 id="764">[764] OPRD: On-Policy Representation Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06021](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06021) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shenzhi Yang, Guangcheng Zhu, Bowen Song, Haobo Wang, Mingxuan Xia, Xing Zheng, Yingfan Ma, Zhongqi Chen, Weiqiang Wang, Gang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, On-Policy Distillation, LLM Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ShenzhiYang2000/OPRD)
- **Summary**: 针对在线蒸馏中输出空间监督的采样方差和黑盒教师问题，提出在线表示蒸馏OPRD，在隐藏状态空间对齐师生模型。在AIME等任务上缩小师生差距，训练速度提升1.44倍，内存减少54%。

<h3 id="765">[765] HANDOFF: Humanoid Agentic Task-Space Whole-Body Control via Distilled Complementary Teachers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06493) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lizhi Yang, Junheng Li, Nehar Poddar, Yiling Hou, Gio Huh, Robert Griffin, Georgia Gkioxari, Aaron Ames
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Humanoid Robot, Robot Manipulation, Whole-Body Control
- **Summary**: 针对人形机器人全身控制需要密集参考、规划器难以生成的问题，提出HANDOFF控制器，通过多教师KL蒸馏和上下文门控的混合专家学生，结合VLM规划器。在Unitree G1上达到SOTA速度跟踪和大型鲁棒操作空间。

<h3 id="766">[766] Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification Using Terahertz Spectroscopy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06554](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Roshni Mahtani, Ilán Carretero, Laura Monroy, Aldo Moreno-Oyervides, Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique, Rocío del Amor
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Industrial AI, Material Discovery, Deep Learning
- **Venue**: EUSIPCO'26
- **Summary**: 针对聚合物分类可靠性问题，利用太赫兹光谱并提出多尺度特征注意力网络MSFAN。集成特征门控和多尺度卷积，在12类聚合物上达到85.2%准确率。

<h3 id="767">[767] ThinkBooster: A Unified Framework for Seamless Test-Time Scaling of LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06915) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vladislav Smirnov, Chieu Nguyen, Sergey Senichev, Minh Ngoc Ta, Ekaterina Fadeeva, Artem Vazhentsev, Daria Galimzianova, Nikolai Rozanov, Viktor Mazanov, Jingwei Ni, Tianyi Wu, Igor Kiselev, Mrinmaya Sachan, Iryna Gurevych, Preslav Nakov, Timothy Baldwin, Artem Shelmanov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="768">[768] Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.07379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07379) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thanawat Lodkaew, Johannes Ackermann, Soichiro Nishimori, Nontawat Charoenphakdee, Masashi Sugiyama, Takashi Ishida
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Agent Evaluation, Deception Detection
- **Summary**: 针对编码代理可能通过捷径欺骗评估的问题，提出CapCode框架，通过上限性能设计和随机测试检测作弊。CapReward奖励设计减少作弊行为，使模型更好遵循任务规范。
